# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/bank_recon/matcher.py
"""
TallyNext Bank Statement Auto-Matcher

Matches bank statement lines to ERPNext GL entries / Payment Entries.

Confidence levels:
  High   — exact amount + date within ±1 day   → auto-accept
  Medium — exact amount + date within ±3 days   → queue for review
  Low    — amount within 1% + date within ±7 days → manual review
"""

import frappe
from frappe.utils import flt, date_diff, getdate, add_days


def run_auto_match(upload_name):
	"""Run auto-matching for all unmatched lines in an upload."""
	doc = frappe.get_doc("TN Bank Statement Upload", upload_name)

	# Get the GL account for this bank account
	bank_gl = frappe.db.get_value("Bank Account", doc.bank_account, "account")
	if not bank_gl:
		frappe.throw(f"Bank Account {doc.bank_account} has no linked GL Account.")

	matched = 0
	for line in doc.lines:
		if line.match_status not in ("Unmatched",):
			continue

		result = _find_match(line, bank_gl, doc.company)
		if result:
			line.match_status      = "Auto-Matched"
			line.confidence        = result["confidence"]
			line.matched_voucher_type = result["voucher_type"]
			line.matched_voucher_no   = result["voucher_no"]
			line.match_note        = result["note"]
			matched += 1

	doc.save()
	frappe.msgprint(f"Auto-matched {matched} of {len(doc.lines)} lines.")
	return matched


def _find_match(line, bank_gl, company):
	txn_date = getdate(line.txn_date)
	amount   = flt(line.credit) if flt(line.credit) else flt(line.debit)
	is_credit = flt(line.credit) > 0

	# Search Payment Entries
	for days_tol in (1, 3, 7):
		from_date = str(add_days(txn_date, -days_tol))
		to_date   = str(add_days(txn_date,  days_tol))

		results = frappe.db.sql("""
			SELECT pe.name, pe.payment_type, pe.paid_amount, pe.posting_date, pe.party
			FROM `tabPayment Entry` pe
			WHERE pe.company = %(company)s
				AND pe.docstatus = 1
				AND pe.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
				AND ABS(pe.paid_amount - %(amount)s) < 0.01
		""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date, "amount": amount}, as_dict=True)

		for r in results:
			confidence = "High" if days_tol == 1 else ("Medium" if days_tol == 3 else "Low")
			return {
				"confidence":    confidence,
				"voucher_type":  "Payment Entry",
				"voucher_no":    r.name,
				"note":          f"Amount match ±{days_tol}d. Party: {r.party or '—'}",
			}

	# Search Journal Entries on this GL account
	for days_tol in (1, 3, 7):
		from_date = str(add_days(txn_date, -days_tol))
		to_date   = str(add_days(txn_date,  days_tol))
		side = "credit" if is_credit else "debit"

		results = frappe.db.sql(f"""
			SELECT jea.parent, jea.{side} AS amount, je.posting_date
			FROM `tabJournal Entry Account` jea
			INNER JOIN `tabJournal Entry` je ON je.name = jea.parent
			WHERE je.company = %(company)s
				AND je.docstatus = 1
				AND je.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
				AND jea.account = %(account)s
				AND ABS(jea.{side} - %(amount)s) < 0.01
		""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date,
			  "account": bank_gl, "amount": amount}, as_dict=True)

		for r in results:
			confidence = "High" if days_tol == 1 else ("Medium" if days_tol == 3 else "Low")
			return {
				"confidence":   confidence,
				"voucher_type": "Journal Entry",
				"voucher_no":   r.parent,
				"note":         f"GL amount match ±{days_tol}d",
			}

	return None

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/bank_recon/parser.py
"""
TallyNext Bank Statement Parser — Generic CSV/Excel/OFX

Returns a list of dicts:
  { date, description, debit, credit, balance, ref_no }

Country modules can register custom parsers via hooks:
  tn_bank_statement_parsers = {
      "HDFC": "tallynext_india.parsers.hdfc.parse",
  }
"""

import csv
import io
from frappe.utils import flt
import frappe


def parse_bank_statement(file_url, fmt="Auto-Detect"):
	"""Main entry point. Detect format and dispatch."""
	file_path = frappe.get_site_path("public", file_url.lstrip("/"))

	if fmt == "Auto-Detect":
		fmt = _detect_format(file_path)

	if fmt == "OFX":
		return _parse_ofx(file_path)
	elif fmt == "Excel":
		return _parse_excel(file_path)
	else:
		return _parse_csv(file_path)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Format detection
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _detect_format(path):
	lower = path.lower()
	if lower.endswith(".ofx") or lower.endswith(".qfx"):
		return "OFX"
	if lower.endswith(".xlsx") or lower.endswith(".xls"):
		return "Excel"
	return "CSV"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CSV parser — generic, auto-detects column positions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

DEBIT_KEYWORDS  = {"debit", "dr", "withdrawal", "withdrawals", "payment", "paid out", "charges"}
CREDIT_KEYWORDS = {"credit", "cr", "deposit", "deposits", "received", "paid in"}
DATE_KEYWORDS   = {"date", "txn date", "transaction date", "value date", "posting date"}
DESC_KEYWORDS   = {"description", "narration", "particulars", "remarks", "details", "transaction", "memo"}
BAL_KEYWORDS    = {"balance", "closing balance", "running balance", "available balance"}
REF_KEYWORDS    = {"ref", "reference", "cheque", "chequeno", "utr", "txn id", "transaction id"}


def _col_idx(headers, keywords):
	for i, h in enumerate(headers):
		if h.strip().lower() in keywords:
			return i
	return None


def _parse_csv(path):
	lines = []
	with open(path, encoding="utf-8-sig", errors="replace") as f:
		# Skip blank/non-header lines at start
		raw = f.read()

	reader = csv.reader(io.StringIO(raw))
	rows = [r for r in reader if any(c.strip() for c in r)]

	if not rows:
		return []

	# Find header row (first row that has recognisable column names)
	header_row_idx = 0
	for i, row in enumerate(rows):
		low = [c.strip().lower() for c in row]
		if any(k in low for k in DATE_KEYWORDS):
			header_row_idx = i
			break

	headers = [c.strip().lower() for c in rows[header_row_idx]]

	date_col  = _col_idx(headers, DATE_KEYWORDS)
	desc_col  = _col_idx(headers, DESC_KEYWORDS)
	dr_col    = _col_idx(headers, DEBIT_KEYWORDS)
	cr_col    = _col_idx(headers, CREDIT_KEYWORDS)
	bal_col   = _col_idx(headers, BAL_KEYWORDS)
	ref_col   = _col_idx(headers, REF_KEYWORDS)

	# Fallback: if only one amount column, split by sign
	amount_col = None
	if dr_col is None and cr_col is None:
		for i, h in enumerate(headers):
			if "amount" in h:
				amount_col = i
				break

	for row in rows[header_row_idx + 1:]:
		if len(row) < 2:
			continue

		def _get(idx):
			if idx is None or idx >= len(row):
				return ""
			return row[idx].strip().replace(",", "")

		raw_date = _get(date_col)
		if not raw_date:
			continue

		try:
			date = frappe.utils.getdate(raw_date)
		except Exception:
			continue

		description = _get(desc_col) or ""
		ref_no      = _get(ref_col)  or ""
		balance     = flt(_get(bal_col))

		if amount_col is not None:
			amt = flt(_get(amount_col))
			debit  = abs(amt) if amt < 0 else 0
			credit = amt if amt > 0 else 0
		else:
			debit  = flt(_get(dr_col))
			credit = flt(_get(cr_col))

		lines.append({
			"date":        str(date),
			"description": description,
			"debit":       debit,
			"credit":      credit,
			"balance":     balance,
			"ref_no":      ref_no,
		})

	return lines


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Excel parser
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _parse_excel(path):
	try:
		import openpyxl
	except ImportError:
		frappe.throw("openpyxl is required to parse Excel files. Run: pip install openpyxl")

	wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(path, data_only=True)
	ws = wb.active

	rows = []
	for row in ws.iter_rows(values_only=True):
		if any(cell is not None for cell in row):
			rows.append([str(c).strip() if c is not None else "" for c in row])

	# Write to CSV string and reuse CSV parser
	buf = io.StringIO()
	writer = csv.writer(buf)
	writer.writerows(rows)
	buf.seek(0)

	# Parse the CSV
	reader = csv.reader(buf)
	all_rows = list(reader)
	if not all_rows:
		return []

	# Reuse CSV logic by writing a temp file
	import tempfile, os
	tmp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".csv", mode="w", delete=False, encoding="utf-8")
	tmp.write(buf.getvalue())
	tmp.close()
	result = _parse_csv(tmp.name)
	os.unlink(tmp.name)
	return result


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# OFX parser
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _parse_ofx(path):
	lines = []
	try:
		with open(path, encoding="utf-8-sig", errors="replace") as f:
			content = f.read()

		import re
		transactions = re.findall(r"<STMTTRN>(.*?)</STMTTRN>", content, re.DOTALL | re.IGNORECASE)

		for txn in transactions:
			def _tag(tag):
				m = re.search(rf"<{tag}>(.*?)[<\n]", txn, re.IGNORECASE)
				return m.group(1).strip() if m else ""

			raw_date = _tag("DTPOSTED")
			if raw_date:
				raw_date = raw_date[:8]  # YYYYMMDD
				try:
					date = frappe.utils.getdate(f"{raw_date[:4]}-{raw_date[4:6]}-{raw_date[6:8]}")
				except Exception:
					continue
			else:
				continue

			trntype = _tag("TRNTYPE").upper()
			amt     = flt(_tag("TRNAMT"))
			memo    = _tag("MEMO") or _tag("NAME") or ""
			ref_no  = _tag("FITID") or ""

			if trntype in ("CREDIT", "DEP", "INT", "DIV") or amt > 0:
				debit, credit = 0, abs(amt)
			else:
				debit, credit = abs(amt), 0

			lines.append({
				"date":        str(date),
				"description": memo,
				"debit":       debit,
				"credit":      credit,
				"balance":     0,
				"ref_no":      ref_no,
			})
	except Exception as e:
		frappe.log_error(f"OFX parse error: {e}", "TN Bank Statement Parser")

	return lines

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_audit_log/tn_audit_log.py
import frappe
from frappe.model.document import Document


class TNAuditLog(Document):
	pass


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Hook called on GL-touching DocTypes after amendment / cancel
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

TRACKED_DOCTYPES = {
	"Journal Entry", "Payment Entry",
	"Sales Invoice", "Purchase Invoice",
	"Sales Order", "Purchase Order",
}

TRACKED_FIELDS = {
	"Journal Entry":    ["posting_date", "total_debit", "total_credit", "cheque_no", "accounts"],
	"Payment Entry":    ["posting_date", "paid_amount", "party", "payment_type"],
	"Sales Invoice":    ["posting_date", "grand_total", "customer", "due_date"],
	"Purchase Invoice": ["posting_date", "grand_total", "supplier", "due_date"],
}


def log_voucher_event(doc, method=None):
	"""Generic hook — fires on after_insert, on_submit, on_cancel, on_update_after_submit."""
	if doc.doctype not in TRACKED_DOCTYPES:
		return

	action_map = {
		"after_insert":            "Created",
		"on_submit":               "Created",
		"on_cancel":               "Cancelled",
		"on_update_after_submit":  "Amended",
	}
	action = action_map.get(method, "Amended")

	# Build field diff
	changes = {}
	if action == "Amended":
		fields = TRACKED_FIELDS.get(doc.doctype, [])
		for f in fields:
			old = doc.get_doc_before_save()
			if old:
				old_val = old.get(f)
				new_val = doc.get(f)
				if str(old_val) != str(new_val):
					changes[f] = {"from": str(old_val), "to": str(new_val)}

	frappe.get_doc({
		"doctype":      "TN Audit Log",
		"voucher_type": doc.doctype,
		"voucher_no":   doc.name,
		"company":      getattr(doc, "company", ""),
		"posting_date": getattr(doc, "posting_date", None),
		"action":       action,
		"changed_by":   frappe.session.user,
		"changed_on":   frappe.utils.now(),
		"field_changes": frappe.as_json(changes) if changes else "{}",
	}).insert(ignore_permissions=True)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_auditor_access/tn_auditor_access.py
"""
TN Auditor Access — time-bounded read-only access for external CAs / auditors.

Features:
- Time-bounded expiry enforced at login and on page load
- Granular permission flags (GL, vouchers, reports, payroll, banking)
- Audit pack generation: zips up trial balance + day book + GL CSV
- Auto-deactivation when access_to date is passed (daily scheduler)
"""

import io
import csv
import zipfile
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import today, getdate, now_datetime


class TNAuditorAccess(Document):

    def validate(self):
        if getdate(self.access_from) > getdate(self.access_to):
            frappe.throw(_("Access From must be before Access To."))
        if not frappe.db.exists("User", {"email": self.auditor_email}):
            frappe.msgprint(
                _(f"User {self.auditor_email} does not exist in the system. "
                  "Create the user first and assign the TN Auditor role."),
                alert=True
            )

    def is_expired(self):
        return getdate(today()) > getdate(self.access_to)

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def generate_audit_pack(self):
        """Generate a ZIP file with key reports and return its file URL."""
        self._check_active()
        company    = self.company
        from_date  = self.access_from
        to_date    = self.access_to

        buf = io.BytesIO()
        with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
            zf.writestr("trial_balance.csv",    _build_trial_balance_csv(company, to_date))
            zf.writestr("day_book.csv",         _build_day_book_csv(company, from_date, to_date))
            zf.writestr("gl_entries.csv",       _build_gl_csv(company, from_date, to_date))

        fname = f"audit_pack_{company}_{from_date}_{to_date}.zip"
        file_doc = frappe.get_doc({
            "doctype":   "File",
            "file_name": fname,
            "content":   buf.getvalue(),
            "is_private": 1,
        })
        file_doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        self.db_set("last_pack_generated", now_datetime())
        self.db_set("last_pack_url",       file_doc.file_url)
        frappe.db.commit()
        return file_doc.file_url

    def _check_active(self):
        if not self.is_active:
            frappe.throw(_("This auditor access is not active."))
        if self.is_expired():
            frappe.throw(_("This auditor access has expired."))


# ── Scheduler ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def expire_auditor_access():
    """Deactivate expired records. Runs daily."""
    expired = frappe.db.get_all(
        "TN Auditor Access",
        filters={"is_active": 1, "access_to": ["<", today()]},
        fields=["name"],
    )
    for rec in expired:
        frappe.db.set_value("TN Auditor Access", rec.name, "is_active", 0)
    if expired:
        frappe.db.commit()


# ── Access check (called from page/report controllers) ─────────────────────

def check_auditor_access(company, permission_field="can_view_vouchers"):
    """
    Return the TN Auditor Access doc if the current user has active access to
    ``company`` with the given permission flag set. Returns None otherwise.
    """
    email = frappe.session.user
    recs = frappe.db.get_all(
        "TN Auditor Access",
        filters={"auditor_email": email, "company": company, "is_active": 1},
        fields=["name", "access_from", "access_to", permission_field],
    )
    for rec in recs:
        if getdate(today()) <= getdate(rec.access_to) and rec.get(permission_field):
            return frappe.get_doc("TN Auditor Access", rec.name)
    return None


# ── CSV builders ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _build_trial_balance_csv(company, as_of_date):
    rows = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT a.name account, a.root_type,
               SUM(g.debit) total_debit, SUM(g.credit) total_credit,
               SUM(g.debit - g.credit) net_balance
        FROM `tabGL Entry` g
        JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
        WHERE g.company=%(co)s AND g.is_cancelled=0
              AND g.posting_date <= %(dt)s
        GROUP BY a.name, a.root_type
        ORDER BY a.root_type, a.name
    """, {"co": company, "dt": as_of_date}, as_dict=True)

    out = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.DictWriter(out, fieldnames=["account", "root_type", "total_debit",
                                         "total_credit", "net_balance"])
    w.writeheader()
    w.writerows(rows)
    return out.getvalue()


def _build_day_book_csv(company, from_date, to_date):
    rows = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT g.posting_date, g.voucher_type, g.voucher_no,
               g.account, g.party, g.debit, g.credit, g.remarks
        FROM `tabGL Entry` g
        WHERE g.company=%(co)s AND g.is_cancelled=0
              AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(fd)s AND %(td)s
        ORDER BY g.posting_date, g.voucher_no
    """, {"co": company, "fd": from_date, "td": to_date}, as_dict=True)

    out = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.DictWriter(out, fieldnames=["posting_date","voucher_type","voucher_no",
                                         "account","party","debit","credit","remarks"])
    w.writeheader()
    w.writerows(rows)
    return out.getvalue()


def _build_gl_csv(company, from_date, to_date):
    # Same as day book but with more fields
    return _build_day_book_csv(company, from_date, to_date)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_bank_statement_upload/tn_bank_statement_upload.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document


class TNBankStatementUpload(Document):
	def validate(self):
		if self.status == "Draft" and self.statement_file:
			self.status = "Draft"  # Parsed on explicit action

	@frappe.whitelist()
	def parse_statement(self):
		"""Parse the uploaded file and populate child lines."""
		if not self.statement_file:
			frappe.throw(_("Please attach a statement file first."))

		from tallynext.tallynext.bank_recon.parser import parse_bank_statement
		lines = parse_bank_statement(self.statement_file, self.statement_format or "Auto-Detect")

		self.set("lines", [])
		total_dr = total_cr = 0

		for ln in lines:
			self.append("lines", {
				"txn_date":    ln.get("date"),
				"description": ln.get("description", ""),
				"debit":       ln.get("debit", 0),
				"credit":      ln.get("credit", 0),
				"balance":     ln.get("balance", 0),
				"ref_no":      ln.get("ref_no", ""),
				"match_status": "Unmatched",
			})
			total_dr += ln.get("debit", 0)
			total_cr += ln.get("credit", 0)

		self.total_debits  = total_dr
		self.total_credits = total_cr
		self.status = "Parsed"
		self.save()
		frappe.msgprint(_("{0} lines parsed successfully.").format(len(lines)))

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_budget/tn_budget.py
"""
TN Budget — company + fiscal-year budget with line-by-line account tracking.
Actuals are pulled from GL Entries on demand; alerts fire daily via scheduler.
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import flt, today, getdate


class TNBudget(Document):

    def before_save(self):
        self.refresh_actuals()

    def refresh_actuals(self):
        """Re-compute actual_amount, variance, variance_pct for each line."""
        fy = frappe.db.get_value("Fiscal Year", self.fiscal_year,
                                  ["year_start_date", "year_end_date"], as_dict=True)
        if not fy:
            return

        total_budget = total_actual = 0.0
        for line in self.budget_details:
            actual = _get_actual(self.company, line.account, line.cost_center,
                                  fy.year_start_date, fy.year_end_date)
            line.actual_amount  = flt(actual)
            line.variance       = flt(line.budget_amount) - flt(actual)
            line.variance_pct   = (flt(line.variance) / flt(line.budget_amount) * 100
                                   if line.budget_amount else 0)
            total_budget += flt(line.budget_amount)
            total_actual += flt(actual)

        self.total_budget   = total_budget
        self.total_actual   = total_actual
        self.total_variance = total_budget - total_actual

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def get_actuals(self):
        self.refresh_actuals()
        return self.budget_details


def _get_actual(company, account, cost_center, from_date, to_date):
    cond = ""
    params = {"co": company, "acct": account, "fd": from_date, "td": to_date}
    if cost_center:
        cond = "AND g.cost_center = %(cc)s"
        params["cc"] = cost_center

    result = frappe.db.sql(f"""
        SELECT SUM(g.debit - g.credit)
        FROM `tabGL Entry` g
        WHERE g.company = %(co)s AND g.account = %(acct)s AND g.is_cancelled = 0
              AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(fd)s AND %(td)s
              {cond}
    """, params)
    return flt(result[0][0] if result else 0)


# ── Scheduler ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def check_budget_alerts():
    """Fire email alerts when actual spend crosses the alert threshold. Runs daily."""
    budgets = frappe.db.get_all("TN Budget", filters={"is_active": 1},
                                 fields=["name", "company", "fiscal_year",
                                         "budget_name", "alert_threshold_pct"])
    for bud in budgets:
        doc = frappe.get_doc("TN Budget", bud.name)
        doc.refresh_actuals()
        threshold = flt(bud.alert_threshold_pct) / 100

        for line in doc.budget_details:
            if line.alert_sent:
                continue
            if not line.budget_amount:
                continue
            utilisation = flt(line.actual_amount) / flt(line.budget_amount)
            if utilisation >= threshold:
                _send_budget_alert(doc, line, utilisation)
                line.alert_sent = 1

        doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    if budgets:
        frappe.db.commit()


def _send_budget_alert(budget_doc, line, utilisation):
    recipients = frappe.db.get_all(
        "Has Role",
        filters={"role": "Accounts Manager", "parenttype": "User"},
        fields=["parent"],
    )
    emails = [r.parent for r in recipients if frappe.db.get_value("User", r.parent, "enabled")]
    if not emails:
        return
    pct = round(utilisation * 100, 1)
    frappe.sendmail(
        recipients=emails,
        subject=f"[TallyNext] Budget Alert — {line.account} at {pct}%",
        message=(
            f"<p>Budget: <b>{budget_doc.budget_name}</b> ({budget_doc.company})</p>"
            f"<p>Account: <b>{line.account}</b></p>"
            f"<p>Budget: {frappe.format(line.budget_amount, {'fieldtype':'Currency'})} | "
            f"Actual: {frappe.format(line.actual_amount, {'fieldtype':'Currency'})} | "
            f"Utilisation: <b>{pct}%</b></p>"
        ),
        now=True,
    )

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_cheque_format/tn_cheque_format.py
"""
TN Cheque Format — per-bank cheque dimensions and field positions.

Built-in templates for major Indian banks are seeded via after_migrate.
Cheque printing is done client-side using a positioned overlay printed
over the physical cheque paper (standard browser print, no PDF library needed).
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document


class TNChequeFormat(Document):

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def get_next_cheque_no(self):
        """Return and advance the next cheque number."""
        no = self.next_cheque_no or 1
        self.db_set("next_cheque_no", no + 1)
        frappe.db.commit()
        return no


# ── Built-in bank templates ────────────────────────────────────────────────

BANK_TEMPLATES = [
    # (format_name, bank_name, width, height, payee_x, payee_y, aw_x, aw_y, an_x, an_y, dt_x, dt_y)
    ("HDFC Standard",  "HDFC Bank",  210, 90,  35, 26, 35, 38, 158, 26, 154, 12),
    ("ICICI Standard", "ICICI Bank", 210, 90,  33, 26, 33, 38, 158, 26, 155, 12),
    ("SBI Standard",   "SBI",        210, 90,  32, 28, 32, 40, 160, 28, 152, 13),
    ("Axis Standard",  "Axis Bank",  210, 90,  34, 25, 34, 37, 157, 25, 153, 11),
    ("Kotak Standard", "Kotak Mahindra Bank", 210, 90, 34, 26, 34, 38, 158, 26, 154, 12),
]


def seed_cheque_formats():
    """Called from after_migrate — idempotent seeding of built-in formats."""
    for tmpl in BANK_TEMPLATES:
        (fmt, bank, w, h, px, py, awx, awy, anx, any_, dx, dy) = tmpl
        if frappe.db.exists("TN Cheque Format", fmt):
            continue
        frappe.get_doc({
            "doctype":               "TN Cheque Format",
            "format_name":           fmt,
            "bank_name":             bank,
            "paper_width_mm":        w,
            "paper_height_mm":       h,
            "payee_x":               px,
            "payee_y":               py,
            "payee_font_size":       11,
            "amount_words_x":        awx,
            "amount_words_y":        awy,
            "amount_words_font_size":11,
            "amount_num_x":          anx,
            "amount_num_y":          any_,
            "amount_num_font_size":  12,
            "date_x":                dx,
            "date_y":                dy,
            "date_font_size":        11,
            "next_cheque_no":        1,
            "validate_sequence":     1,
        }).insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    frappe.db.commit()


# ── Amount in words (Indian number system) ─────────────────────────────────

_ONES = ["", "One", "Two", "Three", "Four", "Five", "Six", "Seven", "Eight", "Nine",
         "Ten", "Eleven", "Twelve", "Thirteen", "Fourteen", "Fifteen", "Sixteen",
         "Seventeen", "Eighteen", "Nineteen"]
_TENS = ["", "", "Twenty", "Thirty", "Forty", "Fifty", "Sixty", "Seventy", "Eighty", "Ninety"]


def amount_in_words_indian(amount):
    """Convert a number to Indian-system words (crore, lakh, thousand)."""
    amount = round(float(amount), 2)
    rupees = int(amount)
    paise  = round((amount - rupees) * 100)

    def _words(n):
        if n == 0:
            return ""
        if n < 20:
            return _ONES[n]
        if n < 100:
            return _TENS[n // 10] + (" " + _ONES[n % 10] if n % 10 else "")
        if n < 1000:
            return _ONES[n // 100] + " Hundred" + (" " + _words(n % 100) if n % 100 else "")
        if n < 100000:
            return _words(n // 1000) + " Thousand" + (" " + _words(n % 1000) if n % 1000 else "")
        if n < 10000000:
            return _words(n // 100000) + " Lakh" + (" " + _words(n % 100000) if n % 100000 else "")
        return _words(n // 10000000) + " Crore" + (" " + _words(n % 10000000) if n % 10000000 else "")

    parts = []
    if rupees:
        parts.append(_words(rupees) + " Rupees")
    if paise:
        parts.append(_words(paise) + " Paise")
    return " and ".join(parts) + " Only" if parts else "Zero Rupees Only"


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_cheque_data(doctype, docname, cheque_format):
    """Return all data needed to render the cheque print overlay."""
    if doctype == "Payment Entry":
        doc = frappe.get_doc("Payment Entry", docname)
        payee  = doc.party_name or doc.party
        amount = doc.paid_amount
        date   = str(doc.posting_date)
    elif doctype == "TN PDC Entry":
        doc = frappe.get_doc("TN PDC Entry", docname)
        payee  = doc.party_name or doc.party
        amount = doc.amount
        date   = str(doc.cheque_date)
    else:
        frappe.throw(_("Cheque printing not supported for ") + doctype)

    fmt = frappe.get_doc("TN Cheque Format", cheque_format)
    cheque_no = fmt.get_next_cheque_no()

    return {
        "payee":        payee,
        "amount":       amount,
        "amount_words": amount_in_words_indian(amount),
        "date":         date,
        "cheque_no":    cheque_no,
        "format":       fmt.as_dict(),
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_compliance_item/tn_compliance_item.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import today, nowdate


class TNComplianceItem(Document):
	def validate(self):
		self._auto_set_overdue()

	def _auto_set_overdue(self):
		if self.status == "Pending" and self.due_date and self.due_date < today():
			self.status = "Overdue"

	@frappe.whitelist()
	def mark_filed(self, reference=None, filed_on=None):
		self.status = "Filed"
		self.filed_on = filed_on or today()
		self.filing_reference = reference or ""
		self.filed_by = frappe.session.user
		self.save()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public API for country modules to register compliance items
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def tn_add_compliance_item(company, compliance_name, due_date, frequency="Monthly", source_module="Custom"):
	"""
	Country modules call this to register a compliance due date.
	Idempotent — will not create a duplicate for the same (company, compliance_name, due_date).
	"""
	if frappe.db.exists("TN Compliance Item", {
		"company": company,
		"compliance_name": compliance_name,
		"due_date": due_date,
	}):
		return

	frappe.get_doc({
		"doctype": "TN Compliance Item",
		"company": company,
		"compliance_name": compliance_name,
		"due_date": due_date,
		"frequency": frequency,
		"source_module": source_module,
		"status": "Pending",
		"alert_days_before": 3,
	}).insert(ignore_permissions=True)


def mark_overdue_items():
	"""Scheduled daily — mark all past-due Pending items as Overdue."""
	items = frappe.db.get_all("TN Compliance Item",
		filters={"status": "Pending", "due_date": ("<", today())},
		fields=["name"])
	for item in items:
		frappe.db.set_value("TN Compliance Item", item.name, "status", "Overdue")
	if items:
		frappe.db.commit()


def send_compliance_alerts():
	"""Scheduled daily — send email/WhatsApp for items due within alert_days_before."""
	from frappe.utils import add_days
	items = frappe.db.get_all("TN Compliance Item",
		filters={"status": "Pending", "alert_sent": 0},
		fields=["name", "compliance_name", "company", "due_date", "alert_days_before"])

	for item in items:
		alert_date = add_days(item.due_date, -(item.alert_days_before or 3))
		if today() >= str(alert_date):
			_send_alert(item)
			frappe.db.set_value("TN Compliance Item", item.name, "alert_sent", 1)

	if items:
		frappe.db.commit()


def _send_alert(item):
	company_email = frappe.db.get_value("Company", item.company, "email") or ""
	if not company_email:
		return
	frappe.sendmail(
		recipients=[company_email],
		subject=f"[TallyNext] Compliance Due: {item.compliance_name} — {item.due_date}",
		message=f"""
		<p>This is a reminder that <strong>{item.compliance_name}</strong> is due on <strong>{item.due_date}</strong>.</p>
		<p>Company: {item.company}</p>
		<p>Please file and mark it as done in TallyNext Compliance Calendar.</p>
		""",
		now=True,
	)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_currency_revaluation/tn_currency_revaluation.py
"""
TN Currency Revaluation — IAS 21 compliant FX revaluation.

Revalues all foreign-currency balance sheet accounts to the closing exchange
rate on the revaluation date, posting the net gain/loss to a nominated account.

Scheduler: run_scheduled_revaluation() fires daily; only posts if month-end.
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import flt, today, getdate, get_last_day


class TNCurrencyRevaluation(Document):

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def run_revaluation(self):
        if self.status != "Draft":
            frappe.throw(_("Only Draft records can be run."))
        gl_rows = self._compute_revaluation_entries()
        if not gl_rows:
            frappe.msgprint(_("No foreign-currency balances found — nothing to revalue."))
            return

        je = self._post_journal_entry(gl_rows)
        self.db_set("journal_entry", je)
        self.db_set("status", "Posted")
        frappe.db.commit()
        return je

    def _compute_revaluation_entries(self):
        """
        For each foreign-currency account with a non-zero balance,
        compute the difference between book balance and closing-rate balance.
        """
        company_currency = frappe.db.get_value("Company", self.company, "default_currency")
        reval_date = self.revaluation_date

        # Accounts with a different account_currency than company currency
        accounts = frappe.db.sql("""
            SELECT a.name, a.account_currency, a.root_type,
                   SUM(g.debit_in_account_currency - g.credit_in_account_currency) AS balance_fc,
                   SUM(g.debit - g.credit) AS balance_base
            FROM `tabAccount` a
            JOIN `tabGL Entry` g ON g.account = a.name
            WHERE a.company = %(co)s
              AND a.account_currency != %(ccur)s
              AND a.root_type IN ('Asset', 'Liability')
              AND g.is_cancelled = 0
              AND g.posting_date <= %(dt)s
            GROUP BY a.name, a.account_currency, a.root_type
            HAVING ABS(balance_fc) > 0.001
        """, {"co": self.company, "ccur": company_currency, "dt": reval_date}, as_dict=True)

        rows = []
        for acct in accounts:
            closing_rate = self._get_exchange_rate(acct.account_currency, company_currency, reval_date)
            if not closing_rate:
                continue
            new_base = flt(acct.balance_fc) * closing_rate
            diff     = new_base - flt(acct.balance_base)
            if abs(diff) < 0.01:
                continue
            rows.append({
                "account":          acct.name,
                "account_currency": acct.account_currency,
                "balance_fc":       flt(acct.balance_fc),
                "balance_base":     flt(acct.balance_base),
                "new_base":         new_base,
                "diff":             diff,
            })
        return rows

    def _get_exchange_rate(self, from_currency, to_currency, date):
        rate = frappe.db.get_value(
            "Currency Exchange",
            {"from_currency": from_currency, "to_currency": to_currency,
             "date": ["<=", date]},
            "exchange_rate",
            order_by="date desc",
        )
        return flt(rate)

    def _post_journal_entry(self, gl_rows):
        je = frappe.new_doc("Journal Entry")
        je.company        = self.company
        je.posting_date   = self.revaluation_date
        je.voucher_type   = "Exchange Gain Or Loss"
        je.user_remark    = f"Currency Revaluation — {self.revaluation_date}"

        total_diff = 0.0
        for row in gl_rows:
            diff = row["diff"]
            total_diff += diff
            acct_row = je.append("accounts", {
                "account":                    row["account"],
                "account_currency":           row["account_currency"],
                "exchange_rate":              (row["new_base"] / row["balance_fc"]
                                               if row["balance_fc"] else 1),
                "cost_center":                self.cost_center,
            })
            if diff > 0:
                acct_row.debit_in_account_currency  = 0
                acct_row.credit_in_account_currency = 0
                acct_row.debit  = 0
                acct_row.credit = diff
            else:
                acct_row.debit  = abs(diff)
                acct_row.credit = 0

        # Balancing gain/loss entry
        gl_entry = je.append("accounts", {
            "account":     self.revaluation_gain_loss_account,
            "cost_center": self.cost_center,
        })
        if total_diff > 0:
            gl_entry.debit  = total_diff
            gl_entry.credit = 0
        else:
            gl_entry.debit  = 0
            gl_entry.credit = abs(total_diff)

        je.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        je.submit()
        return je.name


# ── Scheduler ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def run_scheduled_revaluation():
    """
    Called daily. Posts month-end revaluation for companies that have a
    gain/loss account configured in TN Settings (if present).
    Only fires on the last day of each month.
    """
    if getdate(today()) != getdate(get_last_day(today())):
        return  # Not month-end

    companies = frappe.db.get_all("Company", fields=["name"])
    for co in companies:
        # Check if a gain/loss account is set up — skip otherwise
        gl_acct = frappe.db.get_value("Account", {
            "company": co.name,
            "account_type": "Temporary",
            "account_name": ["like", "%Exchange%"],
        }, "name")
        if not gl_acct:
            continue

        doc = frappe.new_doc("TN Currency Revaluation")
        doc.company                          = co.name
        doc.revaluation_date                 = today()
        doc.revaluation_gain_loss_account    = gl_acct
        doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        try:
            doc.run_revaluation()
        except Exception as e:
            frappe.log_error(f"Currency Revaluation failed for {co.name}: {e}",
                             "TN Currency Revaluation")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_loan/tn_loan.py
"""
TN Loan — bank loan with auto-generated amortization schedule.

Reducing-balance EMI formula:
    EMI = P × r × (1+r)^n / ((1+r)^n - 1)
    where r = monthly rate, n = tenure in months.

Workflow:
    1. Create loan → amortize on save → schedule populated.
    2. Daily job generate_emi_queue_entries() adds Pending EMIs to TN Recurring Queue.
    3. Accountant books EMI → JE created (principal to loan liability, interest to expense).
    4. Prepayment or full closure via whitelisted methods.
"""

import math
from dateutil.relativedelta import relativedelta

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import flt, getdate, today, add_months, formatdate


class TNLoan(Document):

    def before_save(self):
        if not self.loan_schedule:
            self._build_schedule()
        self._refresh_totals()

    # ── Schedule builder ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _build_schedule(self):
        """Generate full amortization schedule using reducing balance method."""
        P = flt(self.sanction_amount)
        annual_rate = flt(self.annual_interest_rate)
        n = int(self.tenure_months or 0)
        if not P or not annual_rate or not n:
            return

        r = annual_rate / 100 / 12  # monthly rate
        if r == 0:
            emi = P / n
        else:
            emi = P * r * (1 + r) ** n / ((1 + r) ** n - 1)

        self.emi_amount = round(emi, 2)
        self.loan_schedule = []

        balance = P
        start   = getdate(self.emi_start_date)

        for i in range(1, n + 1):
            interest_part  = round(balance * r, 2)
            principal_part = round(emi - interest_part, 2)
            # Last EMI — absorb rounding residual
            if i == n:
                principal_part = round(balance, 2)
                interest_part  = round(emi - principal_part, 2)
            balance = round(balance - principal_part, 2)

            due = (getdate(self.emi_start_date) + relativedelta(months=i - 1))
            self.append("loan_schedule", {
                "emi_no":          i,
                "due_date":        due,
                "emi_amount":      round(emi, 2),
                "principal":       principal_part,
                "interest":        interest_part,
                "closing_balance": max(balance, 0),
                "status":          "Pending",
            })

    def _refresh_totals(self):
        booked = [row for row in self.loan_schedule if row.status == "Booked"]
        self.total_interest_paid  = sum(flt(r.interest)   for r in booked)
        booked_principal          = sum(flt(r.principal)  for r in booked)
        self.outstanding_principal = flt(self.sanction_amount) - booked_principal

    # ── Book an EMI ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def book_emi(self, emi_no):
        """Book the Journal Entry for a given EMI number."""
        emi_no = int(emi_no)
        row = next((r for r in self.loan_schedule if r.emi_no == emi_no), None)
        if not row:
            frappe.throw(_(f"EMI #{emi_no} not found."))
        if row.status == "Booked":
            frappe.throw(_(f"EMI #{emi_no} already booked."))

        je = frappe.new_doc("Journal Entry")
        je.company      = self.company
        je.posting_date = row.due_date
        je.voucher_type = "Journal Entry"
        je.user_remark  = f"Loan EMI #{emi_no} — {self.loan_name}"

        # Debit loan liability (principal reduction)
        je.append("accounts", {
            "account": self.loan_account,
            "debit_in_account_currency":  flt(row.principal),
            "credit_in_account_currency": 0,
        })
        # Debit interest expense
        je.append("accounts", {
            "account": self.interest_account,
            "debit_in_account_currency":  flt(row.interest),
            "credit_in_account_currency": 0,
        })
        # Credit bank (EMI payment) — user should have a bank account on this doc
        bank_account = frappe.db.get_value("Account", {
            "company": self.company, "account_type": "Bank", "is_group": 0
        }, "name")
        je.append("accounts", {
            "account": bank_account or self.loan_account,
            "debit_in_account_currency":  0,
            "credit_in_account_currency": flt(row.emi_amount),
        })

        je.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        je.submit()

        row.status        = "Booked"
        row.journal_entry = je.name
        self.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        frappe.db.commit()
        return je.name

    # ── Prepayment ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def prepay(self, amount, posting_date=None):
        """Apply a part-prepayment and rebuild the remaining schedule."""
        amount = flt(amount)
        if amount <= 0:
            frappe.throw(_("Prepayment amount must be positive."))
        posting_date = posting_date or today()

        # Remove Pending rows and rebuild from outstanding balance
        self.outstanding_principal = flt(self.outstanding_principal) - amount
        if self.outstanding_principal < 0:
            frappe.throw(_("Prepayment exceeds outstanding principal."))

        pending_rows = [r for r in self.loan_schedule if r.status == "Pending"]
        for r in pending_rows:
            self.loan_schedule.remove(r)

        # Re-amortize remaining balance over remaining months
        remaining_n = len(pending_rows)
        if remaining_n > 0 and self.outstanding_principal > 0:
            r_rate = flt(self.annual_interest_rate) / 100 / 12
            new_emi = (self.outstanding_principal * r_rate * (1 + r_rate) ** remaining_n
                       / ((1 + r_rate) ** remaining_n - 1)) if r_rate else (
                self.outstanding_principal / remaining_n)
            balance = self.outstanding_principal
            base_emi_no = max((r.emi_no for r in self.loan_schedule), default=0)
            start_date  = getdate(posting_date)

            for i in range(1, remaining_n + 1):
                interest_part  = round(balance * r_rate, 2)
                principal_part = round(new_emi - interest_part, 2)
                if i == remaining_n:
                    principal_part = round(balance, 2)
                balance = round(balance - principal_part, 2)
                due = start_date + relativedelta(months=i)
                self.append("loan_schedule", {
                    "emi_no":          base_emi_no + i,
                    "due_date":        due,
                    "emi_amount":      round(new_emi, 2),
                    "principal":       principal_part,
                    "interest":        interest_part,
                    "closing_balance": max(balance, 0),
                    "status":          "Pending",
                })

        self.emi_amount = round(new_emi, 2) if remaining_n > 0 and self.outstanding_principal > 0 else 0
        self.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        frappe.db.commit()

    # ── Full closure ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def close_loan(self, closure_date=None, foreclosure_charges=0):
        """Close the loan and book the final JE."""
        closure_date = closure_date or today()
        charges      = flt(foreclosure_charges)
        outstanding  = flt(self.outstanding_principal)

        je = frappe.new_doc("Journal Entry")
        je.company      = self.company
        je.posting_date = closure_date
        je.voucher_type = "Journal Entry"
        je.user_remark  = f"Loan Closure — {self.loan_name}"

        bank_account = frappe.db.get_value("Account", {
            "company": self.company, "account_type": "Bank", "is_group": 0
        }, "name")

        je.append("accounts", {"account": self.loan_account,
                                "debit_in_account_currency": outstanding,
                                "credit_in_account_currency": 0})
        if charges:
            je.append("accounts", {"account": self.interest_account,
                                    "debit_in_account_currency": charges,
                                    "credit_in_account_currency": 0})
        je.append("accounts", {"account": bank_account or self.loan_account,
                                "debit_in_account_currency": 0,
                                "credit_in_account_currency": outstanding + charges})

        je.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        je.submit()

        self.status                 = "Closed"
        self.closure_date           = closure_date
        self.foreclosure_charges    = charges
        self.closure_journal_entry  = je.name
        self.outstanding_principal  = 0
        for row in self.loan_schedule:
            if row.status == "Pending":
                row.status = "Skipped"
        self.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        frappe.db.commit()
        return je.name


# ── Scheduler ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def generate_emi_queue_entries():
    """
    Daily: for each active loan, if today is on or past a Pending EMI's due date,
    create a TN Recurring Queue entry so it appears in the accountant's approval list.
    """
    loans = frappe.db.get_all("TN Loan", filters={"status": "Active"},
                               fields=["name", "company", "loan_name"])
    for loan in loans:
        doc = frappe.get_doc("TN Loan", loan.name)
        for row in doc.loan_schedule:
            if row.status != "Pending":
                continue
            if getdate(row.due_date) > getdate(today()):
                break  # schedule is ordered; stop at first future EMI

            # Check if queue entry already exists
            exists = frappe.db.exists("TN Recurring Queue", {
                "reference_doctype": "TN Loan",
                "reference_name":    loan.name,
                "occurrence_date":   row.due_date,
            })
            if exists:
                continue

            frappe.get_doc({
                "doctype":            "TN Recurring Queue",
                "reference_doctype":  "TN Loan",
                "reference_name":     loan.name,
                "occurrence_date":    row.due_date,
                "status":             "Pending",
                "description":        f"Loan EMI #{row.emi_no} — {loan.loan_name}",
            }).insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    frappe.db.commit()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_pdc_entry/tn_pdc_entry.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import today, add_days


class TNPDCEntry(Document):
	def validate(self):
		if self.pdc_type == "Received" and self.party_type != "Customer":
			frappe.throw(_("Received PDC must have Party Type = Customer"))
		if self.pdc_type == "Issued" and self.party_type != "Supplier":
			frappe.throw(_("Issued PDC must have Party Type = Supplier"))

	@frappe.whitelist()
	def deposit(self):
		"""Create a Payment Entry for this PDC and link it."""
		if self.status not in ("Pending", "Sent for Clearing"):
			frappe.throw(_("Can only deposit a Pending or Sent-for-Clearing PDC."))

		pe = frappe.new_doc("Payment Entry")
		pe.payment_type = "Receive" if self.pdc_type == "Received" else "Pay"
		pe.company      = self.company
		pe.posting_date = today()
		pe.party_type   = self.party_type
		pe.party        = self.party
		pe.paid_amount  = self.amount
		pe.received_amount = self.amount
		pe.reference_no = self.cheque_no
		pe.reference_date = self.cheque_date
		pe.remarks = f"PDC Deposit: {self.name}"
		if self.our_bank_account:
			pe.paid_to = frappe.db.get_value("Bank Account", self.our_bank_account, "account")
		pe.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

		self.payment_entry = pe.name
		self.status = "Sent for Clearing"
		self.save()
		return pe.name

	@frappe.whitelist()
	def mark_cleared(self):
		self.status = "Cleared"
		self.save()

	@frappe.whitelist()
	def mark_bounced(self, reason=None, bounce_charges=0):
		self.status = "Bounced"
		self.bounce_reason = reason or ""
		self.bounce_charges = bounce_charges
		self.save()

	@frappe.whitelist()
	def stop_payment(self):
		if self.pdc_type != "Issued":
			frappe.throw(_("Stop Payment is only for Issued PDCs."))
		self.status = "Stop Payment"
		self.save()


def send_pdc_alerts():
	"""Scheduled daily — alert for PDCs due within alert_days_before."""
	pdcs = frappe.db.get_all("TN PDC Entry",
		filters={"status": ["in", ("Pending", "Issued", "Sent for Clearing")]},
		fields=["name", "pdc_type", "party", "cheque_no", "cheque_date",
				"amount", "company", "alert_days_before"])

	for pdc in pdcs:
		alert_date = add_days(pdc.cheque_date, -(pdc.alert_days_before or 2))
		if today() >= str(alert_date) and today() <= str(pdc.cheque_date):
			_send_pdc_alert(pdc)


def _send_pdc_alert(pdc):
	company_email = frappe.db.get_value("Company", pdc.company, "email") or ""
	if not company_email:
		return
	action = "deposit" if pdc.pdc_type == "Received" else "present"
	frappe.sendmail(
		recipients=[company_email],
		subject=f"[TallyNext] PDC Alert: Cheque {pdc.cheque_no} due {pdc.cheque_date}",
		message=f"""
		<p>PDC <strong>{pdc.cheque_no}</strong> from <strong>{pdc.party}</strong>
		is due for {action} on <strong>{pdc.cheque_date}</strong>.</p>
		<p>Amount: {pdc.amount} | Entry: {pdc.name}</p>
		""",
		now=True,
	)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_recurring_queue/tn_recurring_queue.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import now


class TNRecurringQueue(Document):
	@frappe.whitelist()
	def confirm_submit(self):
		"""Create and optionally submit the voucher."""
		tmpl = frappe.get_doc("TN Recurring Template", self.template)
		voucher = _create_voucher(tmpl, self.scheduled_date)
		self.voucher_type = voucher.doctype
		self.voucher_no   = voucher.name
		self.status       = "Submitted"
		self.actioned_by  = frappe.session.user
		self.actioned_on  = now()
		self.save()
		return voucher.name

	@frappe.whitelist()
	def skip(self, reason=""):
		self.status      = "Skipped"
		self.skip_reason = reason
		self.actioned_by = frappe.session.user
		self.actioned_on = now()
		self.save()


def _create_voucher(tmpl, posting_date):
	"""Create Journal Entry from a Recurring Template."""
	je = frappe.new_doc("Journal Entry")
	je.company      = tmpl.company
	je.posting_date = posting_date
	je.user_remark  = tmpl.narration or tmpl.template_name
	je.voucher_type = "Journal Entry"

	je.append("accounts", {
		"account":      tmpl.debit_account,
		"debit_in_account_currency": tmpl.amount,
		"cost_center":  tmpl.cost_center,
		"party_type":   tmpl.party_type or "",
		"party":        tmpl.party or "",
	})
	je.append("accounts", {
		"account":      tmpl.credit_account,
		"credit_in_account_currency": tmpl.amount,
		"cost_center":  tmpl.cost_center,
	})

	je.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
	if tmpl.auto_submit:
		je.submit()

	frappe.db.set_value("TN Recurring Template", tmpl.name, {
		"last_generated_on": posting_date,
		"times_generated":   (tmpl.times_generated or 0) + 1,
	})

	return je


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Scheduler job — runs daily, creates queue entries for due templates
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def generate_recurring_entries():
	"""Daily scheduler job — enqueue all templates due today."""
	from frappe.utils import today, getdate
	td = today()

	templates = frappe.db.get_all("TN Recurring Template",
		filters={"is_active": 1},
		fields=["name", "company", "start_date", "end_date",
				"max_occurrences", "times_generated", "last_generated_on",
				"frequency", "amount", "narration", "auto_submit"])

	for tmpl_data in templates:
		tmpl = frappe.get_doc("TN Recurring Template", tmpl_data.name)

		# Skip if past end date
		if tmpl.end_date and td > str(tmpl.end_date):
			continue
		# Skip if max occurrences reached
		if tmpl.max_occurrences and (tmpl.times_generated or 0) >= tmpl.max_occurrences:
			continue
		# Skip if not yet start date
		if td < str(tmpl.start_date):
			continue

		next_due = tmpl.next_due_date()
		if not next_due or td < next_due:
			continue

		# Skip if already queued for today
		if frappe.db.exists("TN Recurring Queue", {
			"template": tmpl.name,
			"scheduled_date": td,
			"status": ["in", ("Pending", "Submitted")],
		}):
			continue

		q = frappe.new_doc("TN Recurring Queue")
		q.template       = tmpl.name
		q.company        = tmpl.company
		q.scheduled_date = td
		q.amount         = tmpl.amount
		q.narration      = tmpl.narration or tmpl.template_name
		q.status         = "Pending"
		q.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

		# Auto-submit if configured
		if tmpl.auto_submit:
			q.confirm_submit()

	frappe.db.commit()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_recurring_template/tn_recurring_template.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document


class TNRecurringTemplate(Document):
	def validate(self):
		if self.end_date and self.start_date and self.end_date < self.start_date:
			frappe.throw(_("End Date cannot be before Start Date"))

	def next_due_date(self, from_date=None):
		"""Return the next generation date after from_date (or start_date)."""
		from frappe.utils import add_days, add_months, getdate
		base = getdate(from_date or self.last_generated_on or self.start_date)

		freq_map = {
			"Daily":     lambda d: add_days(d, 1),
			"Weekly":    lambda d: add_days(d, 7),
			"Monthly":   lambda d: add_months(d, 1),
			"Quarterly": lambda d: add_months(d, 3),
			"Annual":    lambda d: add_months(d, 12),
		}
		fn = freq_map.get(self.frequency)
		if not fn:
			return None

		next_date = fn(base) if self.last_generated_on else getdate(self.start_date)
		return str(next_date)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_reminder_config/tn_reminder_config.py
"""
TN Reminder Config — escalation ladder for overdue invoice reminders.

Scheduler: send_overdue_reminders() — daily.
Monthly statement: send_monthly_statements() — daily (fires only on configured day).
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import flt, today, getdate, date_diff


class TNReminderConfig(Document):
    pass


# ── Scheduler ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def send_overdue_reminders():
    """
    For each active reminder config, find overdue Sales Invoices and send
    reminders at the appropriate escalation level. Runs daily.
    """
    configs = frappe.db.get_all(
        "TN Reminder Config",
        filters={"is_active": 1},
        fields=["name", "company", "send_email", "send_whatsapp",
                "level_1_days", "level_1_template",
                "level_2_days", "level_2_template",
                "level_3_days", "level_3_template",
                "level_4_days", "level_4_template"],
    )

    for cfg in configs:
        overdue_invoices = frappe.db.sql("""
            SELECT name, customer, customer_name, outstanding_amount, due_date, grand_total
            FROM `tabSales Invoice`
            WHERE company = %(co)s AND docstatus = 1
                AND outstanding_amount > 0 AND due_date < %(today)s
            ORDER BY due_date
        """, {"co": cfg.company, "today": today()}, as_dict=True)

        for inv in overdue_invoices:
            days_overdue = date_diff(today(), str(inv.due_date))
            level = _determine_level(cfg, days_overdue)
            if not level:
                continue
            subject_tpl = cfg.get(f"level_{level}_template") or f"Payment Reminder — {inv.name}"
            subject = subject_tpl.replace("{invoice}", inv.name).replace("{party}", inv.customer_name)
            _send_reminder(cfg, inv, level, subject, days_overdue)

    frappe.db.commit()


def _determine_level(cfg, days_overdue):
    """Return the highest applicable escalation level for the given days overdue."""
    level = None
    for lvl in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        threshold = flt(cfg.get(f"level_{lvl}_days") or 0)
        if threshold and days_overdue >= threshold:
            level = lvl
    return level


def _send_reminder(cfg, inv, level, subject, days_overdue):
    customer_email = frappe.db.get_value("Customer", inv.customer, "email_id")
    if not customer_email:
        return

    body = f"""
    <p>Dear {inv.customer_name},</p>
    <p>This is a reminder regarding your overdue invoice:</p>
    <ul>
        <li><b>Invoice:</b> {inv.name}</li>
        <li><b>Due Date:</b> {inv.due_date}</li>
        <li><b>Outstanding Amount:</b> {frappe.format(inv.outstanding_amount, {'fieldtype':'Currency'})}</li>
        <li><b>Days Overdue:</b> {days_overdue}</li>
    </ul>
    <p>Please arrange payment at the earliest.</p>
    {"<p><b>⚠ This is a final notice. Further delay may result in legal proceedings.</b></p>" if level == 4 else ""}
    <p>Regards,<br>{cfg.company}</p>
    """

    if cfg.send_email:
        try:
            frappe.sendmail(
                recipients=[customer_email],
                subject=subject,
                message=body,
                now=True,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            frappe.log_error(f"Reminder email failed for {inv.name}: {e}", "TN Reminder")

    if cfg.send_whatsapp:
        mobile = frappe.db.get_value("Customer", inv.customer, "mobile_no")
        if mobile:
            try:
                from tallynext.tallynext.whatsapp.service import send_whatsapp
                send_whatsapp(
                    company=cfg.company,
                    mobile=mobile,
                    message_type="Payment Reminder",
                    template_name=cfg.get(f"level_{level}_template") or "payment_reminder",
                    template_params=[inv.customer_name, inv.name,
                                     str(inv.outstanding_amount), str(inv.due_date)],
                    reference_doctype="Sales Invoice",
                    reference_name=inv.name,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                frappe.log_error(f"WhatsApp reminder failed for {inv.name}: {e}", "TN Reminder")


def send_monthly_statements():
    """Send outstanding statements to all customers on configured day. Runs daily."""
    from frappe.utils import getdate, now_datetime
    today_day = getdate(today()).day

    configs = frappe.db.get_all(
        "TN Reminder Config",
        filters={"is_active": 1, "send_monthly_statement": 1},
        fields=["name", "company", "monthly_statement_day"],
    )

    for cfg in configs:
        if int(cfg.monthly_statement_day or 1) != today_day:
            continue

        # Get all customers with outstanding balances
        customers = frappe.db.sql("""
            SELECT customer, customer_name, SUM(outstanding_amount) total_outstanding
            FROM `tabSales Invoice`
            WHERE company = %(co)s AND docstatus = 1 AND outstanding_amount > 0
            GROUP BY customer, customer_name
            HAVING total_outstanding > 0
        """, {"co": cfg.company}, as_dict=True)

        for cust in customers:
            email = frappe.db.get_value("Customer", cust.customer, "email_id")
            if not email:
                continue
            _send_outstanding_statement(cfg.company, cust, email)

    frappe.db.commit()


def _send_outstanding_statement(company, cust, email):
    invoices = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT name, posting_date, grand_total, outstanding_amount, due_date
        FROM `tabSales Invoice`
        WHERE company = %(co)s AND customer = %(cu)s AND docstatus = 1 AND outstanding_amount > 0
        ORDER BY due_date
    """, {"co": company, "cu": cust.customer}, as_dict=True)

    rows = "".join(f"""
        <tr>
            <td style="padding:4px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">{inv.name}</td>
            <td style="padding:4px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">{inv.posting_date}</td>
            <td style="padding:4px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">{inv.due_date}</td>
            <td style="padding:4px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;text-align:right;">{frappe.format(inv.grand_total, {'fieldtype':'Currency'})}</td>
            <td style="padding:4px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;text-align:right;color:#c0392b;">{frappe.format(inv.outstanding_amount, {'fieldtype':'Currency'})}</td>
        </tr>""" for inv in invoices)

    body = f"""
    <p>Dear {cust.customer_name},</p>
    <p>Please find your outstanding statement as of {today()}:</p>
    <table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px;">
        <thead><tr style="background:#f0f4f8;">
            <th style="padding:6px 8px;text-align:left;">Invoice</th>
            <th style="padding:6px 8px;text-align:left;">Date</th>
            <th style="padding:6px 8px;text-align:left;">Due Date</th>
            <th style="padding:6px 8px;text-align:right;">Amount</th>
            <th style="padding:6px 8px;text-align:right;">Outstanding</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>{rows}</tbody>
    </table>
    <p><b>Total Outstanding: {frappe.format(cust.total_outstanding, {'fieldtype':'Currency'})}</b></p>
    <p>Regards,<br>{company}</p>
    """
    try:
        frappe.sendmail(
            recipients=[email],
            subject=f"Outstanding Statement — {company} — {today()}",
            message=body,
            now=True,
        )
    except Exception as e:
        frappe.log_error(f"Monthly statement failed for {cust.customer}: {e}", "TN Statement")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_tally_import_job/tn_tally_import_job.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import now


class TNTallyImportJob(Document):

	@frappe.whitelist()
	def parse_xml(self):
		"""Parse uploaded Tally XML and populate ledger mappings."""
		from tallynext.tallynext.tally_import.parser import parse_tally_xml
		file_path = frappe.get_site_path("public", self.tally_xml_file.lstrip("/"))
		data = parse_tally_xml(file_path)

		self.total_ledgers  = len(data["ledgers"])
		self.total_vouchers = len(data["vouchers"])
		self.status = "Parsed"

		# Auto-create ledger mappings
		from tallynext.tallynext.tally_import.mapper import auto_map_ledgers
		auto_map_ledgers(self.name, self.company, data["ledgers"])
		self.mapped_ledgers = frappe.db.count("TN Ledger Mapping",
			{"import_job": self.name, "erpnext_account": ["!=", ""]})

		# Cache voucher data for later import step
		frappe.cache().set_value(f"tally_import_{self.name}", frappe.as_json(data), expires_in_sec=3600)
		self.save()
		return {"ledgers": len(data["ledgers"]), "vouchers": len(data["vouchers"])}

	@frappe.whitelist()
	def validate_import(self):
		"""Run pre-import validation, return list of errors."""
		from tallynext.tallynext.tally_import.validator import validate
		errors = validate(self.name, self.company)
		if not errors:
			self.status = "Validated"
			self.save()
		return errors

	@frappe.whitelist()
	def start_import(self):
		"""Enqueue background import job."""
		if self.status != "Validated":
			frappe.throw(_("Run validation first before importing."))
		self.status = "Importing"
		self.progress_pct = 0
		self.save()
		frappe.enqueue(
			"tallynext.tallynext.tally_import.importer.run_import",
			job_name=f"tally_import_{self.name}",
			import_job=self.name,
			now=False,
			queue="long",
		)
		return "Import queued."

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_transfer_pricing_log/tn_transfer_pricing_log.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import flt

# Threshold: if actual price deviates > 10% from arm's-length, flag it
FLAG_THRESHOLD = 0.10


class TNTransferPricingLog(Document):

    def before_save(self):
        if self.amount and self.arm_length_price:
            self.variance = flt(self.amount) - flt(self.arm_length_price)
            deviation = abs(self.variance) / flt(self.arm_length_price)
            self.flagged = 1 if deviation > FLAG_THRESHOLD else 0
        else:
            self.variance = 0
            self.flagged  = 0

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/doctype/tn_voucher_lock/tn_voucher_lock.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import nowdate, now


class TNVoucherLock(Document):
	def before_save(self):
		if self.is_active and not self.locked_by:
			self.locked_by = frappe.session.user
			self.locked_on = now()

	def validate(self):
		if self.from_date and self.to_date and self.from_date > self.to_date:
			frappe.throw(_("From Date cannot be after To Date"))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public API used by hooks
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def is_period_locked(company, posting_date):
	"""Returns True if the given date falls in an active lock for the company."""
	return frappe.db.exists("TN Voucher Lock", {
		"company":   company,
		"is_active": 1,
		"from_date": ("<=", posting_date),
		"to_date":   (">=", posting_date),
	})


def enforce_period_lock(doc, method=None):
	"""
	Called via hooks on all GL-touching DocTypes.
	Blocks save/submit if the posting_date is in a locked period.
	"""
	company      = getattr(doc, "company", None)
	posting_date = getattr(doc, "posting_date", None)

	if not company or not posting_date:
		return

	# Allow if user has Period Lock Override permission
	if frappe.has_permission("TN Voucher Lock", "write"):
		return

	if is_period_locked(company, posting_date):
		frappe.throw(
			_("Posting date {0} falls in a locked accounting period. "
			  "Contact your Accounts Manager to unlock.").format(posting_date),
			title=_("Period Locked")
		)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/page/tn_bank_recon/tn_bank_recon.js

	frappe.ui.make_app_page({

			<span id="tn-br-status" style="font-size:12px; color:#888; margin-left:8px;"></span>
		</div>

		<div class="tn-br-panels">
			<div class="tn-br-panel">
				<div class="tn-br-panel-header">
					<span>Bank Statement Lines</span>

			<div class="tn-br-panel">
