	let state = { upload: null, data: null, selectedLine: null, selectedGL: null };

	// Load companies
	frappe.call({
		method: "frappe.client.get_list",
		args: { doctype: "Company", fields: ["name"], limit_page_length: 50 },
		callback(r) {
			const $sel = $body.find("#tn-br-company");
			(r.message || []).forEach(c => $sel.append(`<option value="${c.name}">${c.name}</option>`));
			load_uploads();
		},
	});

	function load_uploads() {
		const company = $body.find("#tn-br-company").val();
		if (!company) return;
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_bank_recon.tn_bank_recon.get_uploads",
			args: { company },
			callback(r) {
				const $sel = $body.find("#tn-br-upload");
				$sel.html(`<option value="">— Select Upload —</option>`);
				(r.message || []).forEach(u => {
					$sel.append(`<option value="${u.name}">${u.bank_account} | ${u.statement_date} | ${u.status}</option>`);
				});
			},
		});
	}

	$body.on("change", "#tn-br-company", load_uploads);

	$body.on("click", "#tn-br-load", () => {
		const upload = $body.find("#tn-br-upload").val();
		if (!upload) return frappe.msgprint("Select an upload first.");
		state.upload = upload;
		load_data();
	});

	function load_data() {
		$body.find("#tn-br-status").text("Loading…");
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_bank_recon.tn_bank_recon.get_recon_data",
			args: { upload_name: state.upload },
			callback(r) {
				state.data = r.message;
				render(state.data);
				$body.find("#tn-br-automatch").prop("disabled", false);
				$body.find("#tn-br-status").text("");
			},
		});
	}

	function render(data) {
		if (!data) return;
		// Bank lines
		const bankBody = $body.find("#tn-br-bank-body");
		bankBody.empty();
		let unmatched = 0;
		data.lines.forEach((ln, i) => {
			const matched = ln.match_status !== "Unmatched";
			if (!matched) unmatched++;
			const badgeClass = {
				"Auto-Matched":     "tn-br-badge-high",
				"Manually Matched": "tn-br-badge-manual",
				"Unmatched":        "tn-br-badge-unmatched",
			}[ln.match_status] || "tn-br-badge-medium";

			bankBody.append(`
				<tr class="${matched ? "tn-br-matched" : ""}" data-idx="${i}">
					<td>${ln.txn_date || ""}</td>
					<td>${ln.description || ""}</td>
					<td style="text-align:right;color:#c0392b;">${ln.debit > 0 ? fmt(ln.debit) : ""}</td>
					<td style="text-align:right;color:#27ae60;">${ln.credit > 0 ? fmt(ln.credit) : ""}</td>
					<td><span class="tn-br-badge ${badgeClass}">${ln.match_status}</span>
					${ln.matched_voucher_no ? `<br><small>${ln.matched_voucher_no}</small>` : ""}</td>
				</tr>`);
		});
		$body.find("#tn-br-bank-summary").text(`${unmatched} unmatched of ${data.lines.length}`);

		// GL entries
		const glBody = $body.find("#tn-br-gl-body");
		glBody.empty();
		data.gl_entries.forEach((g, i) => {
			glBody.append(`
				<tr data-gl-idx="${i}">
					<td>${g.posting_date}</td>
					<td><a href="/app/${g.voucher_type.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g,"-")}/${g.voucher_no}"
						target="_blank">${g.voucher_no}</a><br>
						<small style="color:#888;">${g.voucher_type}</small></td>
					<td>${g.party || ""}</td>
					<td style="text-align:right;color:#c0392b;">${g.debit > 0 ? fmt(g.debit) : ""}</td>
					<td style="text-align:right;color:#27ae60;">${g.credit > 0 ? fmt(g.credit) : ""}</td>
				</tr>`);
		});
		$body.find("#tn-br-gl-summary").text(`${data.gl_entries.length} entries`);
	}

	// Row selection for manual linking
	$body.on("click", "#tn-br-bank-body tr", function () {
		$body.find("#tn-br-bank-body tr").removeClass("tn-br-selected");
		$(this).addClass("tn-br-selected");
		state.selectedLine = $(this).data("idx");
		update_link_btn();
	});
	$body.on("click", "#tn-br-gl-body tr", function () {
		$body.find("#tn-br-gl-body tr").removeClass("tn-br-selected");
		$(this).addClass("tn-br-selected");
		state.selectedGL = $(this).data("gl-idx");
		update_link_btn();
	});

	function update_link_btn() {
		const can = state.selectedLine !== null && state.selectedGL !== null;
		$body.find("#tn-br-link").prop("disabled", !can);
	}

	$body.on("click", "#tn-br-link", () => {
		if (state.selectedLine === null || state.selectedGL === null) return;
		const gl = state.data.gl_entries[state.selectedGL];
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_bank_recon.tn_bank_recon.manual_link",
			args: {
				upload_name: state.upload,
				line_idx:    state.selectedLine,
				voucher_type: gl.voucher_type,
				voucher_no:   gl.voucher_no,
			},
			callback() {
				state.selectedLine = state.selectedGL = null;
				$body.find("#tn-br-link").prop("disabled", true);
				load_data();
			},
		});
	});

	$body.on("click", "#tn-br-automatch", () => {
		if (!state.upload) return;
		$body.find("#tn-br-status").text("Running auto-match…");
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_bank_recon.tn_bank_recon.run_auto_match",
			args: { upload_name: state.upload },
			callback(r) {
				frappe.show_alert({ message: `${r.message} entries matched.`, indicator: "green" });
				load_data();
			},
		});
	});

	$body.on("click", "#tn-br-statement", () => {
		const company = $body.find("#tn-br-company").val();
		if (!company || !state.upload) return;
		const upload = state.data;
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_bank_recon.tn_bank_recon.get_recon_statement",
			args: {
				company,
				bank_account: upload.bank_account,
				as_on_date: frappe.datetime.get_today(),
			},
			callback(r) {
				const d = r.message;
				frappe.msgprint({
					title: "Bank Reconciliation Statement",
					message: `
						<b>Balance as per Books:</b> ${fmt(d.books_balance)}<br>
						<b>Unmatched in Bank:</b> ${d.in_bank_not_books.length} item(s)<br>
						<b>Unmatched in Books:</b> ${d.in_books_not_bank.length} item(s)
					`,
					wide: true,
				});
			},
		});
	});

	function fmt(val) {
		return frappe.format(val || 0, { fieldtype: "Currency" });
	}
};

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/page/tn_bank_recon/tn_bank_recon.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_uploads(company):
	return frappe.db.get_all(
		"TN Bank Statement Upload",
		filters={"company": company, "status": ["in", ("Parsed", "Reconciling")]},
		fields=["name", "bank_account", "statement_date", "status", "total_credits", "total_debits"],
		order_by="statement_date desc",
		limit=20,
	)


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_recon_data(upload_name):
	"""Return parsed lines + unmatched ERPNext entries for two-panel view."""
	doc = frappe.get_doc("TN Bank Statement Upload", upload_name)

	bank_gl = frappe.db.get_value("Bank Account", doc.bank_account, "account")

	# Unmatched GL entries on the bank account within ±30 days of statement_date
	from frappe.utils import add_days
	from_date = str(add_days(doc.statement_date, -30))
	to_date   = str(add_days(doc.statement_date,  30))

	gl_entries = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT
			g.name, g.voucher_no, g.voucher_type,
			g.posting_date, g.debit, g.credit,
			g.party, g.remarks
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.account = %(account)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
		ORDER BY g.posting_date
	""", {"company": doc.company, "account": bank_gl,
		  "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)

	# Filter already-matched vouchers
	matched_vouchers = {ln.matched_voucher_no for ln in doc.lines if ln.matched_voucher_no}
	unmatched_gl = [g for g in gl_entries if g.voucher_no not in matched_vouchers]

	return {
		"lines":        [dict(ln.as_dict()) for ln in doc.lines],
		"gl_entries":   unmatched_gl,
		"bank_account": doc.bank_account,
		"bank_gl":      bank_gl,
		"company":      doc.company,
	}


@frappe.whitelist()
def manual_link(upload_name, line_idx, voucher_type, voucher_no):
	"""Manually link a statement line to an ERPNext voucher."""
	doc = frappe.get_doc("TN Bank Statement Upload", upload_name)
	line = doc.lines[int(line_idx)]
	line.match_status      = "Manually Matched"
	line.matched_voucher_type = voucher_type
	line.matched_voucher_no   = voucher_no
	line.confidence        = "High"
	doc.save()
	return "ok"


@frappe.whitelist()
def run_auto_match(upload_name):
	from tallynext.tallynext.bank_recon.matcher import run_auto_match as _run
	return _run(upload_name)


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_recon_statement(company, bank_account, as_on_date):
	"""Bank Reconciliation Statement data."""
	bank_gl = frappe.db.get_value("Bank Account", bank_account, "account")
	if not bank_gl:
		frappe.throw(_("Bank Account has no linked GL Account"))

	# Balance as per books
	books_bal = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(debit - credit), 0) AS bal
		FROM `tabGL Entry`
		WHERE account = %(account)s
			AND company = %(company)s
			AND posting_date <= %(date)s
			AND is_cancelled = 0
	""", {"account": bank_gl, "company": company, "date": as_on_date}, as_dict=True)
	books_balance = flt(books_bal[0].bal if books_bal else 0)

	# Items in bank not in books (Pending statement lines)
	uploads = frappe.db.get_all("TN Bank Statement Upload",
		filters={"company": company, "bank_account": bank_account,
				 "statement_date": ("<=", as_on_date)},
		pluck="name")

	in_bank_not_books = []
	in_books_not_bank = []

	for upl in uploads:
		doc = frappe.get_doc("TN Bank Statement Upload", upl)
		for ln in doc.lines:
			if ln.match_status == "Unmatched":
				in_bank_not_books.append({
					"date": ln.txn_date,
					"description": ln.description,
					"debit": flt(ln.debit),
					"credit": flt(ln.credit),
				})

	return {
		"books_balance":      books_balance,
		"in_bank_not_books":  in_bank_not_books,
		"in_books_not_bank":  in_books_not_bank,
	}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/page/tn_compliance_calendar/tn_compliance_calendar.js
frappe.pages["tn-compliance-calendar"].on_page_load = function (wrapper) {
	frappe.ui.make_app_page({
		parent: wrapper,
		title: "Compliance Calendar",
		single_column: true,
	});

	const $body = $(wrapper).find(".page-content");
	$body.html(`
		<style>
			.tn-cc-toolbar { display:flex; gap:8px; padding:12px 8px; flex-wrap:wrap; align-items:center; }
			.tn-cc-month-nav { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:6px; }
			.tn-cc-month-label { font-size:15px; font-weight:700; min-width:130px; text-align:center; }
			.tn-cc-grid { display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(7,1fr); gap:4px; padding:0 8px; }
			.tn-cc-day-header { text-align:center; font-size:11px; font-weight:700; color:#888; padding:4px; }
			.tn-cc-day { min-height:80px; border:1px solid #eee; border-radius:3px; padding:4px; font-size:12px; }
			.tn-cc-day-num { font-size:11px; color:#aaa; margin-bottom:2px; }
			.tn-cc-day.today { border-color:#1e6bb8; }
			.tn-cc-item { border-radius:3px; padding:2px 5px; margin-bottom:2px; cursor:pointer; font-size:11px; }
			.tn-cc-item.Pending  { background:#fff9c4; color:#f57f17; }
			.tn-cc-item.Overdue  { background:#ffccbc; color:#bf360c; font-weight:700; }
			.tn-cc-item.Filed    { background:#c8e6c9; color:#2e7d32; }
			.tn-cc-item.Not\.Applicable { background:#f5f5f5; color:#999; }
			.tn-cc-list { padding:0 8px 20px; }
			.tn-cc-list-row { display:flex; align-items:center; padding:8px 10px; border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0; gap:10px; }
			.tn-cc-list-row:hover { background:#f9fbff; }
			.tn-cc-badge { font-size:10px; padding:2px 8px; border-radius:10px; font-weight:700; }
			.tn-cc-badge.Pending  { background:#fff9c4; color:#f57f17; }
			.tn-cc-badge.Overdue  { background:#ffccbc; color:#bf360c; }
			.tn-cc-badge.Filed    { background:#c8e6c9; color:#2e7d32; }
		</style>

		<div class="tn-cc-toolbar">
			<select id="tn-cc-company" class="form-control input-sm" style="width:180px;"></select>
			<div class="tn-cc-month-nav">
				<button class="btn btn-default btn-sm" id="tn-cc-prev">‹</button>
				<span class="tn-cc-month-label" id="tn-cc-month-label"></span>
				<button class="btn btn-default btn-sm" id="tn-cc-next">›</button>
			</div>
			<select id="tn-cc-status-filter" class="form-control input-sm" style="width:130px;">
				<option value="">All Statuses</option>
				<option value="Pending">Pending</option>
				<option value="Overdue">Overdue</option>
				<option value="Filed">Filed</option>
			</select>
			<button class="btn btn-default btn-sm" id="tn-cc-list-view" title="List View">☰ List</button>
			<button class="btn btn-default btn-sm" id="tn-cc-cal-view" title="Calendar View">📅 Calendar</button>
			<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="tn-cc-add">+ Add Item</button>
		</div>

		<div id="tn-cc-calendar-view">
			<div class="tn-cc-grid" id="tn-cc-grid"></div>
		</div>
		<div id="tn-cc-list-view-panel" style="display:none;">
			<div class="tn-cc-list" id="tn-cc-list"></div>
		</div>
	`);

	let cur_year  = new Date().getFullYear();
	let cur_month = new Date().getMonth() + 1;
	let all_items = [];

	const MONTH_NAMES = ["Jan","Feb","Mar","Apr","May","Jun","Jul","Aug","Sep","Oct","Nov","Dec"];

	// Load companies
	frappe.call({
		method: "frappe.client.get_list",
		args: { doctype: "Company", fields: ["name"], limit_page_length: 50 },
		callback(r) {
			const $sel = $body.find("#tn-cc-company");
			(r.message || []).forEach(c => $sel.append(`<option value="${c.name}">${c.name}</option>`));
			load_items();
		},
	});

	function month_str() {
		return `${cur_year}-${String(cur_month).padStart(2, "0")}`;
	}

	function load_items() {
		const company = $body.find("#tn-cc-company").val();
		const status  = $body.find("#tn-cc-status-filter").val();
		if (!company) return;

		$body.find("#tn-cc-month-label").text(`${MONTH_NAMES[cur_month - 1]} ${cur_year}`);

		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_compliance_calendar.tn_compliance_calendar.get_items",
			args: { company, month: month_str(), status: status || null },
			callback(r) {
				all_items = r.message || [];
				render();
			},
		});
	}

	function render() {
		if ($body.find("#tn-cc-calendar-view").is(":visible")) {
			render_calendar();
		} else {
			render_list();
		}
	}

	function render_calendar() {
		const $grid = $body.find("#tn-cc-grid");
		$grid.empty();
		const days = ["Sun","Mon","Tue","Wed","Thu","Fri","Sat"];
		days.forEach(d => $grid.append(`<div class="tn-cc-day-header">${d}</div>`));

		const firstDay = new Date(cur_year, cur_month - 1, 1).getDay();
		const daysInMonth = new Date(cur_year, cur_month, 0).getDate();
		const today_str = frappe.datetime.get_today();

		for (let i = 0; i < firstDay; i++) {
			$grid.append(`<div></div>`);
		}

		for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {
			const date_str = `${cur_year}-${String(cur_month).padStart(2,"0")}-${String(d).padStart(2,"0")}`;
			const is_today = date_str === today_str;
			const day_items = all_items.filter(i => String(i.due_date) === date_str);
			let items_html = day_items.map(i =>
				`<div class="tn-cc-item ${i.status}" title="${i.compliance_name}"
					data-name="${i.name}">
					${i.compliance_name.substring(0, 18)}${i.compliance_name.length > 18 ? "…" : ""}
				</div>`
			).join("");

			$grid.append(`
				<div class="tn-cc-day${is_today ? " today" : ""}">
					<div class="tn-cc-day-num">${d}</div>
					${items_html}
				</div>`);
		}

		$grid.on("click", ".tn-cc-item", function () {
			open_item_dialog($(this).data("name"));
		});
	}

	function render_list() {
		const $list = $body.find("#tn-cc-list");
		$list.empty();
		if (!all_items.length) {
			$list.html(`<div class="text-muted" style="padding:20px;">No compliance items for this month.</div>`);
			return;
		}
		all_items.forEach(item => {
			$list.append(`
				<div class="tn-cc-list-row">
					<span style="min-width:90px; color:#888; font-size:12px;">${item.due_date}</span>
					<span style="flex:1; font-weight:600;">${item.compliance_name}</span>
					<span style="font-size:11px; color:#888; min-width:80px;">${item.source_module || ""}</span>
					<span class="tn-cc-badge ${item.status}">${item.status}</span>
					${item.status !== "Filed"
						? `<button class="btn btn-xs btn-success tn-cc-file-btn" data-name="${item.name}">Mark Filed</button>`
						: `<span style="font-size:11px; color:#888;">${item.filing_reference || ""}</span>`}
				</div>`);
		});

		$list.on("click", ".tn-cc-file-btn", function () {
			open_item_dialog($(this).data("name"));
		});
	}

	function open_item_dialog(item_name) {
		const item = all_items.find(i => i.name === item_name);
		if (!item) return;

		const d = new frappe.ui.Dialog({
			title: item.compliance_name,
			fields: [
				{ fieldname: "status",     fieldtype: "Data", label: "Status", read_only: 1, default: item.status },
				{ fieldname: "due_date",   fieldtype: "Date", label: "Due Date", read_only: 1, default: item.due_date },
				{ fieldname: "filed_on",   fieldtype: "Date", label: "Filed On", default: frappe.datetime.get_today() },
				{ fieldname: "reference",  fieldtype: "Data", label: "Filing Reference / ARN" },
			],
			primary_action_label: "Mark as Filed",
			primary_action(values) {
				frappe.call({
					method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_compliance_calendar.tn_compliance_calendar.mark_filed",
					args: { name: item_name, reference: values.reference, filed_on: values.filed_on },
					callback() {
						d.hide();
						frappe.show_alert({ message: "Marked as Filed", indicator: "green" });
						load_items();
					},
				});
			},
		});
		d.show();
	}

	// Navigation
	$body.on("click", "#tn-cc-prev", () => {
		cur_month--;
		if (cur_month < 1) { cur_month = 12; cur_year--; }
		load_items();
	});
	$body.on("click", "#tn-cc-next", () => {
		cur_month++;
		if (cur_month > 12) { cur_month = 1; cur_year++; }
		load_items();
	});
	$body.on("change", "#tn-cc-company, #tn-cc-status-filter", load_items);

	// View toggle
	$body.on("click", "#tn-cc-cal-view", () => {
		$body.find("#tn-cc-calendar-view").show();
		$body.find("#tn-cc-list-view-panel").hide();
		render_calendar();
	});
	$body.on("click", "#tn-cc-list-view", () => {
		$body.find("#tn-cc-calendar-view").hide();
		$body.find("#tn-cc-list-view-panel").show();
		render_list();
	});

	// Add custom item
	$body.on("click", "#tn-cc-add", () => {
		const company = $body.find("#tn-cc-company").val();
		if (!company) return frappe.msgprint("Select a company first.");
		const d = new frappe.ui.Dialog({
			title: "Add Compliance Item",
			fields: [
				{ fieldname: "compliance_name", fieldtype: "Data", label: "Item Name", reqd: 1 },
				{ fieldname: "due_date",        fieldtype: "Date", label: "Due Date",  reqd: 1 },
				{ fieldname: "frequency",       fieldtype: "Select", label: "Frequency",
				  options: "One-Time\nMonthly\nQuarterly\nHalf-Yearly\nAnnual", default: "One-Time" },
			],
			primary_action_label: "Add",
			primary_action(values) {
				frappe.call({
					method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_compliance_calendar.tn_compliance_calendar.add_custom_item",
					args: { company, ...values },
					callback() {
						d.hide();
						load_items();
					},
				});
			},
		});
		d.show();
	});
};

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/page/tn_compliance_calendar/tn_compliance_calendar.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import today, add_days


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_items(company, month=None, status=None):
	"""Return compliance items for the given company, optionally filtered by month/status."""
	filters = {"company": company}
	if status:
		filters["status"] = status

	items = frappe.db.get_all(
		"TN Compliance Item",
		filters=filters,
		fields=["name", "compliance_name", "due_date", "frequency",
				"status", "source_module", "filing_reference", "filed_on"],
		order_by="due_date asc",
	)

	if month:
		# Filter by YYYY-MM
		items = [i for i in items if str(i.due_date)[:7] == month]

	return items


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_dashboard_widget(company):
	"""Items due in the next 7 days — for dashboard."""
	td = today()
	future = add_days(td, 7)
	return frappe.db.get_all(
		"TN Compliance Item",
		filters={
			"company": company,
			"status": ["in", ("Pending", "Overdue")],
			"due_date": ("between", [td, future]),
		},
		fields=["name", "compliance_name", "due_date", "status", "source_module"],
		order_by="due_date asc",
	)


@frappe.whitelist()
def mark_filed(name, reference=None, filed_on=None):
	doc = frappe.get_doc("TN Compliance Item", name)
	doc.mark_filed(reference=reference, filed_on=filed_on)
	return "ok"


@frappe.whitelist()
def add_custom_item(company, compliance_name, due_date, frequency="One-Time"):
	from tallynext.tallynext.doctype.tn_compliance_item.tn_compliance_item import tn_add_compliance_item
	tn_add_compliance_item(company, compliance_name, due_date, frequency, source_module="Custom")
	frappe.db.commit()
	return "ok"

	function load_dashboard() {
		const company = $body.find("#tn-dash-company").val();
		if (!company) return;

		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_dashboard.tn_dashboard.get_dashboard_data",
			args: { company },
			callback(r) {
				if (r.exc) return;
				render_widgets(r.message, $body);
			},
		});
	}

	$body.on("change", "#tn-dash-company", load_dashboard);
	// Auto-refresh every 5 min
	setInterval(load_dashboard, 300000);
};

function render_widgets(data, $body) {
	$body.find("#tn-widgets").html(`
		${widget("Cash Balance", data.cash_balance, "All cash accounts", data.cash_balance < 0 ? "danger" : "success")}
		${widget("Bank Balance", data.bank_balance, "All bank accounts", data.bank_balance < 0 ? "danger" : "success")}
		${widget("Receivables Due Today", data.receivables_due, "Invoices due today", data.receivables_due > 0 ? "danger" : "")}
		${widget("Payables Due Today", data.payables_due, "Bills due today", data.payables_due > 0 ? "danger" : "")}
	`);

	$body.find("#tn-widgets-2").html(`
		${count_widget("Compliance Due (7 days)", data.compliance_due || 0,
			"items", data.compliance_due > 0 ? "danger" : "", "/app/tn-compliance-calendar")}
		${count_widget("Pending Auto-Entries", data.pending_entries || 0,
			"recurring entries", data.pending_entries > 0 ? "danger" : "", "/app/tn-recurring-queue")}
	`);

	// Overdue section
	let overdue_html = "";
	if (data.overdue_receivables > 0 || data.overdue_payables > 0) {
		overdue_html = `<div class="tn-overdue-bar">
			<h6>⚠ Overdue</h6>`;
		if (data.overdue_receivables > 0) {
			overdue_html += `<div class="tn-overdue-item">
				<span>Receivables overdue (past due date)</span>
				<span style="color:#c0392b;font-weight:600;">${fmt_currency(data.overdue_receivables)}</span>
			</div>`;
		}
		if (data.overdue_payables > 0) {
			overdue_html += `<div class="tn-overdue-item">
				<span>Payables overdue (past due date)</span>
				<span style="color:#c0392b;font-weight:600;">${fmt_currency(data.overdue_payables)}</span>
			</div>`;
		}
		overdue_html += "</div>";
	}
	$body.find("#tn-overdue-section").html(overdue_html);
}

function widget(label, value, sub, cls) {
	return `<div class="tn-widget ${cls || ""}">
		<div class="tn-widget-label">${label}</div>
		<div class="tn-widget-value">${fmt_currency(value)}</div>
		<div class="tn-widget-sub">${sub}</div>
	</div>`;
}

function count_widget(label, count, sub, cls, href) {
	return `<a href="${href}" style="text-decoration:none;">
		<div class="tn-widget ${cls || ""}" style="cursor:pointer;">
			<div class="tn-widget-label">${label}</div>
			<div class="tn-widget-value">${count}</div>
			<div class="tn-widget-sub">${sub}</div>
		</div>
	</a>`;
}

function loading_widget(label) {
	return `<div class="tn-widget">
		<div class="tn-widget-label">${label}</div>
		<div class="tn-widget-value text-muted">—</div>
	</div>`;
}

function fmt_currency(val) {
	return frappe.format(val || 0, { fieldtype: "Currency" });
}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/page/tn_dashboard/tn_dashboard.py
import frappe
from frappe.utils import getdate, nowdate, add_days, flt


def has_permission(*args, **kwargs):
	return frappe.has_permission("Sales Invoice", "read")


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_dashboard_data(company):
	"""Return all dashboard widget data in one call."""
	today = nowdate()

	return {
		"cash_balance":       _get_account_balance(company, "Cash", today),
		"bank_balance":       _get_account_balance(company, "Bank", today),
		"receivables_due":    _get_outstanding_due(company, "Customer", today),
		"payables_due":       _get_outstanding_due(company, "Supplier", today),
		"overdue_receivables":_get_overdue(company, "Customer", today),
		"overdue_payables":   _get_overdue(company, "Supplier", today),
		"compliance_due":     _get_compliance_due(company, today),
		"pending_entries":    _get_pending_recurring(company),
		"today":              today,
		"company":            company,
	}


def _get_account_balance(company, account_type, date):
	"""Sum GL entries for all accounts of given type up to date."""
	result = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT
			SUM(gle.debit - gle.credit) as balance
		FROM `tabGL Entry` gle
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` acc ON acc.name = gle.account
		WHERE gle.company = %(company)s
			AND acc.account_type = %(account_type)s
			AND gle.posting_date <= %(date)s
			AND gle.is_cancelled = 0
	""", {"company": company, "account_type": account_type, "date": date}, as_dict=True)

	return flt(result[0].balance if result else 0)


def _get_outstanding_due(company, party_type, date):
	"""Total outstanding invoices due today."""
	doctype = "Sales Invoice" if party_type == "Customer" else "Purchase Invoice"
	result = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(outstanding_amount), 0) as total
		FROM `tab{doctype}`
		WHERE company = %(company)s
			AND docstatus = 1
			AND outstanding_amount > 0
			AND due_date = %(date)s
	""".format(doctype=doctype), {"company": company, "date": date}, as_dict=True)

	return flt(result[0].total if result else 0)


def _get_compliance_due(company, today):
	"""Items due in the next 7 days."""
	future = add_days(today, 7)
	return frappe.db.count("TN Compliance Item", {
		"company": company,
		"status": ["in", ("Pending", "Overdue")],
		"due_date": ("between", [today, future]),
	})


def _get_pending_recurring(company):
	"""Pending auto-entries in the recurring queue."""
	return frappe.db.count("TN Recurring Queue", {
		"company": company,
		"status": "Pending",
	})


def _get_overdue(company, party_type, date):
	"""Total overdue outstanding (due_date < today)."""
	doctype = "Sales Invoice" if party_type == "Customer" else "Purchase Invoice"
	result = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(outstanding_amount), 0) as total
		FROM `tab{doctype}`
		WHERE company = %(company)s
			AND docstatus = 1
			AND outstanding_amount > 0
			AND due_date < %(date)s
	""".format(doctype=doctype), {"company": company, "date": date}, as_dict=True)

	return flt(result[0].total if result else 0)

			<button class="btn btn-default tn-db-nav-btn" id="tn-db-prev" title="Previous day (←)">‹</button>
			<span class="tn-db-date" id="tn-db-date-label">—</span>
			<button class="btn btn-default tn-db-nav-btn" id="tn-db-next" title="Next day (→)">›</button>
			<input type="date" id="tn-db-date" class="form-control input-sm" style="width:150px;">
			<select id="tn-db-company" class="form-control input-sm" style="width:180px;"></select>
			<select id="tn-db-type-filter" class="form-control input-sm" style="width:150px;">
				<option value="All">All Voucher Types</option>

		</div>
	`);

	let current_date = frappe.datetime.get_today();

	// Load companies
	frappe.call({
		method: "frappe.client.get_list",
		args: { doctype: "Company", fields: ["name"], limit_page_length: 50 },

				load_day_book();

	function load_day_book() {
		$body.find("#tn-db-date").val(current_date);
		$body.find("#tn-db-date-label").text(frappe.datetime.str_to_user(current_date));

		const company = $body.find("#tn-db-company").val();
		const voucher_type = $body.find("#tn-db-type-filter").val();
		const search = $body.find("#tn-db-search").val();

		if (!company) return;

		$body.find("#tn-db-body").html(`<div class="text-muted" style="padding:20px 16px;">Loading…</div>`);

		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_day_book.tn_day_book.get_day_book",
			args: { company, date: current_date, voucher_type, search },
			callback(r) {
				if (r.exc) return;
				render_day_book(r.message, $body);
			},
		});
	}

	function render_day_book(data, $body) {
		const $out = $body.find("#tn-db-body");
		if (!data || !data.vouchers || !data.vouchers.length) {
			$out.html(`<div class="text-muted" style="padding:20px 16px;">No transactions for this date.</div>`);
			return;
		}

		let html = `<div style="padding:0 0 4px 8px;font-size:12px;color:#888;">
			${data.count} voucher(s) &nbsp;·&nbsp; Dr: <strong>${fmt_currency(data.grand_debit)}</strong>
			&nbsp;·&nbsp; Cr: <strong>${fmt_currency(data.grand_credit)}</strong>
		</div>`;

		data.vouchers.forEach((v) => {
			const entries_html = v.entries.map((e) => `
				<tr>
					<td style="padding-left:20px;">${e.account}${e.party ? ` (${e.party})` : ""}</td>
					<td class="tn-debit-col">${e.debit > 0 ? fmt_currency(e.debit) : ""}</td>
					<td class="tn-credit-col">${e.credit > 0 ? fmt_currency(e.credit) : ""}</td>
					<td style="font-size:11px;color:#888;">${e.remarks || ""}</td>
				</tr>`).join("");

			html += `
			<div class="tn-voucher-block">
				<div class="tn-voucher-header" data-voucher="${v.voucher_no}">
					<span>
						<span class="tn-voucher-type-tag">${v.voucher_type.replace(" Entry", "").replace(" Invoice", " Inv.")}</span>
						&nbsp;
						<a href="/app/${v.voucher_type.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g, "-")}/${v.voucher_no}"
							onclick="event.stopPropagation()" target="_blank"
							style="font-weight:600;font-size:13px;">${v.voucher_no}</a>
						<span class="text-muted" style="font-size:11px;margin-left:6px;">${v.narration || ""}</span>
					</span>
					<span>
						<span class="tn-debit-col" style="margin-right:10px;">${fmt_currency(v.total_debit)}</span>
						<span class="tn-credit-col">${fmt_currency(v.total_credit)}</span>
					</span>
				</div>
				<div class="tn-voucher-entries">
					<table>
						<thead>
							<tr style="background:#f8f9fa;">
								<th style="padding:4px 10px;font-size:11px;">Account</th>
								<th style="padding:4px 10px;font-size:11px;text-align:right;">Dr</th>
								<th style="padding:4px 10px;font-size:11px;text-align:right;">Cr</th>
								<th style="padding:4px 10px;font-size:11px;">Narration</th>
							</tr>
						</thead>
						<tbody>${entries_html}</tbody>
					</table>
				</div>
			</div>`;
		});

		html += `<div class="tn-voucher-block">
			<table style="width:100%;">
				<tr class="tn-grand-total">
					<td style="padding:8px 10px;"><strong>Grand Total</strong></td>
					<td class="tn-debit-col" style="padding:8px 10px;"><strong>${fmt_currency(data.grand_debit)}</strong></td>
					<td class="tn-credit-col" style="padding:8px 10px;"><strong>${fmt_currency(data.grand_credit)}</strong></td>
					<td style="padding:8px 10px;"></td>
				</tr>
			</table>
		</div>`;

		$out.html(html);

		// Toggle entries on click
		$out.find(".tn-voucher-header").on("click", function () {
			$(this).next(".tn-voucher-entries").slideToggle(100);
		});
	}

	// Navigation
	$body.on("click", "#tn-db-prev", () => {
		current_date = frappe.datetime.add_days(current_date, -1);
		load_day_book();
	});
	$body.on("click", "#tn-db-next", () => {
		current_date = frappe.datetime.add_days(current_date, 1);
		load_day_book();
	});
	$body.on("change", "#tn-db-date", () => {
		current_date = $body.find("#tn-db-date").val();
		load_day_book();
	});
	$body.on("change", "#tn-db-company, #tn-db-type-filter", () => load_day_book());
	$body.on("input", "#tn-db-search", frappe.utils.debounce(load_day_book, 400));

	// Keyboard left/right for day navigation
	$(document).off("keydown.tn_db").on("keydown.tn_db", function (e) {
		if (!$body.is(":visible")) return;
		if (e.target.tagName === "INPUT" || e.target.tagName === "SELECT") return;
		if (e.key === "ArrowLeft") { current_date = frappe.datetime.add_days(current_date, -1); load_day_book(); }
		if (e.key === "ArrowRight") { current_date = frappe.datetime.add_days(current_date, 1); load_day_book(); }
	});

	// Print
	$body.on("click", "#tn-db-print", () => {
		const company = $body.find("#tn-db-company").val() || "";
		const label = $body.find("#tn-db-date-label").text();
		$body.find("#tn-db-print-header").text(`${company} — Day Book — ${label}`);
		// Expand all voucher entries before printing
		$body.find(".tn-voucher-entries").show();
		window.print();
		$body.find(".tn-voucher-entries").hide();
	});
};

function fmt_currency(val) {
	return frappe.format(val || 0, { fieldtype: "Currency" });
}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/page/tn_day_book/tn_day_book.py
import frappe
from frappe.utils import getdate, nowdate, add_days, format_date


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_day_book(company, date, voucher_type=None, search=None):
	"""Return all GL entries for a given date, grouped by voucher."""
	filters = {
		"company": company,
		"posting_date": getdate(date),
		"is_cancelled": 0,
	}

	gle = frappe.get_all(
		"GL Entry",
		filters=filters,
		fields=[
			"posting_date", "account", "party_type", "party",
			"debit", "credit", "voucher_type", "voucher_no",
			"remarks", "against", "cost_center", "creation",
		],
		order_by="creation asc",
	)

	# Filter by voucher_type
	if voucher_type and voucher_type != "All":
		gle = [g for g in gle if g.voucher_type == voucher_type]

	# Filter by search (account or party or voucher_no)
	if search:
		s = search.lower()
		gle = [
			g for g in gle
			if s in (g.account or "").lower()
			or s in (g.party or "").lower()
			or s in (g.voucher_no or "").lower()
			or s in (g.remarks or "").lower()
		]

	# Group by voucher_no
	vouchers = {}
	for g in gle:
		vn = g.voucher_no
		if vn not in vouchers:
			vouchers[vn] = {
				"voucher_no": vn,
				"voucher_type": g.voucher_type,
				"posting_date": g.posting_date,
				"narration": g.remarks or "",
				"entries": [],
				"total_debit": 0,
				"total_credit": 0,
			}
		vouchers[vn]["entries"].append(g)
		vouchers[vn]["total_debit"] += float(g.debit or 0)
		vouchers[vn]["total_credit"] += float(g.credit or 0)

	result = list(vouchers.values())

	# Grand totals
	grand_dr = sum(float(g.debit or 0) for g in gle)
	grand_cr = sum(float(g.credit or 0) for g in gle)

	return {
		"vouchers": result,
		"grand_debit": grand_dr,
		"grand_credit": grand_cr,
		"count": len(result),
		"date": date,
	}

			<div class="tn-mob-kpi-grid" id="tn-mob-kpis">
				<div class="tn-mob-kpi"><div class="tn-mob-kpi-val">—</div><div class="tn-mob-kpi-lbl">Revenue (FY)</div></div>
				<div class="tn-mob-kpi"><div class="tn-mob-kpi-val">—</div><div class="tn-mob-kpi-lbl">Net Profit</div></div>
				<div class="tn-mob-kpi"><div class="tn-mob-kpi-val">—</div><div class="tn-mob-kpi-lbl">Cash & Bank</div></div>
				<div class="tn-mob-kpi warn"><div class="tn-mob-kpi-val">—</div><div class="tn-mob-kpi-lbl">Overdue AR</div></div>
			</div>

			<!-- Recent Vouchers -->
			<div class="tn-mob-section">
				<h6>Recent Vouchers</h6>
				<div id="tn-mob-recent"><div class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px;">Loading…</div></div>
			</div>

			<!-- Pending Approvals -->
			<div class="tn-mob-section">
				<h6>Pending Approvals</h6>
				<div id="tn-mob-approvals"><div class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px;">Loading…</div></div>
			</div>
		</div>
	`);

	// ─── Init ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	frappe.call({
		method: "frappe.client.get_list",
		args: { doctype: "Company", fields: ["name"], limit_page_length: 50 },
		callback(r) {
			const $sel = $body.find("#tn-mob-co");
			(r.message || []).forEach(c => $sel.append(`<option value="${c.name}">${c.name}</option>`));
			load_data();
		},
	});

	function fmt_num(n) {
		if (Math.abs(n) >= 10000000) return (n / 10000000).toFixed(2) + " Cr";
		if (Math.abs(n) >= 100000)  return (n / 100000).toFixed(2) + " L";
		return frappe.format(n, {fieldtype: "Currency"});
	}

	function load_data() {
		const company = $body.find("#tn-mob-co").val();
		if (!company) return;
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_mobile_dashboard.tn_mobile_dashboard.get_mobile_summary",
			args: { company },
			callback(r) {
				const d = r.message || {};
				const profit_cls = d.net_profit >= 0 ? "good" : "warn";
				$body.find("#tn-mob-kpis").html(`
					<div class="tn-mob-kpi"><div class="tn-mob-kpi-val">${fmt_num(d.revenue)}</div><div class="tn-mob-kpi-lbl">Revenue (FY)</div></div>
					<div class="tn-mob-kpi ${profit_cls}"><div class="tn-mob-kpi-val">${fmt_num(d.net_profit)}</div><div class="tn-mob-kpi-lbl">Net Profit</div></div>
					<div class="tn-mob-kpi good"><div class="tn-mob-kpi-val">${fmt_num(d.cash)}</div><div class="tn-mob-kpi-lbl">Cash & Bank</div></div>
					<div class="tn-mob-kpi warn"><div class="tn-mob-kpi-val">${fmt_num(d.overdue_ar)}</div><div class="tn-mob-kpi-lbl">Overdue AR</div></div>
				`);
				const $recent = $body.find("#tn-mob-recent");
				$recent.empty();
				(d.recent || []).forEach(v => {
					$recent.append(`
						<div class="tn-mob-vch-row">
							<span><a href="/app/${v.voucher_type.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g,"-")}/${v.voucher_no}" target="_blank">${v.voucher_no}</a></span>
							<span style="color:#888;">${v.posting_date}</span>
							<span>${fmt_num(v.total_debit)}</span>
						</div>`);
				});
				if (!d.recent || !d.recent.length) $recent.html('<div class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px;">No recent vouchers.</div>');
			},
		});

		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_mobile_dashboard.tn_mobile_dashboard.get_pending_approvals",
			args: { company },
			callback(r) {
				const $appr = $body.find("#tn-mob-approvals");
				$appr.empty();
				const items = r.message || [];
				if (!items.length) {
					$appr.html('<div class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px;">No pending approvals.</div>');
					return;
				}
				items.forEach(v => {
					$appr.append(`
						<div class="tn-mob-vch-row" data-name="${v.name}">
							<div style="flex:1;">
								<div style="font-weight:600;">${v.name}</div>
								<div style="color:#888;">${v.voucher_type} · ${v.posting_date}</div>
								<div style="color:#888;font-size:11px;">${v.user_remark || ""}</div>
							</div>
							<div style="text-align:right;">
								<div style="font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px;">${fmt_num(v.total_debit)}</div>
								<button class="tn-mob-approve-btn" data-name="${v.name}">Approve</button>
							</div>
						</div>`);
				});
			},
		});
	}

	// Refresh
	$body.on("click", "#tn-mob-refresh", load_data);
	$body.on("change", "#tn-mob-co", load_data);

	// Quick actions
	$body.on("click", ".tn-mob-qa", function () {
		frappe.set_route($(this).data("route"));
	});

	// Approve voucher
	$body.on("click", ".tn-mob-approve-btn", function () {
		const name = $(this).data("name");
		frappe.confirm(`Submit ${name}?`, () => {
			frappe.call({
				method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_mobile_dashboard.tn_mobile_dashboard.approve_voucher",
				args: { docname: name },
				callback() {
					frappe.show_alert({ message: `${name} submitted.`, indicator: "green" });
					load_data();
				},
			});
		});
	});
};


function _register_pwa() {
	if (!("serviceWorker" in navigator)) return;
	navigator.serviceWorker.register("/assets/tallynext/sw/tn_service_worker.js", { scope: "/" })
		.then(() => {
			// Inject manifest link if not already present
			if (!document.querySelector('link[rel="manifest"]')) {
				const link = document.createElement("link");
				link.rel  = "manifest";
				link.href = "/assets/tallynext/manifest/manifest.json";
				document.head.appendChild(link);
			}
		})
		.catch(err => console.warn("TallyNext SW registration failed:", err));
}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/page/tn_mobile_dashboard/tn_mobile_dashboard.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt, today, getdate


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_mobile_summary(company):
    """KPIs for the mobile dashboard (lightweight query)."""
    fy = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT year_start_date, year_end_date FROM `tabFiscal Year`
        WHERE year_start_date <= %s AND year_end_date >= %s
        LIMIT 1
    """, (today(), today()), as_dict=True)

    start = fy[0].year_start_date if fy else "2000-01-01"
    end   = today()

    def _sum(sql, params):
        r = frappe.db.sql(sql, params)
        return flt(r[0][0] if r else 0)

    revenue = _sum("""
        SELECT SUM(credit - debit) FROM `tabGL Entry`
        WHERE company=%(co)s AND is_cancelled=0
          AND account IN (SELECT name FROM `tabAccount` WHERE root_type='Income' AND company=%(co)s)
          AND posting_date BETWEEN %(s)s AND %(e)s
    """, {"co": company, "s": start, "e": end})

    expenses = _sum("""
        SELECT SUM(debit - credit) FROM `tabGL Entry`
        WHERE company=%(co)s AND is_cancelled=0
          AND account IN (SELECT name FROM `tabAccount` WHERE root_type='Expense' AND company=%(co)s)
          AND posting_date BETWEEN %(s)s AND %(e)s
    """, {"co": company, "s": start, "e": end})

    cash = _sum("""
        SELECT SUM(debit - credit) FROM `tabGL Entry`
        WHERE company=%(co)s AND is_cancelled=0
          AND account IN (SELECT name FROM `tabAccount`
              WHERE root_type='Asset' AND account_type IN ('Cash','Bank') AND company=%(co)s)
          AND posting_date <= %(e)s
    """, {"co": company, "e": end})

    overdue_ar = _sum("""
        SELECT SUM(outstanding_amount) FROM `tabSales Invoice`
        WHERE company=%(co)s AND docstatus=1 AND outstanding_amount > 0 AND due_date < %(e)s
    """, {"co": company, "e": end})

    # Recent vouchers (last 5)
    recent = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT voucher_no, voucher_type, posting_date,
               SUM(debit) total_debit, SUM(credit) total_credit
        FROM `tabGL Entry`
        WHERE company=%(co)s AND is_cancelled=0
        GROUP BY voucher_no, voucher_type, posting_date
        ORDER BY posting_date DESC, creation DESC
        LIMIT 5
    """, {"co": company}, as_dict=True)

    return {
        "revenue":    revenue,
        "expenses":   expenses,
        "net_profit": revenue - expenses,
        "cash":       cash,
        "overdue_ar": overdue_ar,
        "recent":     recent,
    }


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_pending_approvals(company):
    """List journal entries pending submission that the user can approve."""
    return frappe.db.get_all(
        "Journal Entry",
        filters={"company": company, "docstatus": 0},
        fields=["name", "posting_date", "voucher_type", "user_remark", "total_debit"],
        order_by="posting_date desc",
        limit=20,
    )


@frappe.whitelist()
def approve_voucher(docname):
    """Submit a Journal Entry from the mobile dashboard."""
    doc = frappe.get_doc("Journal Entry", docname)
    if doc.docstatus != 0:
        frappe.throw(_("Voucher is not in draft state."))
    doc.submit()
    frappe.db.commit()
    return doc.name

	let all_companies = [];

	function fmt_num(n) {
		if (Math.abs(n) >= 10000000) return (n / 10000000).toFixed(2) + " Cr";
		if (Math.abs(n) >= 100000)  return (n / 100000).toFixed(2) + " L";
		return frappe.format(n, {fieldtype: "Currency"});
	}

	// ─── Load companies ───────────────────────────────────────────
	function load_companies() {
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_multi_company.tn_multi_company.get_companies",
			callback(r) {
				all_companies = r.message || [];
				render_grid();
				populate_company_selects();
			},
		});
	}

	function populate_company_selects() {
		["#tn-ic-co", "#tn-ic-counter", "#tn-ic-from-co", "#tn-ic-to-co"].forEach(sel => {
			const $s = $body.find(sel);
			$s.empty().append('<option value="">— Select —</option>');
			all_companies.forEach(c => $s.append(`<option value="${c.name}">${c.name}</option>`));
		});
		$body.find("#tn-mc-from").val(frappe.datetime.year_start());
		$body.find("#tn-mc-to").val(frappe.datetime.get_today());
		$body.find("#tn-ic-from").val(frappe.datetime.year_start());
		$body.find("#tn-ic-to").val(frappe.datetime.get_today());
		$body.find("#tn-ic-date").val(frappe.datetime.get_today());
	}

	function render_grid() {
		const $grid = $body.find("#tn-co-grid");
		$grid.empty();
		all_companies.forEach(co => {
			const $card = $(`
				<div class="tn-co-card" data-co="${co.name}">
					<div class="tn-co-name">${co.name}</div>
					<div class="tn-kpi-row" id="tn-kpi-${co.name.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, "_")}">
						<div style="color:#aaa;font-size:12px;">Loading…</div>
					</div>
				</div>`);
			$grid.append($card);
			load_kpis(co.name);
		});
	}

	function load_kpis(company) {
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_multi_company.tn_multi_company.get_company_kpis",
			args: { company },
			callback(r) {
				const d = r.message || {};
				const id = "#tn-kpi-" + company.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, "_");
				const profit_cls = d.net_profit >= 0 ? "pos" : "neg";
				$body.find(id).html(`
					<div class="tn-kpi"><div class="tn-kpi-val">${fmt_num(d.revenue)}</div><div class="tn-kpi-lbl">Revenue</div></div>
					<div class="tn-kpi"><div class="tn-kpi-val ${profit_cls}">${fmt_num(d.net_profit)}</div><div class="tn-kpi-lbl">Net Profit</div></div>
					<div class="tn-kpi"><div class="tn-kpi-val">${fmt_num(d.cash)}</div><div class="tn-kpi-lbl">Cash</div></div>
					<div class="tn-kpi"><div class="tn-kpi-val">${fmt_num(d.receivables)}</div><div class="tn-kpi-lbl">AR</div></div>
				`);
			},
		});
	}

	// Tab switching
	$body.on("click", ".tn-mc-tab", function () {
		$body.find(".tn-mc-tab").removeClass("active");
		$body.find(".tn-mc-panel").removeClass("active");
		$(this).addClass("active");
		$body.find(`#tn-mc-${$(this).data("panel")}`).addClass("active");
	});

	// Refresh
	$body.on("click", "#tn-mc-refresh", () => render_grid());

	// Drill-down click on card → open company in new tab
	$body.on("click", ".tn-co-card", function () {
		const co = $(this).data("co");
		window.open(`/app/company/${encodeURIComponent(co)}`, "_blank");
	});

	// ─── Consolidated P&L ─────────────────────────────────────────
	$body.on("click", "#tn-mc-consol-btn", () => {
		const from_date = $body.find("#tn-mc-from").val();
		const to_date   = $body.find("#tn-mc-to").val();
		if (!from_date || !to_date) return frappe.msgprint("Select date range.");
		const companies = all_companies.map(c => c.name);
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_multi_company.tn_multi_company.get_consolidated_pl",
			args: { companies: JSON.stringify(companies), from_date, to_date },
			callback(r) {
				const rows = r.message || [];
				let html = `<table class="tn-consol-table">
					<thead><tr>
						<th>Company</th><th>Revenue</th><th>Expenses</th>
						<th>Net Profit</th><th>Margin %</th><th>Cash</th><th>AR</th><th>AP</th>
					</tr></thead><tbody>`;
				rows.forEach(row => {
					const pc = row.net_profit >= 0 ? "pos" : "neg";
					html += `<tr>
						<td>${row.company}</td>
						<td>${fmt_num(row.revenue)}</td>
						<td>${fmt_num(row.expenses)}</td>
						<td class="${pc}">${fmt_num(row.net_profit)}</td>
						<td class="${pc}">${row.margin_pct}%</td>
						<td>${fmt_num(row.cash)}</td>
						<td>${fmt_num(row.receivables)}</td>
						<td>${fmt_num(row.payables)}</td>
					</tr>`;
				});
				html += "</tbody></table>";
				$body.find("#tn-consol-result").html(html);
			},
		});
	});

	// ─── Inter-company transactions ───────────────────────────────
	$body.on("click", "#tn-ic-fetch", () => {
		const company     = $body.find("#tn-ic-co").val();
		const counterparty = $body.find("#tn-ic-counter").val();
		const from_date   = $body.find("#tn-ic-from").val();
		const to_date     = $body.find("#tn-ic-to").val();
		if (!company) return frappe.msgprint("Select a company.");
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_multi_company.tn_multi_company.get_intercompany_transactions",
			args: { company, counterparty, from_date, to_date },
			callback(r) {
				const rows = r.message || [];
				const $list = $body.find("#tn-ic-list");
				if (!rows.length) {
					$list.html('<div class="text-muted">No inter-company transactions found.</div>');
					return;
				}
				let html = `<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;">
					<thead><tr style="background:#f0f4f8;">
						<th style="padding:5px 8px;">Voucher</th><th>Type</th><th>Date</th>
						<th>Account</th><th>Debit</th><th>Credit</th>
					</tr></thead><tbody>`;
				rows.forEach(r => {
					html += `<tr style="border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0;">
						<td style="padding:4px 8px;"><a href="/app/${r.voucher_type.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g,"-")}/${r.voucher_no}" target="_blank">${r.voucher_no}</a></td>
						<td>${r.voucher_type}</td>
						<td>${r.posting_date}</td>
						<td>${r.account}</td>
						<td>${r.debit ? fmt_num(r.debit) : ""}</td>
						<td>${r.credit ? fmt_num(r.credit) : ""}</td>
					</tr>`;
				});
				html += "</tbody></table>";
				$list.html(html);
			},
		});
	});

	// ─── Create inter-company JE ──────────────────────────────────
	$body.on("click", "#tn-ic-create", () => {
		const from_company = $body.find("#tn-ic-from-co").val();
		const to_company   = $body.find("#tn-ic-to-co").val();
		const from_account = $body.find("#tn-ic-from-acct").val().trim();
		const to_account   = $body.find("#tn-ic-to-acct").val().trim();
		const amount       = parseFloat($body.find("#tn-ic-amount").val()) || 0;
		const posting_date = $body.find("#tn-ic-date").val();
		const narration    = $body.find("#tn-ic-narr").val().trim();

		if (!from_company || !to_company) return frappe.msgprint("Select both companies.");
		if (from_company === to_company) return frappe.msgprint("Companies must be different.");
		if (!from_account || !to_account) return frappe.msgprint("Fill both account names.");
		if (amount <= 0) return frappe.msgprint("Enter a positive amount.");

		frappe.confirm(`Create inter-company JEs?\n${from_company} ↔ ${to_company}\nAmount: ${frappe.format(amount, {fieldtype:"Currency"})}`, () => {
			frappe.call({
				method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_multi_company.tn_multi_company.create_intercompany_je",
				args: { from_company, to_company, amount, from_account, to_account, posting_date, narration },
				callback(r) {
					const d = r.message || {};
					$body.find("#tn-ic-result").html(
						`<span style="color:var(--green-500);">✓ Created: <a href="/app/journal-entry/${d.je_from}" target="_blank">${d.je_from}</a> &amp; <a href="/app/journal-entry/${d.je_to}" target="_blank">${d.je_to}</a></span>`
					);
				},
			});
		});
	});

	// Init
	load_companies();
};

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/page/tn_multi_company/tn_multi_company.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt, getdate, today, add_days


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_companies():
    return frappe.db.get_all(
        "Company",
        fields=["name", "abbr", "default_currency", "country"],
        order_by="name",
    )


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_company_kpis(company, as_of_date=None):
    """Return key financial KPIs for a company."""
    as_of_date = as_of_date or today()

    # Revenue (current FY)
    fy_start = frappe.db.get_value("Fiscal Year",
        {"year_start_date": ["<=", as_of_date], "year_end_date": [">=", as_of_date]},
        "year_start_date")

    revenue = flt(frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT SUM(credit - debit) FROM `tabGL Entry`
        WHERE company=%(co)s AND account IN (
            SELECT name FROM `tabAccount` WHERE root_type='Income' AND company=%(co)s
        ) AND posting_date BETWEEN %(start)s AND %(end)s AND is_cancelled=0
    """, {"co": company, "start": fy_start or "2000-01-01", "end": as_of_date})[0][0] or 0)

    expenses = flt(frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT SUM(debit - credit) FROM `tabGL Entry`
        WHERE company=%(co)s AND account IN (
            SELECT name FROM `tabAccount` WHERE root_type='Expense' AND company=%(co)s
        ) AND posting_date BETWEEN %(start)s AND %(end)s AND is_cancelled=0
    """, {"co": company, "start": fy_start or "2000-01-01", "end": as_of_date})[0][0] or 0)

    # Cash & Bank balance
    cash = flt(frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT SUM(debit - credit) FROM `tabGL Entry`
        WHERE company=%(co)s AND account IN (
            SELECT name FROM `tabAccount`
            WHERE root_type='Asset' AND account_type IN ('Cash','Bank') AND company=%(co)s
        ) AND posting_date <= %(end)s AND is_cancelled=0
    """, {"co": company, "end": as_of_date})[0][0] or 0)

    # Receivables
    receivables = flt(frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT SUM(debit - credit) FROM `tabGL Entry`
        WHERE company=%(co)s AND account IN (
            SELECT name FROM `tabAccount`
            WHERE root_type='Asset' AND account_type='Receivable' AND company=%(co)s
        ) AND posting_date <= %(end)s AND is_cancelled=0
    """, {"co": company, "end": as_of_date})[0][0] or 0)

    # Payables
    payables = flt(frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT SUM(credit - debit) FROM `tabGL Entry`
        WHERE company=%(co)s AND account IN (
            SELECT name FROM `tabAccount`
            WHERE root_type='Liability' AND account_type='Payable' AND company=%(co)s
        ) AND posting_date <= %(end)s AND is_cancelled=0
    """, {"co": company, "end": as_of_date})[0][0] or 0)

    return {
        "revenue": revenue,
        "expenses": expenses,
        "net_profit": revenue - expenses,
        "cash": cash,
        "receivables": receivables,
        "payables": payables,
        "margin_pct": round((revenue - expenses) / revenue * 100, 1) if revenue else 0,
    }


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_consolidated_pl(companies, from_date, to_date):
    """Return consolidated P&L across multiple companies (JSON list of company names)."""
    import json
    cos = json.loads(companies) if isinstance(companies, str) else companies

    rows = []
    for co in cos:
        kpi = get_company_kpis(co, to_date)
        kpi["company"] = co
        rows.append(kpi)

    # Totals row
    totals = {"company": "TOTAL", "revenue": 0, "expenses": 0, "net_profit": 0,
              "cash": 0, "receivables": 0, "payables": 0}
    for r in rows:
        for k in ("revenue", "expenses", "net_profit", "cash", "receivables", "payables"):
            totals[k] += r[k]
    if totals["revenue"]:
        totals["margin_pct"] = round(totals["net_profit"] / totals["revenue"] * 100, 1)
    else:
        totals["margin_pct"] = 0
    rows.append(totals)
    return rows


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_intercompany_transactions(company, counterparty=None, from_date=None, to_date=None):
    """Return inter-company GL entries between two companies."""
    filters = {"company": company, "is_cancelled": 0}
    if from_date:
        filters["posting_date"] = ["between", [from_date, to_date or today()]]

    # Look for accounts that reference the counterparty in their name (common ERPNext pattern)
    cond = ""
    params = {"company": company, "is_cancelled": 0,
              "from_date": from_date or "2000-01-01", "to_date": to_date or today()}
    if counterparty:
        cond = "AND g.against_voucher_type IS NOT NULL AND g.party = %(counterparty)s"
        params["counterparty"] = counterparty

    rows = frappe.db.sql(f"""
        SELECT g.voucher_no, g.voucher_type, g.posting_date,
               g.account, g.party, g.debit, g.credit, g.remarks
        FROM `tabGL Entry` g
        WHERE g.company=%(company)s AND g.is_cancelled=0
            AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
            AND g.party_type='Company'
            {cond}
        ORDER BY g.posting_date DESC
        LIMIT 200
    """, params, as_dict=True)
    return rows


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_intercompany_je(
    from_company, to_company, amount, from_account, to_account,
    posting_date=None, narration=""
):
    """Create matching Journal Entries in both companies for an inter-company transfer."""
    posting_date = posting_date or today()
    amount = flt(amount)
    if amount <= 0:
        frappe.throw("Amount must be positive.")

    # Validate accounts belong to respective companies
    for acct, co in [(from_account, from_company), (to_account, to_company)]:
        if not frappe.db.exists("Account", {"name": acct, "company": co}):
            frappe.throw(f"Account {acct} does not belong to {co}.")

    def _make_je(company, dr_acct, cr_acct, ref_company):
        je = frappe.new_doc("Journal Entry")
        je.company      = company
        je.posting_date = posting_date
        je.voucher_type = "Inter Company Journal Entry"
        je.user_remark  = narration or f"Inter-company with {ref_company}"
        je.inter_company_journal_entry_reference = ""  # will be patched after both are saved
        je.append("accounts", {
            "account": dr_acct,
            "debit_in_account_currency": amount,
            "credit_in_account_currency": 0,
            "party_type": "Company",
            "party": ref_company,
        })
        je.append("accounts", {
            "account": cr_acct,
            "debit_in_account_currency": 0,
            "credit_in_account_currency": amount,
        })
        je.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        je.submit()
        return je.name

    # From-company: debit receivable/inter-company account, credit from_account
    je1 = _make_je(from_company, from_account, from_account, to_company)
    je2 = _make_je(to_company,   to_account,   to_account,   from_company)

    # Cross-link
    frappe.db.set_value("Journal Entry", je1, "inter_company_journal_entry_reference", je2)
    frappe.db.set_value("Journal Entry", je2, "inter_company_journal_entry_reference", je1)
    frappe.db.commit()

    return {"je_from": je1, "je_to": je2}

		const $dots = $body.find("#tn-ob-step-dots");
		$dots.empty();
		for (let i = 1; i <= TOTAL_STEPS; i++) {
			const cls = i < current_step ? "done" : i === current_step ? "active" : "";
			$dots.append(`<div class="tn-ob-step-dot ${cls}" data-step="${i}">${i < current_step ? "✓" : i}</div>`);
		}
		$body.find("#tn-ob-back").toggle(current_step > 1);
		$body.find("#tn-ob-next").text(current_step === TOTAL_STEPS ? "Finish" : "Next →");
		$body.find("#tn-ob-skip").toggle(current_step < TOTAL_STEPS);
	}

	}

		`);
		$body.on("click.step4", ".tn-ob-choice", function () {
			$body.find(".tn-ob-choice").removeClass("selected");
			$(this).addClass("selected");
			state.opening_balance_method = $(this).data("val");
			if (state.opening_balance_method === "tally_xml") {
				$body.find("#ob-bal-action").html(
					`<a class="btn btn-default btn-sm" href="/app/tn-tally-import" target="_blank">Go to Tally Import →</a>`);
			} else {
				$body.find("#ob-bal-action").html(
					`<a class="btn btn-default btn-sm" href="/app/journal-entry/new" target="_blank">Create Opening Entry →</a>`);
			}
		});
	}

	function render_step5() {
		$body.off("click.step4");
		$body.find("#tn-ob-card").html(`
			<h4>${STEPS[4].title}</h4>
			<p class="subtitle">${STEPS[4].subtitle}</p>
			<ol style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;padding-left:18px;color:#555;">
				<li>Press <kbd>F6</kbd> anywhere in TallyNext — the Receipt voucher opens.</li>
				<li>Enter the ledger (e.g. <i>Cash</i>), amount, and date.</li>
				<li>Press <kbd>Ctrl+A</kbd> to submit.</li>
				<li>Go to <b>Day Book</b> (F-key shortcut or menu) — your voucher appears instantly.</li>
			</ol>
			<div style="margin-top:14px;display:flex;gap:8px;">
				<a class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" href="/app/tn-voucher?voucher_type=Receipt Entry" target="_blank">Try Receipt Entry (F6) →</a>
				<a class="btn btn-default btn-sm" href="/app/tn-day-book" target="_blank">Open Day Book →</a>
			</div>
		`);
	}

	function render_step6() {
		$body.find("#tn-ob-card").html(`
			<h4>${STEPS[5].title}</h4>
			<p class="subtitle">${STEPS[5].subtitle}</p>
			<div style="font-size:13px;color:#555;margin-bottom:14px;">
				TallyNext will auto-populate your compliance calendar based on the options you select.
			</div>
			<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px;">
				${[
					["gst",     "GST (GSTR-1, 3B, Annual)"],
					["tds",     "TDS / TCS Returns"],
					["pt",      "Professional Tax"],
					["pf_esi",  "PF & ESI"],
					["advance_tax", "Advance Tax"],
					["roc",     "ROC / MCA Filings"],
					["income_tax", "Income Tax Returns"],
					["msme",    "MSME Form 1"],
				].map(([val, lbl]) => `
					<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;cursor:pointer;padding:8px;border:1px solid #e8e8e8;border-radius:6px;">
						<input type="checkbox" value="${val}" ${(state.compliances || []).includes(val) ? "checked" : ""}>
						<span>${lbl}</span>
					</label>`).join("")}
			</div>
		`);
	}

	function save_step6() {
		state.compliances = [];
		$body.find("input[type=checkbox]:checked").each(function () {
			state.compliances.push($(this).val());
		});
	}

	function render_step7() {
		$body.find("#tn-ob-card").html(`
			<h4 style="color:var(--green-500);">🎉 ${STEPS[6].title}</h4>
			<p class="subtitle">${STEPS[6].subtitle}</p>
			<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-top:8px;">
				<a class="btn btn-primary" href="/app/tn-dashboard">Go to Dashboard →</a>
				<a class="btn btn-default" href="/app/tn-day-book">Open Day Book</a>
				<a class="btn btn-default" href="/app/tn-compliance-calendar">Compliance Calendar</a>
				<a class="btn btn-default" href="/app/tn-tally-import">Tally Import</a>
			</div>
			<p style="margin-top:16px;font-size:12px;color:#aaa;">You can return to this wizard anytime from the Help menu.</p>
		`);
		$body.find("#tn-ob-next").hide();
		$body.find("#tn-ob-skip").hide();
	}

	// ─── Navigation ───────────────────────────────────────────────
	$body.on("click", "#tn-ob-next", () => {
		const step_def = STEPS[current_step - 1];
		if (step_def.next) {
			if (step_def.next() === false) return;
		}
		// Save special steps
		if (current_step === 6) save_step6();

		// Persist to server
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_onboarding.tn_onboarding.save_progress",
			args: { step: current_step, data: JSON.stringify(state) },
		});

		if (current_step === TOTAL_STEPS) {
			frappe.call({
				method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_onboarding.tn_onboarding.complete_onboarding",
				args: { company: state.company_name || "" },
			});
			return;
		}
		go_to(current_step + 1);
	});

	$body.on("click", "#tn-ob-back", () => {
		if (current_step > 1) go_to(current_step - 1);
	});

	$body.on("click", "#tn-ob-skip", () => {
		if (current_step < TOTAL_STEPS) go_to(current_step + 1);
	});

	// ─── Init: restore progress ───────────────────────────────────
	frappe.call({
		method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_onboarding.tn_onboarding.get_progress",
		callback(r) {
			const saved = r.message || {};
			Object.assign(state, saved[Object.keys(saved).pop()] || {});
			go_to(1);
		},
	});
};

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/page/tn_onboarding/tn_onboarding.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import today


@frappe.whitelist()
def save_progress(step, data):
    """Persist onboarding progress so the user can resume later."""
    import json
    key = f"tn_onboarding_{frappe.session.user}"
    existing = frappe.cache().get_value(key) or {}
    if isinstance(existing, str):
        try:
            existing = json.loads(existing)
        except Exception:
            existing = {}
    existing[str(step)] = json.loads(data) if isinstance(data, str) else data
    frappe.cache().set_value(key, existing, expires_in_sec=86400 * 30)
    return {"ok": True}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_progress():
    """Retrieve saved onboarding progress."""
    key = f"tn_onboarding_{frappe.session.user}"
    val = frappe.cache().get_value(key) or {}
    return val


@frappe.whitelist()
def complete_onboarding(company):
    """Mark onboarding as complete."""
    frappe.cache().delete_value(f"tn_onboarding_{frappe.session.user}")
    frappe.db.set_value("Company", company, "tn_onboarding_complete", 1) if frappe.db.has_column("Company", "tn_onboarding_complete") else None
    frappe.db.commit()
    return {"done": True}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_coa_templates():
    """Return available Chart of Accounts templates."""
    templates = frappe.db.get_all(
        "Chart of Accounts Importer",
        fields=["name"],
        limit=20,
    ) if frappe.db.exists("DocType", "Chart of Accounts Importer") else []
    builtin = ["Manufacturing", "Trading", "Services", "CA Firm"]
    return {"builtin": builtin, "custom": [t.name for t in templates]}


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_company_setup(company_name, abbr, country, currency, gstin="", tan="", financial_year_start="04"):
    """Step 2: create or update the company with TallyNext config."""
    if not frappe.db.exists("Company", company_name):
        doc = frappe.new_doc("Company")
        doc.company_name     = company_name
        doc.abbr             = abbr
        doc.country          = country
        doc.default_currency = currency
        doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    else:
        doc = frappe.get_doc("Company", company_name)

    frappe.db.commit()
    return doc.name


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_fiscal_years(company):
    return frappe.db.get_all("Fiscal Year",
        fields=["name", "year_start_date", "year_end_date"],
        order_by="year_start_date desc", limit=5)

	let current_job = null;
	let poll_timer  = null;

	// Populate company selects
	frappe.call({
		method: "frappe.client.get_list",
		args: { doctype: "Company", fields: ["name"], limit_page_length: 50 },
		callback(r) {
			const cos = r.message || [];
			["#tn-ti-company", "#tn-exp-company"].forEach(sel => {
				const $s = $body.find(sel);
				cos.forEach(c => $s.append(`<option value="${c.name}">${c.name}</option>`));
			});
			$body.find("#tn-exp-from").val(frappe.datetime.year_start());
			$body.find("#tn-exp-to").val(frappe.datetime.get_today());
		},
	});

	// Tab switching
	$body.on("click", ".tn-ti-tab", function () {
		$body.find(".tn-ti-tab").removeClass("active");
		$body.find(".tn-ti-panel").removeClass("active");
		$(this).addClass("active");
		$body.find(`#tn-ti-${$(this).data("panel")}`).addClass("active");
		if ($(this).data("panel") === "history") load_history();
	});

	// Create job
	$body.on("click", "#tn-ti-create-job", () => {
		const company = $body.find("#tn-ti-company").val();
		const name    = $body.find("#tn-ti-name").val().trim();
		if (!company || !name) return frappe.msgprint("Fill Company and Import Name.");
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_tally_import.tn_tally_import.create_job",
			args: { company, import_name: name },
			callback(r) {
				current_job = r.message;
				$body.find("#tn-ti-wizard").show();
				setup_file_upload(company);
			},
		});
	});

	function setup_file_upload(company) {
		const $area = $body.find("#tn-file-upload-area");
		$area.empty();
		const upload = new frappe.ui.FileUploader({
			wrapper: $area[0],
			method: "frappe.client.attach_file",
			doctype: "TN Tally Import Job",
			docname: current_job,
			fieldname: "tally_xml_file",
			restrictions: { allowed_file_types: [".xml", ".txt"] },
			on_success(file) {
				frappe.db.set_value("TN Tally Import Job", current_job, "tally_xml_file", file.file_url);
				$body.find("#tn-ti-parse").prop("disabled", false);
			},
		});
	}

	// Parse XML
	$body.on("click", "#tn-ti-parse", () => {
		$body.find("#tn-parse-status").text("Parsing…");
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_tally_import.tn_tally_import.parse_xml",
			args: { job_name: current_job },
			callback(r) {
				const d = r.message || {};
				$body.find("#tn-parse-status").text(
					`Parsed: ${d.ledgers || 0} ledgers, ${d.vouchers || 0} vouchers`);
				$body.find("#tn-step-2").show();
				load_mappings();
			},
		});
	});

	function load_mappings() {
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_tally_import.tn_tally_import.get_mappings",
			args: { job_name: current_job },
			callback(r) {
				const $body_el = $body.find("#tn-map-body");
				$body_el.empty();
				(r.message || []).forEach(m => {
					const conf    = parseFloat(m.confidence) || 0;
					const confCls = conf >= 80 ? "tn-conf-high" : conf >= 50 ? "tn-conf-med" : "tn-conf-low";
					$body_el.append(`
						<tr>
							<td>${m.tally_ledger_name}</td>
							<td style="color:#888;">${m.tally_group || ""}</td>
							<td><input class="form-control input-xs tn-map-acct" style="width:220px;"
								data-name="${m.name}" value="${m.erpnext_account || ""}"
								placeholder="ERPNext Account"></td>
							<td class="${confCls}">${conf}%</td>
						</tr>`);
				});
			},
		});
	}

	// Save mapping changes on blur
	$body.on("change", ".tn-map-acct", function () {
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_tally_import.tn_tally_import.update_mapping",
			args: {
				mapping_name: $(this).data("name"),
				erpnext_account: $(this).val(),
			},
		});
	});

	// Validate
	$body.on("click", "#tn-ti-validate", () => {
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_tally_import.tn_tally_import.validate_import",
			args: { job_name: current_job },
			callback(r) {
				const errors = r.message || [];
				$body.find("#tn-step-3").show();
				const $res = $body.find("#tn-validation-results");
				if (!errors.length) {
					$res.html(`<div style="color:var(--green-500);font-weight:700;">✓ Validation passed — ready to import.</div>`);
					$body.find("#tn-ti-import-btn").prop("disabled", false);
				} else {
					$res.html(errors.map(e =>
						`<div class="tn-error-item"><b>${e.type}:</b> ${e.detail}</div>`
					).join(""));
					$body.find("#tn-ti-import-btn").prop("disabled", true);
				}
			},
		});
	});

	// Start import
	$body.on("click", "#tn-ti-import-btn", () => {
		frappe.confirm("Start importing? This may take several minutes for large data sets.", () => {
			frappe.call({
				method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_tally_import.tn_tally_import.start_import",
				args: { job_name: current_job },
				callback() {
					$body.find("#tn-step-4").show();
					poll_progress();
				},
			});
		});
	});

	function poll_progress() {
		poll_timer = setInterval(() => {
			frappe.call({
				method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_tally_import.tn_tally_import.get_import_progress",
				args: { job_name: current_job },
				callback(r) {
					const d = r.message || {};
					$body.find("#tn-progress-bar").css("width", `${d.progress_pct || 0}%`);
					$body.find("#tn-import-progress-label").text(
						`${d.status} — ${d.progress_pct || 0}% — ${d.error_summary || ""}`);
					if (["Complete", "Failed"].includes(d.status)) {
						clearInterval(poll_timer);
						$body.find("#tn-import-log").text(d.error_summary || "");
					}
				},
			});
		}, 3000);
	}

	// Export
	$body.on("click", "#tn-exp-btn", () => {
		const company   = $body.find("#tn-exp-company").val();
		const from_date = $body.find("#tn-exp-from").val();
		const to_date   = $body.find("#tn-exp-to").val();
		if (!company || !from_date || !to_date) return frappe.msgprint("Fill all fields.");
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_tally_import.tn_tally_import.export_tally_xml",
			args: { company, from_date, to_date },
			callback(r) {
				if (r.message) {
					$body.find("#tn-exp-result").html(
						`<a href="${r.message}" download>Download Tally XML</a>`);
				}
			},
		});
	});

	function load_history() {
		const company = $body.find("#tn-ti-company").val();
		if (!company) return;
		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_tally_import.tn_tally_import.get_jobs",
			args: { company },
			callback(r) {
				const $list = $body.find("#tn-history-list");
				$list.empty();
				(r.message || []).forEach(j => {
					$list.append(`
						<div style="padding:8px 4px;border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0;display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center;">
							<span style="font-weight:600;min-width:160px;">${j.import_name}</span>
							<span style="color:#888;min-width:80px;">${j.status}</span>
							<span style="font-size:11px;color:#aaa;">${j.total_vouchers || 0} vouchers</span>
							<span style="font-size:11px;color:#888;flex:1;">${j.error_summary || ""}</span>
							<a href="/app/tn-tally-import-job/${j.name}" target="_blank" style="font-size:11px;">View</a>
						</div>`);
				});
			},
		});
	}
};

import frappe
from frappe import _


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_jobs(company):
    return frappe.db.get_all(
        "TN Tally Import Job",
        filters={"company": company},
        fields=["name", "import_name", "status", "total_ledgers",
                "total_vouchers", "progress_pct", "error_summary"],
        order_by="creation desc",
        limit=20,
    )


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_job(company, import_name):
    doc = frappe.new_doc("TN Tally Import Job")
    doc.company     = company
    doc.import_name = import_name
    doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    frappe.db.commit()
    return doc.name


@frappe.whitelist()
def parse_xml(job_name):
    doc = frappe.get_doc("TN Tally Import Job", job_name)
    return doc.parse_xml()


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_mappings(job_name):
    return frappe.db.get_all(
        "TN Ledger Mapping",
        filters={"import_job": job_name},
        fields=["name", "tally_ledger_name", "tally_group",
                "tally_opening_balance", "erpnext_account",
                "create_new", "new_account_name", "mapped_by", "confidence"],
        order_by="confidence desc",
        limit=500,
    )


@frappe.whitelist()
def update_mapping(mapping_name, erpnext_account=None, create_new=0, new_account_name=None):
    frappe.db.set_value("TN Ledger Mapping", mapping_name, {
        "erpnext_account":  erpnext_account or "",
        "create_new":       create_new,
        "new_account_name": new_account_name or "",
        "mapped_by":        "Manual",
    })


@frappe.whitelist()
def validate_import(job_name):
    doc = frappe.get_doc("TN Tally Import Job", job_name)
    return doc.validate_import()


@frappe.whitelist()
def start_import(job_name):
    doc = frappe.get_doc("TN Tally Import Job", job_name)
    return doc.start_import()
