def get_import_progress(job_name):
    return frappe.db.get_value(
        "TN Tally Import Job", job_name,
        ["status", "progress_pct", "error_summary"], as_dict=True
    )


@frappe.whitelist()
def export_tally_xml(company, from_date, to_date, voucher_types=None):
    from tallynext.tallynext.tally_import.exporter import export_to_tally_xml
    import json
    types = json.loads(voucher_types) if voucher_types else None
    xml_content = export_to_tally_xml(company, from_date, to_date, types)
    # Save as a temp file and return URL
    fname = f"tally_export_{company}_{from_date}_{to_date}.xml"
    file_doc = frappe.get_doc({
        "doctype": "File",
        "file_name": fname,
        "content": xml_content,
        "is_private": 1,
    })
    file_doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    return file_doc.file_url

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/page/tn_voucher/tn_voucher.js
frappe.pages["tn-voucher"].on_page_load = function (wrapper) {
	frappe.ui.make_app_page({
		parent: wrapper,
		title: "Voucher Entry",
		single_column: true,
	});

	const page = wrapper.page;
	const $body = $(wrapper).find(".page-content");
	$body.html(tn_voucher_html());

	const state = {
		voucher_type: "Journal",
		company: "",
		companies: [],
		entries: [
			{ account: "", debit: "", credit: "", party_type: "", party: "", narration: "" },
			{ account: "", debit: "", credit: "", party_type: "", party: "", narration: "" },
		],
		session_count: 0,
	};

	// Load defaults
	frappe.call({
		method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_voucher.tn_voucher.get_voucher_defaults",
		callback(r) {
			if (!r.exc) {
				state.company = r.message.default_company;
				state.voucher_type = r.message.default_voucher_type || "Journal";
				state.companies = r.message.companies || [];
				render(state, $body);
				bind_events($body, state);
				// Focus date field
				$body.find("#tn-date").focus();
			}
		},
	});

	render(state, $body);
	bind_events($body, state);
};

function render(state, $body) {
	const companyOptions = state.companies
		.map((c) => `<option value="${c.name}" ${c.name === state.company ? "selected" : ""}>${c.name}</option>`)
		.join("");

	const voucherTypes = [
		{ key: "F4", type: "Contra" },
		{ key: "F5", type: "Payment" },
		{ key: "F6", type: "Receipt" },
		{ key: "F7", type: "Journal" },
		{ key: "F8", type: "Sales Invoice" },
		{ key: "F9", type: "Purchase Invoice" },
	];

	const typeButtons = voucherTypes
		.map(
			(v) =>
				`<button class="tn-type-btn btn btn-sm ${state.voucher_type === v.type ? "btn-primary" : "btn-default"}"
				data-type="${v.type}" title="${v.key}">
				<kbd>${v.key}</kbd> ${v.type}
			</button>`
		)
		.join(" ");

	const entriesHtml = state.entries
		.map(
			(e, i) => `
		<tr class="tn-entry-row" data-idx="${i}">
			<td><input class="form-control tn-account input-sm" data-field="account" data-idx="${i}"
				value="${e.account}" placeholder="Ledger…" autocomplete="off"></td>
			<td><input class="form-control tn-debit input-sm text-right" data-field="debit" data-idx="${i}"
				value="${e.debit}" placeholder="0.00"></td>
			<td><input class="form-control tn-credit input-sm text-right" data-field="credit" data-idx="${i}"
				value="${e.credit}" placeholder="0.00"></td>
			<td><input class="form-control tn-narration input-sm" data-field="narration" data-idx="${i}"
				value="${e.narration}" placeholder="Narration…"></td>
			<td>
				<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default tn-remove-row" data-idx="${i}" title="Remove row">✕</button>
			</td>
		</tr>`
		)
		.join("");

	// Totals
	const totalDr = state.entries.reduce((s, e) => s + (parseFloat(eval_expr(e.debit)) || 0), 0);
	const totalCr = state.entries.reduce((s, e) => s + (parseFloat(eval_expr(e.credit)) || 0), 0);
	const balanced = Math.abs(totalDr - totalCr) < 0.01;

	$body.find("#tn-voucher-inner").html(`
		<div class="tn-voucher-wrap" style="max-width:960px;margin:0 auto;">

			<!-- Voucher type bar -->
			<div class="tn-type-bar" style="margin-bottom:12px;">
				${typeButtons}
				<span class="text-muted" style="margin-left:8px;font-size:11px;">Press F4–F9 to switch type</span>
			</div>

			<!-- Header row -->
			<div class="form-inline" style="margin-bottom:10px;gap:8px;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;">
				<div class="form-group">
					<label class="tn-label">Date</label>
					<input id="tn-date" type="date" class="form-control input-sm"
						value="${frappe.datetime.get_today()}" style="width:140px;">
				</div>
				<div class="form-group">
					<label class="tn-label">Company</label>
					<select id="tn-company" class="form-control input-sm" style="width:200px;">${companyOptions}</select>
				</div>
				<div class="form-group" style="flex:1;min-width:200px;">
					<label class="tn-label">Narration</label>
					<input id="tn-narration" type="text" class="form-control input-sm"
						placeholder="Narration for all entries…" style="width:100%;">
				</div>
			</div>

			<!-- Entries table -->
			<table class="table table-bordered table-condensed tn-entries-table" style="margin-bottom:6px;">
				<thead>
					<tr>
						<th style="width:35%">Ledger</th>
						<th style="width:15%">Dr</th>
						<th style="width:15%">Cr</th>
						<th style="width:30%">Narration</th>
						<th style="width:5%"></th>
					</tr>
				</thead>
				<tbody id="tn-entries-body">
					${entriesHtml}
				</tbody>
				<tfoot>
					<tr>
						<td><button class="btn btn-xs btn-default" id="tn-add-row">+ Add Row (Alt+A)</button></td>
						<td class="text-right"><strong>${fmt_money(totalDr)}</strong></td>
						<td class="text-right"><strong>${fmt_money(totalCr)}</strong></td>
						<td colspan="2">
							<span class="${balanced ? "text-success" : "text-danger"}" style="font-size:12px;">
								${balanced ? "✓ Balanced" : `Diff: ${fmt_money(Math.abs(totalDr - totalCr))}`}
							</span>
						</td>
					</tr>
				</tfoot>
			</table>

			<!-- Submit bar -->
			<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin-top:8px;">
				<button id="tn-submit" class="btn btn-primary" ${balanced && totalDr > 0 ? "" : "disabled"}>
					Submit <kbd>Ctrl+A</kbd>
				</button>
				<button id="tn-clear" class="btn btn-default">Clear <kbd>Esc</kbd></button>
				<span class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px;">
					Session: <strong id="tn-session-count">${state.session_count}</strong> vouchers entered
				</span>
			</div>
		</div>
	`);
}

function bind_events($body, state) {
	// Voucher type buttons
	$body.on("click", ".tn-type-btn", function () {
		state.voucher_type = $(this).data("type");
		render(state, $body);
		$body.find("#tn-date").focus();
	});

	// Keyboard shortcuts F4–F9
	$(document).off("keydown.tn_voucher").on("keydown.tn_voucher", function (e) {
		if (!$body.is(":visible")) return;
		const map = { 115: "Contra", 116: "Payment", 117: "Receipt", 118: "Journal", 119: "Sales Invoice", 120: "Purchase Invoice" };
		if (map[e.which]) {
			e.preventDefault();
			state.voucher_type = map[e.which];
			render(state, $body);
			bind_events($body, state);
			$body.find("#tn-date").focus();
		}
		// Ctrl+A = Submit
		if (e.ctrlKey && e.key === "a") {
			e.preventDefault();
			$body.find("#tn-submit").trigger("click");
		}
		// Escape = Clear
		if (e.key === "Escape") {
			clear_form(state, $body);
		}
		// Alt+A = Add row
		if (e.altKey && e.key === "a") {
			e.preventDefault();
			add_row(state, $body);
		}
	});

	// Account autocomplete
	$body.on("input", ".tn-account", function () {
		const $input = $(this);
		const idx = parseInt($input.data("idx"));
		const query = $input.val();
		const company = $body.find("#tn-company").val() || state.company;

		frappe.call({
			method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_voucher.tn_voucher.get_accounts",
			args: { company, query },
			callback(r) {
				if (r.exc || !r.message) return;
				show_account_dropdown($input, r.message, function (selected) {
					state.entries[idx].account = selected;
					$input.val(selected);
					// Move to Dr field
					$input.closest("tr").find(".tn-debit").focus();
				});
			},
		});
	});

	// Amount expression evaluation on blur
	$body.on("blur", ".tn-debit, .tn-credit", function () {
		const $input = $(this);
		const idx = parseInt($input.data("idx"));
		const field = $input.data("field");
		const val = eval_expr($input.val());
		$input.val(val ? fmt_number(val) : "");
		state.entries[idx][field] = val ? String(val) : "";

		// Auto-balance: if last row and only dr/cr filled, fill the other side
		auto_balance(state, $body, idx, field);
		update_totals($body, state);
	});

	// Narration per row
	$body.on("input", ".tn-narration", function () {
		const idx = parseInt($(this).data("idx"));
		state.entries[idx].narration = $(this).val();
	});

	// Tab key — move through rows top-to-bottom, add row at end
	$body.on("keydown", ".tn-entries-table input", function (e) {
		if (e.key === "Tab" && !e.shiftKey) {
			const $inputs = $body.find(".tn-entries-table input:visible");
			const idx = $inputs.index(this);
			if (idx === $inputs.length - 1) {
				e.preventDefault();
				add_row(state, $body);
			}
		}
	});

	// Remove row
	$body.on("click", ".tn-remove-row", function () {
		const idx = parseInt($(this).data("idx"));
		if (state.entries.length > 2) {
			state.entries.splice(idx, 1);
			render(state, $body);
			bind_events($body, state);
		}
	});

	// Add row
	$body.on("click", "#tn-add-row", function () {
		add_row(state, $body);
	});

	// Submit
	$body.on("click", "#tn-submit", function () {
		submit_voucher(state, $body);
	});

	// Clear
	$body.on("click", "#tn-clear", function () {
		clear_form(state, $body);
	});
}

function add_row(state, $body) {
	state.entries.push({ account: "", debit: "", credit: "", party_type: "", party: "", narration: "" });
	render(state, $body);
	bind_events($body, state);
	// Focus last account input
	$body.find(".tn-account").last().focus();
}

function clear_form(state, $body) {
	state.entries = [
		{ account: "", debit: "", credit: "", party_type: "", party: "", narration: "" },
		{ account: "", debit: "", credit: "", party_type: "", party: "", narration: "" },
	];
	render(state, $body);
	bind_events($body, state);
	$body.find("#tn-date").focus();
}

function auto_balance(state, $body, changed_idx, changed_field) {
	// If exactly two rows and one side is filled, fill the other side of the other row
	if (state.entries.length !== 2) return;
	const other_idx = changed_idx === 0 ? 1 : 0;
	const other = state.entries[other_idx];
	const current = state.entries[changed_idx];

	const dr = parseFloat(current.debit) || 0;
	const cr = parseFloat(current.credit) || 0;
	const amount = dr || cr;

	if (amount > 0 && !other.debit && !other.credit) {
		// Fill opposite side on other row
		if (changed_field === "debit" || dr > 0) {
			state.entries[other_idx].credit = fmt_number(amount);
		} else {
			state.entries[other_idx].debit = fmt_number(amount);
		}
		render(state, $body);
		bind_events($body, state);
		// Focus narration of the changed row
		$body.find(`.tn-narration[data-idx="${changed_idx}"]`).focus();
	}
}

function update_totals($body, state) {
	const totalDr = state.entries.reduce((s, e) => s + (parseFloat(eval_expr(e.debit)) || 0), 0);
	const totalCr = state.entries.reduce((s, e) => s + (parseFloat(eval_expr(e.credit)) || 0), 0);
	const balanced = Math.abs(totalDr - totalCr) < 0.01;
	// Partial re-render of totals
	render(state, $body);
	bind_events($body, state);
}

function submit_voucher(state, $body) {
	// Validate
	const totalDr = state.entries.reduce((s, e) => s + (parseFloat(e.debit) || 0), 0);
	const totalCr = state.entries.reduce((s, e) => s + (parseFloat(e.credit) || 0), 0);

	if (Math.abs(totalDr - totalCr) > 0.01) {
		frappe.msgprint("Voucher is not balanced. Dr ≠ Cr.");
		return;
	}
	if (totalDr === 0) {
		frappe.msgprint("Please enter amounts.");
		return;
	}

	const empty = state.entries.filter((e) => e.account && (parseFloat(e.debit) > 0 || parseFloat(e.credit) > 0));
	if (empty.length < 2) {
		frappe.msgprint("At least 2 ledger entries required.");
		return;
	}

	const entries = state.entries.filter((e) => e.account).map((e) => ({
		account: e.account,
		debit: parseFloat(e.debit) || 0,
		credit: parseFloat(e.credit) || 0,
		narration: e.narration,
	}));

	frappe.call({
		method: "tallynext.tallynext.page.tn_voucher.tn_voucher.submit_voucher",
		freeze: true,
		freeze_message: "Submitting voucher…",
		args: {
			voucher_type: state.voucher_type,
			company: $body.find("#tn-company").val() || state.company,
			date: $body.find("#tn-date").val(),
			narration: $body.find("#tn-narration").val(),
			entries: JSON.stringify(entries),
		},
		callback(r) {
			if (r.exc) return;
			state.session_count += 1;
			frappe.show_alert({ message: `✓ ${r.message.name} submitted`, indicator: "green" });
			clear_form(state, $body);
		},
	});
}

// ── Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function eval_expr(val) {
	if (!val) return 0;
	val = String(val).replace(/,/g, "");
	// Allow simple arithmetic: 10000+5000, 50000-1000
	try {
		const result = Function(`"use strict"; return (${val.replace(/[^0-9+\-*/.() ]/g, "")})`)();
		return isNaN(result) ? 0 : Math.round(result * 100) / 100;
	} catch {
		return parseFloat(val) || 0;
	}
}

function fmt_number(n) {
	return parseFloat(n).toFixed(2);
}

function fmt_money(n) {
	return frappe.format(n, { fieldtype: "Currency" });
}

function show_account_dropdown($input, accounts, on_select) {
	$(".tn-dropdown").remove();
	if (!accounts.length) return;

	const $dd = $(`<ul class="tn-dropdown dropdown-menu" style="display:block;position:absolute;z-index:9999;max-height:200px;overflow-y:auto;min-width:280px;">`);
	accounts.slice(0, 15).forEach((a) => {
		$dd.append(
			`<li><a class="tn-dd-item" href="#" data-name="${a.name}">
				${a.account_name}
				<small class="text-muted">(${a.account_type || ""})</small>
			</a></li>`
		);
	});

	$input.after($dd);
	$dd.on("click", ".tn-dd-item", function (e) {
		e.preventDefault();
		on_select($(this).data("name"));
		$dd.remove();
	});

	// Close on outside click
	$(document).one("click.tn_dd", function () { $(".tn-dropdown").remove(); });

	// Keyboard nav in dropdown
	$input.on("keydown.tn_dd", function (e) {
		if (e.key === "ArrowDown") { $dd.find("a").first().focus(); e.preventDefault(); }
		if (e.key === "Escape") { $dd.remove(); $input.off("keydown.tn_dd"); }
	});
	$dd.on("keydown", "a", function (e) {
		if (e.key === "ArrowDown") { $(this).parent().next().find("a").focus(); e.preventDefault(); }
		if (e.key === "ArrowUp") { $(this).parent().prev().find("a").focus(); e.preventDefault(); }
		if (e.key === "Enter") { $(this).trigger("click"); e.preventDefault(); }
		if (e.key === "Escape") { $dd.remove(); $input.focus(); }
	});
}

function tn_voucher_html() {
	return `
	<style>
		.tn-label { font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; color: #6c757d; margin-bottom: 2px; display: block; }
		.tn-type-btn { margin-right: 4px; }
		.tn-type-btn kbd { font-size: 10px; }
		.tn-entries-table th { font-size: 12px; background: #f8f9fa; }
		.tn-entries-table td { padding: 4px 6px; vertical-align: middle; }
		.tn-entries-table input { border-radius: 2px; }
		.tn-dropdown { top: 100%; left: 0; }
	</style>
	<div id="tn-voucher-inner" style="padding: 16px 8px;"></div>
	`;
}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/page/tn_voucher/tn_voucher.py
import frappe


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_voucher_defaults():
	"""Return default values for new voucher entry."""
	settings = frappe.get_single("TN Settings")
	companies = frappe.get_all("Company", fields=["name", "default_currency", "abbr"], order_by="name")
	return {
		"default_company": settings.default_company or (companies[0].name if companies else ""),
		"default_voucher_type": settings.default_voucher_type or "Journal",
		"companies": companies,
	}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_accounts(company, account_type=None, query=None):
	"""Fuzzy search accounts/ledgers for autocomplete."""
	filters = {"company": company, "is_group": 0}
	if account_type:
		filters["account_type"] = account_type

	fields = ["name", "account_name", "account_type", "account_currency"]
	accounts = frappe.get_all("Account", filters=filters, fields=fields, order_by="account_name", limit=50)

	if query:
		q = query.lower()
		accounts = [a for a in accounts if q in a.account_name.lower() or q in a.name.lower()][:20]

	return accounts


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_parties(party_type, query=None):
	"""Fuzzy search customers or suppliers."""
	doctype = "Customer" if party_type == "Customer" else "Supplier"
	filters = {}
	if query:
		filters["name"] = ["like", f"%{query}%"]

	return frappe.get_all(doctype, filters=filters, fields=["name"], limit=20)


@frappe.whitelist()
def submit_voucher(voucher_type, company, date, entries, narration=None, cost_center=None, party_type=None, party=None):
	"""
	Create and submit the appropriate ERPNext document for the given voucher type.
	entries: list of {account, debit_in_account_currency, credit_in_account_currency, party_type, party}
	"""
	import json

	if isinstance(entries, str):
		entries = json.loads(entries)

	voucher_type = voucher_type or "Journal"

	if voucher_type in ("Journal", "Contra"):
		return _submit_journal_entry(company, date, entries, narration, cost_center)
	elif voucher_type in ("Payment", "Receipt"):
		return _submit_payment_entry(voucher_type, company, date, entries, narration, party_type, party)
	elif voucher_type == "Sales Invoice":
		frappe.throw("Sales Invoice requires full invoice form. Use New Sales Invoice.")
	elif voucher_type == "Purchase Invoice":
		frappe.throw("Purchase Invoice requires full invoice form. Use New Purchase Invoice.")
	else:
		frappe.throw(f"Unknown voucher type: {voucher_type}")


def _submit_journal_entry(company, date, entries, narration, cost_center):
	je = frappe.new_doc("Journal Entry")
	je.company = company
	je.posting_date = date
	je.user_remark = narration or ""

	for e in entries:
		row = je.append("accounts", {
			"account": e.get("account"),
			"debit_in_account_currency": float(e.get("debit", 0)),
			"credit_in_account_currency": float(e.get("credit", 0)),
			"cost_center": e.get("cost_center") or cost_center,
			"party_type": e.get("party_type") or "",
			"party": e.get("party") or "",
			"user_remark": e.get("narration") or narration or "",
		})

	je.insert()
	je.submit()
	return {"doctype": "Journal Entry", "name": je.name, "status": "Submitted"}


def _submit_payment_entry(voucher_type, company, date, entries, narration, party_type, party):
	pe = frappe.new_doc("Payment Entry")
	pe.company = company
	pe.posting_date = date
	pe.payment_type = "Pay" if voucher_type == "Payment" else "Receive"
	pe.party_type = party_type or ("Supplier" if voucher_type == "Payment" else "Customer")
	pe.party = party or ""
	pe.remarks = narration or ""

	# Determine paid from / paid to accounts from entries
	for e in entries:
		if float(e.get("credit", 0)) > 0:
			pe.paid_from = e.get("account")
			pe.paid_amount = float(e.get("credit", 0))
		elif float(e.get("debit", 0)) > 0:
			pe.paid_to = e.get("account")
			pe.received_amount = float(e.get("debit", 0))

	pe.insert()
	pe.submit()
	return {"doctype": "Payment Entry", "name": pe.name, "status": "Submitted"}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_alteration_log/tn_alteration_log.py
import frappe
from frappe import _


def execute(filters=None):
	filters = filters or {}
	columns = get_columns()
	data = get_data(filters)
	return columns, data


def get_columns():
	return [
		{"label": _("Changed On"),    "fieldname": "changed_on",    "fieldtype": "Datetime",      "width": 150},
		{"label": _("Voucher Type"),  "fieldname": "voucher_type",  "fieldtype": "Data",          "width": 150},
		{"label": _("Voucher No"),    "fieldname": "voucher_no",    "fieldtype": "Dynamic Link",  "options": "voucher_type", "width": 160},
		{"label": _("Posting Date"),  "fieldname": "posting_date",  "fieldtype": "Date",          "width": 100},
		{"label": _("Action"),        "fieldname": "action",        "fieldtype": "Data",          "width": 100},
		{"label": _("Changed By"),    "fieldname": "changed_by",    "fieldtype": "Link",          "options": "User", "width": 150},
		{"label": _("Field Changes"), "fieldname": "field_changes", "fieldtype": "Data",          "width": 300},
	]


def get_data(filters):
	company    = filters.get("company")
	from_date  = filters.get("from_date")
	to_date    = filters.get("to_date")
	changed_by = filters.get("changed_by")
	action     = filters.get("action")

	cond = "1=1"
	params = {}

	if company:
		cond += " AND company = %(company)s"
		params["company"] = company
	if from_date:
		cond += " AND DATE(changed_on) >= %(from_date)s"
		params["from_date"] = from_date
	if to_date:
		cond += " AND DATE(changed_on) <= %(to_date)s"
		params["to_date"] = to_date
	if changed_by:
		cond += " AND changed_by = %(changed_by)s"
		params["changed_by"] = changed_by
	if action:
		cond += " AND action = %(action)s"
		params["action"] = action

	return frappe.db.sql(f"""
		SELECT
			changed_on, voucher_type, voucher_no, posting_date,
			action, changed_by, field_changes
		FROM `tabTN Audit Log`
		WHERE {cond}
		ORDER BY changed_on DESC
		LIMIT 1000
	""", params, as_dict=True)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_budget_vs_actual/tn_budget_vs_actual.py
"""
TN Budget vs Actual — compare budgeted vs actual spend per account/cost centre.
Filters: company, fiscal_year, budget (optional), cost_center (optional).
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt


def execute(filters=None):
    filters = filters or {}
    columns = get_columns()
    data    = get_data(filters)
    return columns, data


def get_columns():
    return [
        {"label": _("Budget"),         "fieldname": "budget_name",   "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 160},
        {"label": _("Account"),        "fieldname": "account",        "fieldtype": "Link",     "options": "Account", "width": 220},
        {"label": _("Cost Centre"),    "fieldname": "cost_center",    "fieldtype": "Link",     "options": "Cost Center", "width": 160},
        {"label": _("Budget Amount"),  "fieldname": "budget_amount",  "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
        {"label": _("Actual Amount"),  "fieldname": "actual_amount",  "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
        {"label": _("Variance"),       "fieldname": "variance",       "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 120},
        {"label": _("Variance %"),     "fieldname": "variance_pct",   "fieldtype": "Percent",  "width": 100},
        {"label": _("Utilisation %"),  "fieldname": "utilisation_pct","fieldtype": "Percent",  "width": 110},
    ]


def get_data(filters):
    company     = filters.get("company")
    fiscal_year = filters.get("fiscal_year")
    budget_name = filters.get("budget")
    cost_center = filters.get("cost_center")

    if not company or not fiscal_year:
        return []

    bud_filters = {"company": company, "fiscal_year": fiscal_year, "is_active": 1}
    if budget_name:
        bud_filters["name"] = budget_name

    budgets = frappe.db.get_all("TN Budget", filters=bud_filters,
                                 fields=["name", "budget_name"])

    fy = frappe.db.get_value("Fiscal Year", fiscal_year,
                              ["year_start_date", "year_end_date"], as_dict=True)

    rows = []
    totals = {"budget_amount": 0, "actual_amount": 0, "variance": 0}

    for bud in budgets:
        details = frappe.db.get_all(
            "TN Budget Detail",
            filters={"parent": bud.name, **({"cost_center": cost_center} if cost_center else {})},
            fields=["account", "cost_center", "budget_amount", "actual_amount",
                    "variance", "variance_pct"],
        )
        for d in details:
            utilisation = (flt(d.actual_amount) / flt(d.budget_amount) * 100
                           if d.budget_amount else 0)
            rows.append({
                "budget_name":    bud.budget_name,
                "account":        d.account,
                "cost_center":    d.cost_center or "",
                "budget_amount":  flt(d.budget_amount),
                "actual_amount":  flt(d.actual_amount),
                "variance":       flt(d.variance),
                "variance_pct":   flt(d.variance_pct),
                "utilisation_pct": round(utilisation, 1),
            })
            totals["budget_amount"] += flt(d.budget_amount)
            totals["actual_amount"] += flt(d.actual_amount)
            totals["variance"]      += flt(d.variance)

    if rows:
        util_total = (totals["actual_amount"] / totals["budget_amount"] * 100
                      if totals["budget_amount"] else 0)
        rows.append({
            "budget_name":    "TOTAL",
            "account":        "",
            "cost_center":    "",
            "budget_amount":  totals["budget_amount"],
            "actual_amount":  totals["actual_amount"],
            "variance":       totals["variance"],
            "variance_pct":   round(totals["variance"] / totals["budget_amount"] * 100, 1)
                               if totals["budget_amount"] else 0,
            "utilisation_pct": round(util_total, 1),
            "bold": 1,
        })

    return rows

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_cash_bank_summary/tn_cash_bank_summary.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt, nowdate


def execute(filters=None):
	filters = filters or {}
	columns = get_columns()
	data = get_data(filters)
	return columns, data


def get_columns():
	return [
		{"label": _("Account"), "fieldname": "account", "fieldtype": "Link", "options": "Account", "width": 220},
		{"label": _("Account Type"), "fieldname": "account_type", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 100},
		{"label": _("Opening Balance"), "fieldname": "opening", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 140},
		{"label": _("Total Inflow"), "fieldname": "inflow", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Total Outflow"), "fieldname": "outflow", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Closing Balance"), "fieldname": "closing", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 140},
	]


def get_data(filters):
	company = filters.get("company")
	as_on_date = filters.get("as_on_date") or nowdate()
	from_date = filters.get("from_date") or as_on_date

	if not company:
		frappe.throw(_("Company is required"))

	# Get all Cash and Bank accounts for the company
	accounts = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT name, account_type
		FROM `tabAccount`
		WHERE company = %(company)s
			AND account_type IN ('Cash', 'Bank')
			AND is_group = 0
		ORDER BY account_type, name
	""", {"company": company}, as_dict=True)

	data = []
	totals = {"opening": 0, "inflow": 0, "outflow": 0, "closing": 0}

	current_type = None

	for acc in accounts:
		# Group header
		if acc.account_type != current_type:
			if current_type is not None:
				data.append({})  # spacer
			current_type = acc.account_type
			data.append({
				"account": acc.account_type,
				"account_type": "",
				"opening": None,
				"inflow": None,
				"outflow": None,
				"closing": None,
				"bold": 1,
			})

		# Opening balance (sum of all GL before from_date)
		opening_row = frappe.db.sql("""
			SELECT COALESCE(SUM(debit - credit), 0) AS bal
			FROM `tabGL Entry`
			WHERE company = %(company)s
				AND account = %(account)s
				AND posting_date < %(from_date)s
				AND is_cancelled = 0
		""", {"company": company, "account": acc.name, "from_date": from_date}, as_dict=True)
		opening = flt(opening_row[0].bal if opening_row else 0)

		# Period flows
		period_row = frappe.db.sql("""
			SELECT
				COALESCE(SUM(debit), 0) AS inflow,
				COALESCE(SUM(credit), 0) AS outflow
			FROM `tabGL Entry`
			WHERE company = %(company)s
				AND account = %(account)s
				AND posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(as_on_date)s
				AND is_cancelled = 0
		""", {"company": company, "account": acc.name, "from_date": from_date, "as_on_date": as_on_date}, as_dict=True)

		inflow = flt(period_row[0].inflow if period_row else 0)
		outflow = flt(period_row[0].outflow if period_row else 0)
		closing = opening + inflow - outflow

		data.append({
			"account": acc.name,
			"account_type": acc.account_type,
			"opening": opening,
			"inflow": inflow,
			"outflow": outflow,
			"closing": closing,
		})

		totals["opening"] += opening
		totals["inflow"] += inflow
		totals["outflow"] += outflow
		totals["closing"] += closing

	# Grand total
	data.append({})
	data.append({
		"account": _("TOTAL"),
		"account_type": "",
		"opening": totals["opening"],
		"inflow": totals["inflow"],
		"outflow": totals["outflow"],
		"closing": totals["closing"],
		"bold": 1,
	})

	return data

frappe.query_reports["TN Cash Flow Statement"] = {
	filters: [
		{
			fieldname: "company",
			label: __("Company"),
			fieldtype: "Link",
			options: "Company",
			reqd: 1,
			default: frappe.defaults.get_user_default("Company"),
		},
		{
			fieldname: "from_date",
			label: __("From Date"),
			fieldtype: "Date",
			reqd: 1,
			default: frappe.datetime.year_start(),
		},
		{
			fieldname: "to_date",
			label: __("To Date"),
			fieldtype: "Date",
			reqd: 1,
			default: frappe.datetime.get_today(),
		},
	],

	formatter(value, row, column, data, default_formatter) {
		let result = default_formatter(value, row, column, data);
		if (data && data.bold) {
			result = `<strong>${result}</strong>`;
		}
		if (data && data.is_header) {
			result = `<span style="color: var(--text-color); font-weight: 700;">${result}</span>`;
		}
		return result;
	},
};

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_cash_flow/tn_cash_flow.py
"""
TN Cash Flow Statement — Indirect Method

Structure:
  A. Cash Flow from Operating Activities
       Net Profit / (Loss) for the period
     + Adjustments for non-cash items (Depreciation, Amortisation)
     ± Changes in Working Capital
         (Increase)/Decrease in Trade Receivables
         (Increase)/Decrease in Inventories
         Increase/(Decrease) in Trade Payables
     = Net Cash from Operating Activities

  B. Cash Flow from Investing Activities
       Purchase of Fixed Assets
       Sale of Fixed Assets
       Purchase / Sale of Investments
     = Net Cash from Investing Activities

  C. Cash Flow from Financing Activities
       Proceeds from Loans / Borrowings
       Repayment of Loans
       Capital Introduced
       Dividends / Drawings paid
     = Net Cash from Financing Activities

  Net Increase / (Decrease) in Cash & Equivalents (A+B+C)
  Opening Cash & Cash Equivalents
  Closing Cash & Cash Equivalents
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt


def execute(filters=None):
	filters = filters or {}
	columns = get_columns()
	data = get_data(filters)
	return columns, data


def get_columns():
	return [
		{"label": _("Particulars"), "fieldname": "label", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 350},
		{"label": _("Amount"), "fieldname": "amount", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 160},
	]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _net_change(company, from_date, to_date, account_types=None, account_roots=None, sign=1):
	"""
	Return debit - credit for GL entries in the period matching the given
	account_types (list of Account.account_type) or account_roots (list of
	Account.root_type).  sign=-1 inverts the result (e.g. for liability
	increases that are cash inflows).
	"""
	if not account_types and not account_roots:
		return 0.0

	clauses = []
	params = {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}

	if account_types:
		placeholders = ", ".join(f"%(at{i})s" for i, _ in enumerate(account_types))
		for i, v in enumerate(account_types):
			params[f"at{i}"] = v
		clauses.append(f"a.account_type IN ({placeholders})")

	if account_roots:
		placeholders = ", ".join(f"%(ar{i})s" for i, _ in enumerate(account_roots))
		for i, v in enumerate(account_roots):
			params[f"ar{i}"] = v
		clauses.append(f"a.root_type IN ({placeholders})")

	where = " AND ".join(clauses)

	row = frappe.db.sql(f"""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND {where}
	""", params, as_dict=True)

	return flt(row[0].net if row else 0) * sign


def _cash_balance(company, as_on_date):
	row = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS bal
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date <= %(as_on_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.account_type IN ('Cash', 'Bank')
	""", {"company": company, "as_on_date": as_on_date}, as_dict=True)
	return flt(row[0].bal if row else 0)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Section builders
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _section_header(label):
	return {"label": label, "amount": None, "bold": 1, "is_header": True}


def _line(label, amount, indent=1):
	return {"label": ("    " * indent) + label, "amount": amount}


def _subtotal(label, amount):
	return {"label": label, "amount": amount, "bold": 1}


def _spacer():
	return {"label": "", "amount": None}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def get_data(filters):
	company = filters.get("company")
	from_date = filters.get("from_date")
	to_date = filters.get("to_date")

	if not company or not from_date or not to_date:
		frappe.throw(_("Company, From Date and To Date are required"))

	data = []

	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	# A. Operating Activities
	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	data.append(_section_header(_("A. Cash Flow from Operating Activities")))

	# Net profit = Income - Expense
	net_income = _net_change(company, from_date, to_date, account_roots=["Income"])
	net_expense = _net_change(company, from_date, to_date, account_roots=["Expense"])
	net_profit = net_income - net_expense   # income reduces debit-credit; adjust sign
	# Income accounts: credit increases → debit-credit is negative → negate
	net_profit = -(_net_change(company, from_date, to_date, account_roots=["Income"])) \
				 + _net_change(company, from_date, to_date, account_roots=["Expense"])
	# Simplify: profit = sum(income credits - income debits) - sum(expense debits - expense credits)
	inc_row = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(credit - debit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.root_type = 'Income'
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)

	exp_row = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(debit - credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.root_type = 'Expense'
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)

	net_profit = flt(inc_row[0].net if inc_row else 0) - flt(exp_row[0].net if exp_row else 0)
	data.append(_line(_("Net Profit / (Loss) for the period"), net_profit))

	# Depreciation (Depreciation account type)
	depr = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.account_type = 'Depreciation'
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)
	depr_val = flt(depr[0].net if depr else 0)
	if depr_val:
		data.append(_line(_("Add: Depreciation & Amortisation"), depr_val))

	# Working capital changes
	# Increase in receivables = cash outflow (negative)
	rec_change = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.account_type = 'Receivable'
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)
	rec_val = -flt(rec_change[0].net if rec_change else 0)
	if rec_val:
		data.append(_line(_("(Increase) / Decrease in Trade Receivables"), rec_val))

	# Increase in inventory = cash outflow (negative)
	inv_change = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.account_type = 'Stock'
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)
	inv_val = -flt(inv_change[0].net if inv_change else 0)
	if inv_val:
		data.append(_line(_("(Increase) / Decrease in Inventories"), inv_val))

	# Increase in payables = cash inflow (positive)
	pay_change = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.credit - g.debit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.account_type = 'Payable'
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)
	pay_val = flt(pay_change[0].net if pay_change else 0)
	if pay_val:
		data.append(_line(_("Increase / (Decrease) in Trade Payables"), pay_val))

	net_operating = net_profit + depr_val + rec_val + inv_val + pay_val
	data.append(_subtotal(_("Net Cash from Operating Activities (A)"), net_operating))
	data.append(_spacer())

	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	# B. Investing Activities
	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	data.append(_section_header(_("B. Cash Flow from Investing Activities")))

	# Fixed asset purchases (debit to Fixed Asset accounts)
	fa_change = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.account_type = 'Fixed Asset'
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)
	fa_val = -flt(fa_change[0].net if fa_change else 0)
	data.append(_line(_("Purchase / (Sale) of Fixed Assets"), fa_val))

	# Investments
	inv_acct_change = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.account_type = 'Investments'
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)
	inv_acct_val = -flt(inv_acct_change[0].net if inv_acct_change else 0)
	if inv_acct_val:
		data.append(_line(_("Purchase / (Sale) of Investments"), inv_acct_val))

	net_investing = fa_val + inv_acct_val
	data.append(_subtotal(_("Net Cash from Investing Activities (B)"), net_investing))
	data.append(_spacer())

	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	# C. Financing Activities
	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	data.append(_section_header(_("C. Cash Flow from Financing Activities")))

	# Loans payable (credit = borrowing = inflow)
	loan_change = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.credit - g.debit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.account_type IN ('Loan', 'Bank Overdraft Account')
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)
	loan_val = flt(loan_change[0].net if loan_change else 0)
	if loan_val:
		data.append(_line(_("Proceeds from / (Repayment of) Loans"), loan_val))

	# Equity / Capital (credit = capital introduced = inflow)
	equity_change = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.credit - g.debit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.root_type = 'Equity'
			AND a.account_type NOT IN ('Accumulated Depreciation')
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)
	equity_val = flt(equity_change[0].net if equity_change else 0)
	if equity_val:
		data.append(_line(_("Capital Introduced / (Drawings / Dividends)"), equity_val))

	net_financing = loan_val + equity_val
	data.append(_subtotal(_("Net Cash from Financing Activities (C)"), net_financing))
	data.append(_spacer())

	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	# Summary
	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	net_change = net_operating + net_investing + net_financing
	from frappe.utils import add_days
	opening_cash = _cash_balance(company, frappe.utils.add_days(from_date, -1))
	closing_cash = _cash_balance(company, to_date)

	data.append(_subtotal(_("Net Increase / (Decrease) in Cash (A + B + C)"), net_change))
	data.append(_line(_("Opening Cash & Cash Equivalents"), opening_cash))
	data.append({
		"label": _("Closing Cash & Cash Equivalents"),
		"amount": closing_cash,
		"bold": 1,
	})

	return data

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_collection_efficiency/tn_collection_efficiency.py
"""
TN Collection Efficiency — party-wise receivables collection analysis.

Columns: Customer, Invoiced, Collected, Outstanding, Avg Collection Days, Follow-up Count.
Filters: company, from_date, to_date.
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt, date_diff, today


def execute(filters=None):
    filters = filters or {}
    columns = get_columns()
    data    = get_data(filters)
    return columns, data


def get_columns():
    return [
        {"label": _("Customer"),           "fieldname": "customer",         "fieldtype": "Link",     "options": "Customer", "width": 200},
        {"label": _("Invoiced"),           "fieldname": "total_invoiced",   "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
        {"label": _("Collected"),          "fieldname": "total_collected",  "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
        {"label": _("Outstanding"),        "fieldname": "outstanding",      "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
        {"label": _("Collection %"),       "fieldname": "collection_pct",   "fieldtype": "Percent",  "width": 110},
        {"label": _("Avg Collection Days"),"fieldname": "avg_days",         "fieldtype": "Float",    "width": 140},
        {"label": _("Follow-up Calls"),    "fieldname": "followup_count",   "fieldtype": "Int",      "width": 120},
        {"label": _("Overdue Invoices"),   "fieldname": "overdue_count",    "fieldtype": "Int",      "width": 120},
    ]


def get_data(filters):
    company   = filters.get("company")
    from_date = filters.get("from_date")
    to_date   = filters.get("to_date")

    if not company:
        return []

    date_cond = ""
    params = {"co": company}
    if from_date:
        date_cond += " AND si.posting_date >= %(fd)s"
        params["fd"] = from_date
    if to_date:
        date_cond += " AND si.posting_date <= %(td)s"
        params["td"] = to_date

    invoices = frappe.db.sql(f"""
        SELECT si.customer, si.customer_name,
               si.grand_total, si.outstanding_amount,
               si.posting_date, si.due_date, si.status
        FROM `tabSales Invoice` si
        WHERE si.company = %(co)s AND si.docstatus = 1
              {date_cond}
        ORDER BY si.customer, si.posting_date
    """, params, as_dict=True)

    # Aggregate per customer
    agg = {}
    for inv in invoices:
        c = inv.customer
        if c not in agg:
            agg[c] = {
                "customer":         c,
                "customer_name":    inv.customer_name,
                "total_invoiced":   0,
                "total_collected":  0,
                "outstanding":      0,
                "paid_days_sum":    0,
                "paid_count":       0,
                "overdue_count":    0,
            }
        agg[c]["total_invoiced"]  += flt(inv.grand_total)
        collected = flt(inv.grand_total) - flt(inv.outstanding_amount)
        agg[c]["total_collected"] += collected
        agg[c]["outstanding"]     += flt(inv.outstanding_amount)

        if inv.outstanding_amount <= 0 and inv.status == "Paid":
            # Approximate collection days as posting_date to today (no payment date available easily)
            agg[c]["paid_days_sum"] += date_diff(today(), str(inv.posting_date))
            agg[c]["paid_count"]    += 1

        if flt(inv.outstanding_amount) > 0 and str(inv.due_date) < today():
            agg[c]["overdue_count"] += 1

    # Fetch follow-up counts
    followups = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT party, COUNT(*) cnt FROM `tabTN Collection Log`
        WHERE party_type = 'Customer'
        GROUP BY party
    """, as_dict=True)
    fu_map = {r.party: r.cnt for r in followups}

    rows = []
    for c, d in sorted(agg.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]["outstanding"]):
        coll_pct = (d["total_collected"] / d["total_invoiced"] * 100
                    if d["total_invoiced"] else 0)
        avg_days = (d["paid_days_sum"] / d["paid_count"]
                    if d["paid_count"] else 0)
        rows.append({
            "customer":        d["customer"],
            "total_invoiced":  d["total_invoiced"],
            "total_collected": d["total_collected"],
            "outstanding":     d["outstanding"],
            "collection_pct":  round(coll_pct, 1),
            "avg_days":        round(avg_days, 1),
            "followup_count":  fu_map.get(c, 0),
            "overdue_count":   d["overdue_count"],
        })

    if rows:
        tot_inv = sum(r["total_invoiced"]  for r in rows)
        tot_col = sum(r["total_collected"] for r in rows)
        tot_out = sum(r["outstanding"]     for r in rows)
        rows.append({
            "customer":        "TOTAL",
            "total_invoiced":  tot_inv,
            "total_collected": tot_col,
            "outstanding":     tot_out,
            "collection_pct":  round(tot_col / tot_inv * 100, 1) if tot_inv else 0,
            "avg_days":        0,
            "followup_count":  sum(r["followup_count"] for r in rows[:-1] if isinstance(r.get("followup_count"), int)),
            "overdue_count":   sum(r["overdue_count"]  for r in rows[:-1] if isinstance(r.get("overdue_count"), int)),
            "bold":            1,
        })

    return rows

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_consolidated_pl/tn_consolidated_pl.py
"""
TN Consolidated P&L — IAS 21 compliant P&L across multiple companies.

Income and Expense accounts are translated at the average exchange rate for
the period (P&L items). All amounts are expressed in the selected
presentation currency.

Filters: companies (multi-select), from_date, to_date, presentation_currency.
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt


def execute(filters=None):
    filters = filters or {}
    columns = get_columns(filters)
    data    = get_data(filters)
    return columns, data


def get_columns(filters):
    curr = filters.get("presentation_currency", "INR")
    return [
        {"label": _("Account"),       "fieldname": "account",      "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 240},
        {"label": _("Root Type"),     "fieldname": "root_type",    "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 90},
        {"label": _(f"Amount ({curr})"), "fieldname": "amount",   "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 150},
    ]


def get_data(filters):
    companies  = filters.get("companies") or []
    from_date  = filters.get("from_date")
    to_date    = filters.get("to_date")
    pres_curr  = filters.get("presentation_currency", "INR")

    if not companies or not from_date or not to_date:
        return []

    if isinstance(companies, str):
        import json
        try:
            companies = json.loads(companies)
        except Exception:
            companies = [companies]

    # Fetch P&L GL entries for all companies
    pl_data = {}  # account → {root_type, amount_in_pres_curr}

    for company in companies:
        company_currency = frappe.db.get_value("Company", company, "default_currency")
        avg_rate = _get_avg_rate(company_currency, pres_curr, from_date, to_date)

        rows = frappe.db.sql("""
            SELECT a.name account, a.root_type,
                   SUM(g.credit - g.debit) net_amount
            FROM `tabGL Entry` g
            JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
            WHERE g.company = %(co)s AND g.is_cancelled = 0
                  AND a.root_type IN ('Income','Expense')
                  AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(fd)s AND %(td)s
            GROUP BY a.name, a.root_type
        """, {"co": company, "fd": from_date, "td": to_date}, as_dict=True)

        for r in rows:
            translated = flt(r.net_amount) * avg_rate
            key = r.account
            if key not in pl_data:
                pl_data[key] = {"account": key, "root_type": r.root_type, "amount": 0.0}
            pl_data[key]["amount"] += translated

    # Sort: Income first, then Expense
    income_rows  = [v for v in pl_data.values() if v["root_type"] == "Income"]
    expense_rows = [v for v in pl_data.values() if v["root_type"] == "Expense"]
    income_rows.sort(key=lambda x: x["account"])
    expense_rows.sort(key=lambda x: x["account"])

    total_income  = sum(r["amount"] for r in income_rows)
    total_expense = sum(r["amount"] for r in expense_rows)

    result = []
    result.append({"account": "── INCOME ──", "root_type": "", "amount": None, "bold": 1})
    result.extend(income_rows)
    result.append({"account": "Total Income", "root_type": "Income", "amount": total_income, "bold": 1})

    result.append({"account": "── EXPENSES ──", "root_type": "", "amount": None, "bold": 1})
    result.extend(expense_rows)
    result.append({"account": "Total Expenses", "root_type": "Expense", "amount": total_expense, "bold": 1})

    net = total_income - total_expense
    result.append({"account": "NET PROFIT / (LOSS)", "root_type": "", "amount": net, "bold": 1})

    return result


def _get_avg_rate(from_currency, to_currency, from_date, to_date):
    """Average of daily rates in the period. Falls back to 1 for same currency."""
    if from_currency == to_currency:
        return 1.0
    result = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT AVG(exchange_rate) FROM `tabCurrency Exchange`
        WHERE from_currency = %(fc)s AND to_currency = %(tc)s
              AND date BETWEEN %(fd)s AND %(td)s
    """, {"fc": from_currency, "tc": to_currency, "fd": from_date, "td": to_date})
    rate = flt(result[0][0] if result else None)
    if not rate:
        # Fallback: latest available rate
        rate = flt(frappe.db.get_value(
            "Currency Exchange",
            {"from_currency": from_currency, "to_currency": to_currency,
             "date": ["<=", to_date]},
            "exchange_rate",
            order_by="date desc",
        ) or 1.0)
    return rate

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_dept_pl/tn_dept_pl.js
frappe.query_reports["TN Department P&L"] = {
	filters: [
		{
			fieldname: "company",
			label: __("Company"),
			fieldtype: "Link",
			options: "Company",
			reqd: 1,
			default: frappe.defaults.get_user_default("Company"),
		},
		{
			fieldname: "from_date",
			label: __("From Date"),
			fieldtype: "Date",
			reqd: 1,
			default: frappe.datetime.year_start(),
		},
		{
			fieldname: "to_date",
			label: __("To Date"),
			fieldtype: "Date",
			reqd: 1,
			default: frappe.datetime.get_today(),
		},
		{
			fieldname: "cost_center",
			label: __("Cost Centre"),
			fieldtype: "Link",
			options: "Cost Center",
			get_query() {
				return {
					filters: {
						company: frappe.query_report.get_filter_value("company"),
						is_group: 0,
					},
				};
			},
		},
	],

	formatter(value, row, column, data, default_formatter) {
		let result = default_formatter(value, row, column, data);
		if (data && data.bold) {
			result = `<strong>${result}</strong>`;
		}
		// Colour variance: negative = red (over budget), positive = green
		if (column.fieldname && column.fieldname.endsWith("_var_pct") && value) {
			const num = parseFloat(value);
			if (!isNaN(num)) {
				const color = num < 0 ? "var(--red-500)" : "var(--green-500)";
				result = `<span style="color:${color}; font-weight:600">${value}</span>`;
			}
		}
		return result;
	},
};

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_dept_pl/tn_dept_pl.py
"""
TN Department P&L

Shows Income and Expense broken down by Cost Centre (department).
Columns: Account | [CC1 Budget | CC1 Actual | CC1 Var%] | [CC2 …] | Total Actual

Dynamic columns — one Budget/Actual/Variance triplet per Cost Centre.
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt


def execute(filters=None):
	filters = filters or {}

	company   = filters.get("company")
	from_date = filters.get("from_date")
	to_date   = filters.get("to_date")
	cc_filter = filters.get("cost_center")

	if not company or not from_date or not to_date:
		frappe.throw(_("Company, From Date and To Date are required"))

	cost_centres = _get_cost_centres(company, cc_filter)
	columns      = _build_columns(cost_centres)
	data         = _build_data(company, from_date, to_date, cost_centres)

	return columns, data


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _get_cost_centres(company, cc_filter=None):
	cond = "company = %(company)s AND is_group = 0"
	params = {"company": company}
	if cc_filter:
		cond += " AND name = %(cc)s"
		params["cc"] = cc_filter
	rows = frappe.db.sql(f"""
		SELECT name FROM `tabCost Center`
		WHERE {cond}
		ORDER BY name
	""", params, as_dict=True)
	return [r.name for r in rows]


def _build_columns(cost_centres):
	cols = [
		{"label": _("Account"), "fieldname": "account", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 250},
	]
	for cc in cost_centres:
		safe = cc.replace(" ", "_").replace("-", "_").replace("/", "_")
		cols += [
			{"label": f"{cc} — Budget",  "fieldname": f"{safe}_budget",  "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 120},
			{"label": f"{cc} — Actual",  "fieldname": f"{safe}_actual",  "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 120},
			{"label": f"{cc} — Var %",   "fieldname": f"{safe}_var_pct", "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 90},
		]
	cols.append({"label": _("Total Actual"), "fieldname": "total_actual", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130})
	return cols


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _fetch_actuals(company, from_date, to_date, cost_centres):
	"""Returns {(account, cost_centre): amount} — income as positive, expense as positive."""
	if not cost_centres:
		return {}
	ph = ", ".join(f"%(cc{i})s" for i in range(len(cost_centres)))
	params = {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}
	for i, v in enumerate(cost_centres):
		params[f"cc{i}"] = v

	rows = frappe.db.sql(f"""
		SELECT
			g.account,
			g.cost_center,
			a.root_type,
			COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.root_type IN ('Income', 'Expense')
			AND g.cost_center IN ({ph})
		GROUP BY g.account, g.cost_center, a.root_type
	""", params, as_dict=True)

	result = {}
	for r in rows:
		# Income: credit > debit → net is negative → negate for display
		val = -flt(r.net) if r.root_type == "Income" else flt(r.net)
		result[(r.account, r.cost_center)] = val
	return result


def _fetch_budgets(company, from_date, to_date, cost_centres):
	"""Returns {(account, cost_centre): budget_amount} from ERPNext Budget doctype."""
	if not cost_centres:
		return {}
	ph = ", ".join(f"%(cc{i})s" for i in range(len(cost_centres)))
	params = {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}
	for i, v in enumerate(cost_centres):
		params[f"cc{i}"] = v
	try:
		rows = frappe.db.sql(f"""
			SELECT
				ba.account,
				b.cost_center,
				SUM(bma.budget_amount) AS budget
			FROM `tabBudget` b
			INNER JOIN `tabBudget Account` ba ON ba.parent = b.name
			INNER JOIN `tabMonthly Distribution Percentage` bma ON bma.parent = b.monthly_distribution
			WHERE b.company = %(company)s
				AND b.cost_center IN ({ph})
				AND b.docstatus = 1
			GROUP BY ba.account, b.cost_center
		""", params, as_dict=True)
		return {(r.account, r.cost_center): flt(r.budget) for r in rows}
	except Exception:
		return {}


def _build_data(company, from_date, to_date, cost_centres):
	actuals = _fetch_actuals(company, from_date, to_date, cost_centres)
	budgets = _fetch_budgets(company, from_date, to_date, cost_centres)

	# Gather all accounts that have actuals, grouped by root_type
	accounts_meta = {}
	for (acct, cc), val in actuals.items():
		if acct not in accounts_meta:
			a = frappe.db.get_value("Account", acct, ["root_type", "account_name"], as_dict=True)
			accounts_meta[acct] = a

	income_accounts  = sorted([a for a, m in accounts_meta.items() if m and m.root_type == "Income"])
	expense_accounts = sorted([a for a, m in accounts_meta.items() if m and m.root_type == "Expense"])

	data = []

	def _row(acct, ccs, section_totals):
		safe_ccs = [cc.replace(" ", "_").replace("-", "_").replace("/", "_") for cc in ccs]
		row = {"account": acct}
		total = 0
		for cc, safe in zip(ccs, safe_ccs):
			actual  = flt(actuals.get((acct, cc), 0))
			budget  = flt(budgets.get((acct, cc), 0))
			var_pct = f"{round((actual - budget) / budget * 100, 1)}%" if budget else ""
			row[f"{safe}_budget"]  = budget
			row[f"{safe}_actual"]  = actual
			row[f"{safe}_var_pct"] = var_pct
			total += actual
			section_totals[cc] = section_totals.get(cc, 0) + actual
		row["total_actual"] = total
		return row

	def _total_row(label, section_totals, ccs):
		safe_ccs = [cc.replace(" ", "_").replace("-", "_").replace("/", "_") for cc in ccs]
		row = {"account": label, "bold": 1}
		grand = 0
		for cc, safe in zip(ccs, safe_ccs):
			val = flt(section_totals.get(cc, 0))
			row[f"{safe}_budget"]  = None
			row[f"{safe}_actual"]  = val
			row[f"{safe}_var_pct"] = ""
			grand += val
		row["total_actual"] = grand
		return row

	# Income section
	if income_accounts:
		data.append({"account": _("INCOME"), "bold": 1, "total_actual": None})
		income_totals = {}
		for acct in income_accounts:
			data.append(_row(acct, cost_centres, income_totals))
		data.append(_total_row(_("Total Income"), income_totals, cost_centres))
		data.append({"account": ""})

	# Expense section
	if expense_accounts:
		data.append({"account": _("EXPENSES"), "bold": 1, "total_actual": None})
		expense_totals = {}
		for acct in expense_accounts:
			data.append(_row(acct, cost_centres, expense_totals))
		data.append(_total_row(_("Total Expenses"), expense_totals, cost_centres))
		data.append({"account": ""})

	# Net P&L row
	safe_ccs = [cc.replace(" ", "_").replace("-", "_").replace("/", "_") for cc in cost_centres]
	net_row = {"account": _("NET PROFIT / (LOSS)"), "bold": 1}
	grand_net = 0
	for cc, safe in zip(cost_centres, safe_ccs):
		inc = flt(income_totals.get(cc, 0) if income_accounts else 0)
		exp = flt(expense_totals.get(cc, 0) if expense_accounts else 0)
		net = inc - exp
		net_row[f"{safe}_actual"]  = net
		net_row[f"{safe}_budget"]  = None
		net_row[f"{safe}_var_pct"] = ""
		grand_net += net
	net_row["total_actual"] = grand_net
	data.append(net_row)

	return data

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_ledger_statement/tn_ledger_statement.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt, getdate


def execute(filters=None):
	filters = filters or {}
	columns = get_columns()
	data = get_data(filters)
	return columns, data


def get_columns():
	return [
		{"label": _("Date"), "fieldname": "posting_date", "fieldtype": "Date", "width": 100},
		{"label": _("Voucher No"), "fieldname": "voucher_no", "fieldtype": "Dynamic Link", "options": "voucher_type", "width": 160},
		{"label": _("Voucher Type"), "fieldname": "voucher_type", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 120},
		{"label": _("Party"), "fieldname": "party", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 140},
		{"label": _("Narration"), "fieldname": "remarks", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 250},
		{"label": _("Debit"), "fieldname": "debit", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 120},
		{"label": _("Credit"), "fieldname": "credit", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 120},
		{"label": _("Balance"), "fieldname": "balance", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
	]


def get_data(filters):
	company = filters.get("company")
	account = filters.get("account")
	from_date = filters.get("from_date")
	to_date = filters.get("to_date")
	party = filters.get("party")

	if not company or not account:
		frappe.throw(_("Company and Account are required"))

	# Opening balance before from_date
	opening_result = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(debit - credit), 0) as opening
		FROM `tabGL Entry`
		WHERE company = %(company)s
			AND account = %(account)s
			AND posting_date < %(from_date)s
			AND is_cancelled = 0
	""", {"company": company, "account": account, "from_date": from_date}, as_dict=True)

	opening_balance = flt(opening_result[0].opening if opening_result else 0)

	# Entries in range
	conditions = """
		company = %(company)s
		AND account = %(account)s
		AND posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
		AND is_cancelled = 0
	"""
	params = {"company": company, "account": account, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}

	if party:
		conditions += " AND party = %(party)s"
		params["party"] = party

	entries = frappe.db.sql(f"""
		SELECT
			posting_date, voucher_no, voucher_type,
			party_type, party, remarks,
			debit, credit
		FROM `tabGL Entry`
		WHERE {conditions}
		ORDER BY posting_date, creation
	""", params, as_dict=True)

	data = []

	# Opening row
	data.append({
		"posting_date": from_date,
		"voucher_no": _("Opening Balance"),
		"voucher_type": "",
		"party": "",
		"remarks": "",
		"debit": opening_balance if opening_balance > 0 else 0,
		"credit": -opening_balance if opening_balance < 0 else 0,
		"balance": opening_balance,
		"bold": 1,
	})

	running_balance = opening_balance
	total_dr = total_cr = 0

	for e in entries:
		running_balance += flt(e.debit) - flt(e.credit)
		total_dr += flt(e.debit)
		total_cr += flt(e.credit)

		data.append({
			"posting_date": e.posting_date,
			"voucher_no": e.voucher_no,
			"voucher_type": e.voucher_type,
			"party": e.party or "",
			"remarks": e.remarks or "",
			"debit": flt(e.debit),
			"credit": flt(e.credit),
			"balance": running_balance,
		})

	# Closing row
	data.append({
		"posting_date": to_date,
		"voucher_no": _("Closing Balance"),
		"voucher_type": "",
		"party": "",
		"remarks": "",
		"debit": total_dr,
		"credit": total_cr,
		"balance": running_balance,
		"bold": 1,
	})

	return data

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_loan_outstanding/tn_loan_outstanding.py
"""
TN Loan Outstanding — summary of all active loans with amortization stats.
Filters: company, status (Active / Closed / All).
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt, today, getdate


def execute(filters=None):
    filters = filters or {}
    columns = get_columns()
    data    = get_data(filters)
    return columns, data


def get_columns():
    return [
        {"label": _("Loan"),               "fieldname": "loan_name",          "fieldtype": "Link",     "options": "TN Loan", "width": 160},
        {"label": _("Lender"),             "fieldname": "bank_name",          "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 160},
        {"label": _("Status"),             "fieldname": "status",             "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 80},
        {"label": _("Sanction Amount"),    "fieldname": "sanction_amount",    "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 140},
        {"label": _("Outstanding"),        "fieldname": "outstanding_principal","fieldtype": "Currency","width": 130},
        {"label": _("Total Interest Paid"),"fieldname": "total_interest_paid","fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 150},
        {"label": _("Monthly EMI"),        "fieldname": "emi_amount",         "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 120},
        {"label": _("EMIs Remaining"),     "fieldname": "emis_remaining",     "fieldtype": "Int",      "width": 120},
        {"label": _("Maturity Date"),      "fieldname": "maturity_date",      "fieldtype": "Date",     "width": 110},
    ]


def get_data(filters):
    company = filters.get("company")
    status  = filters.get("status") or "Active"

    loan_filters = {}
    if company:
        loan_filters["company"] = company
    if status != "All":
        loan_filters["status"] = status

    loans = frappe.db.get_all(
        "TN Loan",
        filters=loan_filters,
        fields=["name", "loan_name", "bank_name", "status",
                "sanction_amount", "outstanding_principal",
                "total_interest_paid", "emi_amount"],
    )

    rows = []
    for loan in loans:
        pending_schedule = frappe.db.get_all(
            "TN Loan Schedule",
            filters={"parent": loan.name, "status": "Pending"},
            fields=["emi_no", "due_date"],
            order_by="due_date desc",
            limit=1,
        )
        maturity = pending_schedule[0].due_date if pending_schedule else None
        emis_rem = frappe.db.count("TN Loan Schedule", {"parent": loan.name, "status": "Pending"})

        rows.append({
            "loan_name":           loan.name,
            "bank_name":           loan.bank_name or "",
            "status":              loan.status,
            "sanction_amount":     flt(loan.sanction_amount),
            "outstanding_principal": flt(loan.outstanding_principal),
            "total_interest_paid": flt(loan.total_interest_paid),
            "emi_amount":          flt(loan.emi_amount),
            "emis_remaining":      emis_rem,
            "maturity_date":       maturity,
        })

    if rows:
        rows.append({
            "loan_name":             "TOTAL",
            "bank_name":             "",
            "status":                "",
            "sanction_amount":       sum(r["sanction_amount"]       for r in rows),
            "outstanding_principal": sum(r["outstanding_principal"]  for r in rows),
            "total_interest_paid":   sum(r["total_interest_paid"]   for r in rows),
            "emi_amount":            sum(r["emi_amount"]             for r in rows),
            "emis_remaining":        0,
            "maturity_date":         None,
            "bold":                  1,
        })

    return rows

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_outstanding_payables/tn_outstanding_payables.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt, date_diff, nowdate


def execute(filters=None):
	filters = filters or {}
	columns = get_columns()
	data = get_data(filters)
	return columns, data


def get_columns():
	return [
		{"label": _("Supplier"), "fieldname": "supplier", "fieldtype": "Link", "options": "Supplier", "width": 180},
		{"label": _("Invoice No"), "fieldname": "name", "fieldtype": "Link", "options": "Purchase Invoice", "width": 150},
		{"label": _("Invoice Date"), "fieldname": "posting_date", "fieldtype": "Date", "width": 100},
		{"label": _("Due Date"), "fieldname": "due_date", "fieldtype": "Date", "width": 100},
		{"label": _("Invoice Amount"), "fieldname": "grand_total", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Outstanding"), "fieldname": "outstanding_amount", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Age (Days)"), "fieldname": "age", "fieldtype": "Int", "width": 90},
		{"label": _("0-30"), "fieldname": "range1", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 110},
		{"label": _("31-60"), "fieldname": "range2", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 110},
		{"label": _("61-90"), "fieldname": "range3", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 110},
		{"label": _("90+"), "fieldname": "range4", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 110},
	]


def get_data(filters):
	company = filters.get("company")
	as_on_date = filters.get("as_on_date") or nowdate()
	supplier = filters.get("supplier")

	if not company:
		frappe.throw(_("Company is required"))

	conditions = "company = %(company)s AND docstatus = 1 AND outstanding_amount > 0.01"
	params = {"company": company, "as_on_date": as_on_date}

	if supplier:
		conditions += " AND supplier = %(supplier)s"
		params["supplier"] = supplier

	invoices = frappe.db.sql(f"""
		SELECT
			name, supplier, posting_date, due_date,
			grand_total, outstanding_amount, currency
		FROM `tabPurchase Invoice`
		WHERE {conditions}
			AND posting_date <= %(as_on_date)s
		ORDER BY supplier, due_date
	""", params, as_dict=True)

	data = []
	totals = {"outstanding_amount": 0, "range1": 0, "range2": 0, "range3": 0, "range4": 0}
	prev_supplier = None

	for inv in invoices:
		age = date_diff(as_on_date, inv.due_date)
		if age < 0:
			age = 0  # not yet due

		outstanding = flt(inv.outstanding_amount)
		r1 = outstanding if age <= 30 else 0
		r2 = outstanding if 31 <= age <= 60 else 0
		r3 = outstanding if 61 <= age <= 90 else 0
		r4 = outstanding if age > 90 else 0

		# Supplier group separator
		if inv.supplier != prev_supplier:
			if prev_supplier:
				data.append({"supplier": "", "name": "", "bold": 0, "indent": 0})
			prev_supplier = inv.supplier

		data.append({
			"supplier": inv.supplier,
			"name": inv.name,
			"posting_date": inv.posting_date,
			"due_date": inv.due_date,
			"grand_total": flt(inv.grand_total),
			"outstanding_amount": outstanding,
			"age": age,
			"range1": r1,
			"range2": r2,
			"range3": r3,
			"range4": r4,
		})

		totals["outstanding_amount"] += outstanding
		totals["range1"] += r1
		totals["range2"] += r2
		totals["range3"] += r3
		totals["range4"] += r4

	# Grand total row
	data.append({
		"supplier": _("TOTAL"),
		"name": "",
		"posting_date": None,
		"due_date": None,
		"grand_total": None,
		"outstanding_amount": totals["outstanding_amount"],
		"age": None,
		"range1": totals["range1"],
		"range2": totals["range2"],
		"range3": totals["range3"],
		"range4": totals["range4"],
		"bold": 1,
	})

	return data

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_pdc_register/tn_pdc_register.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt


def execute(filters=None):
	filters = filters or {}
	columns = get_columns()
	data = get_data(filters)
	return columns, data


def get_columns():
	return [
		{"label": _("Type"),        "fieldname": "pdc_type",    "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 80},
		{"label": _("Party"),       "fieldname": "party",       "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 160},
		{"label": _("Cheque No"),   "fieldname": "cheque_no",   "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Cheque Date"), "fieldname": "cheque_date", "fieldtype": "Date",     "width": 110},
		{"label": _("Bank"),        "fieldname": "bank_name",   "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Amount"),      "fieldname": "amount",      "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Status"),      "fieldname": "status",      "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 120},
		{"label": _("Payment Entry"),"fieldname":"payment_entry","fieldtype":"Link",     "options":"Payment Entry","width":150},
	]


def get_data(filters):
	company     = filters.get("company")
	pdc_type    = filters.get("pdc_type")
	status      = filters.get("status")
	party       = filters.get("party")
	from_date   = filters.get("from_date")
	to_date     = filters.get("to_date")

	cond = "1=1"
	params = {}

	if company:
		cond += " AND company = %(company)s"
		params["company"] = company
	if pdc_type:
		cond += " AND pdc_type = %(pdc_type)s"
		params["pdc_type"] = pdc_type
	if status:
		cond += " AND status = %(status)s"
		params["status"] = status
	if party:
		cond += " AND party = %(party)s"
		params["party"] = party
	if from_date:
		cond += " AND cheque_date >= %(from_date)s"
		params["from_date"] = from_date
	if to_date:
		cond += " AND cheque_date <= %(to_date)s"
		params["to_date"] = to_date

	rows = frappe.db.sql(f"""
		SELECT pdc_type, party, cheque_no, cheque_date,
			bank_name, amount, status, payment_entry
		FROM `tabTN PDC Entry`
		WHERE {cond}
		ORDER BY cheque_date, pdc_type
	""", params, as_dict=True)

	data = list(rows)

	# Summary totals
	total = sum(flt(r.amount) for r in data)
	if data:
		data.append({
			"pdc_type": "TOTAL", "party": "", "cheque_no": "",
			"cheque_date": None, "bank_name": "",
			"amount": total, "status": "", "payment_entry": "",
			"bold": 1,
		})

	return data
