# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_ratio_analysis/tn_ratio_analysis.py
"""
TN Ratio Analysis
Calculates key financial ratios for current period vs prior period.

Categories:
  Liquidity    — Current Ratio, Quick Ratio, Cash Ratio
  Profitability — Gross Margin %, Net Margin %, ROE, ROA, EBITDA %
  Solvency     — Debt/Equity, Interest Coverage, Debt Service Coverage
  Efficiency   — Debtor Days, Creditor Days, Inventory Turnover, Asset Turnover
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt, add_years, get_first_day, get_last_day


def execute(filters=None):
	filters = filters or {}
	columns = get_columns()
	data = get_data(filters)
	return columns, data


def get_columns():
	return [
		{"label": _("Category"), "fieldname": "category", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 160},
		{"label": _("Ratio"), "fieldname": "ratio_name", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 220},
		{"label": _("Current Period"), "fieldname": "current", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 140},
		{"label": _("Prior Period"), "fieldname": "prior", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 140},
		{"label": _("Benchmark"), "fieldname": "benchmark", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 130},
	]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Balance sheet helpers — snapshot at a given date
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _sum_by_type(company, as_on, account_types):
	if not account_types:
		return 0.0
	ph = ", ".join(f"%(t{i})s" for i in range(len(account_types)))
	params = {"company": company, "as_on": as_on}
	for i, v in enumerate(account_types):
		params[f"t{i}"] = v
	row = frappe.db.sql(f"""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date <= %(as_on)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.account_type IN ({ph})
	""", params, as_dict=True)
	return flt(row[0].net if row else 0)


def _sum_by_root(company, as_on, root_types):
	if not root_types:
		return 0.0
	ph = ", ".join(f"%(r{i})s" for i in range(len(root_types)))
	params = {"company": company, "as_on": as_on}
	for i, v in enumerate(root_types):
		params[f"r{i}"] = v
	row = frappe.db.sql(f"""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date <= %(as_on)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.root_type IN ({ph})
	""", params, as_dict=True)
	return flt(row[0].net if row else 0)


def _period_sum_by_type(company, from_date, to_date, account_types):
	if not account_types:
		return 0.0
	ph = ", ".join(f"%(t{i})s" for i in range(len(account_types)))
	params = {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}
	for i, v in enumerate(account_types):
		params[f"t{i}"] = v
	row = frappe.db.sql(f"""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.account_type IN ({ph})
	""", params, as_dict=True)
	return flt(row[0].net if row else 0)


def _period_sum_by_root(company, from_date, to_date, root_type, sign=1):
	row = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT COALESCE(SUM(g.debit - g.credit), 0) AS net
		FROM `tabGL Entry` g
		INNER JOIN `tabAccount` a ON a.name = g.account
		WHERE g.company = %(company)s
			AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND g.is_cancelled = 0
			AND a.root_type = %(root_type)s
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date, "root_type": root_type}, as_dict=True)
	return flt(row[0].net if row else 0) * sign


def _safe_div(n, d, decimals=2):
	if not d:
		return "N/A"
	val = round(flt(n) / flt(d), decimals)
	return str(val) + "x" if decimals == 2 else f"{val:.{decimals}f}"


def _pct(n, d):
	if not d:
		return "N/A"
	return f"{round(flt(n) / flt(d) * 100, 1)}%"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _build_ratios(company, from_date, to_date):
	"""Compute all ratios for a given period."""
	as_on = to_date

	# Balance sheet items
	cash       = _sum_by_type(company, as_on, ["Cash", "Bank"])
	receivable = _sum_by_type(company, as_on, ["Receivable"])
	stock      = _sum_by_type(company, as_on, ["Stock"])
	current_assets = cash + receivable + stock + \
		_sum_by_type(company, as_on, ["Current Asset", "Prepaid Expenses", "Tax Assets"])

	current_liab = abs(_sum_by_type(company, as_on, ["Payable", "Current Liability", "Tax Payable"]))
	total_assets = _sum_by_root(company, as_on, ["Asset"])
	total_equity = abs(_sum_by_root(company, as_on, ["Equity"]))
	total_debt   = abs(_sum_by_type(company, as_on, ["Loan", "Bank Overdraft Account"]))

	# P&L items for the period
	revenue      = -_period_sum_by_root(company, from_date, to_date, "Income")   # credits > debits
	cogs         = _period_sum_by_type(company, from_date, to_date, ["Cost of Goods Sold", "Stock Received But Not Billed"])
	gross_profit = revenue - cogs
	expenses     = _period_sum_by_root(company, from_date, to_date, "Expense")
	ebitda       = gross_profit - expenses + \
		_period_sum_by_type(company, from_date, to_date, ["Depreciation"])
	interest_exp = _period_sum_by_type(company, from_date, to_date, ["Chargeable"])
	net_profit   = gross_profit - expenses

	# Averages for turnover ratios
	avg_receivable = receivable  # simplified — use period-end
	avg_stock      = stock
	avg_assets     = total_assets

	# Debtor days = (Receivables / Revenue) * 365
	debtor_days = f"{round(flt(receivable) / flt(revenue) * 365, 0):.0f} days" if revenue else "N/A"
	creditor_days_raw = abs(_sum_by_type(company, as_on, ["Payable"]))
	creditor_days = f"{round(flt(creditor_days_raw) / flt(cogs or revenue) * 365, 0):.0f} days" if (cogs or revenue) else "N/A"
	inv_turn = _safe_div(cogs or revenue, stock) if stock else "N/A"
	asset_turn = _safe_div(revenue, total_assets)

	return {
		# Liquidity
		"current_ratio":  _safe_div(current_assets, current_liab),
		"quick_ratio":    _safe_div(cash + receivable, current_liab),
		"cash_ratio":     _safe_div(cash, current_liab),
		# Profitability
		"gross_margin":   _pct(gross_profit, revenue),
		"net_margin":     _pct(net_profit, revenue),
		"roe":            _pct(net_profit, total_equity),
		"roa":            _pct(net_profit, total_assets),
		"ebitda_pct":     _pct(ebitda, revenue),
		# Solvency
		"debt_equity":    _safe_div(total_debt, total_equity),
		"interest_cov":   _safe_div(ebitda, interest_exp),
		"dscr":           _safe_div(net_profit + _period_sum_by_type(company, from_date, to_date, ["Depreciation"]), total_debt / 5 + interest_exp) if total_debt else "N/A",
		# Efficiency
		"debtor_days":    debtor_days,
		"creditor_days":  creditor_days,
		"inv_turnover":   inv_turn,
		"asset_turnover": asset_turn,
	}


RATIO_META = [
	# (category, key, label, benchmark)
	("Liquidity",     "current_ratio",  "Current Ratio",          "> 2.0x"),
	("Liquidity",     "quick_ratio",    "Quick Ratio",            "> 1.0x"),
	("Liquidity",     "cash_ratio",     "Cash Ratio",             "> 0.5x"),
	("Profitability", "gross_margin",   "Gross Margin %",         "> 40%"),
	("Profitability", "net_margin",     "Net Margin %",           "> 10%"),
	("Profitability", "roe",            "Return on Equity (ROE)", "> 15%"),
	("Profitability", "roa",            "Return on Assets (ROA)", "> 5%"),
	("Profitability", "ebitda_pct",     "EBITDA %",               "> 20%"),
	("Solvency",      "debt_equity",    "Debt / Equity",          "< 2.0x"),
	("Solvency",      "interest_cov",   "Interest Coverage",      "> 3.0x"),
	("Solvency",      "dscr",           "Debt Service Coverage",  "> 1.25x"),
	("Efficiency",    "debtor_days",    "Debtor Days",            "< 45 days"),
	("Efficiency",    "creditor_days",  "Creditor Days",          "30-60 days"),
	("Efficiency",    "inv_turnover",   "Inventory Turnover",     "> 6x/yr"),
	("Efficiency",    "asset_turnover", "Asset Turnover",         "> 1.0x"),
]


def get_data(filters):
	company    = filters.get("company")
	from_date  = filters.get("from_date")
	to_date    = filters.get("to_date")

	if not company or not from_date or not to_date:
		frappe.throw(_("Company, From Date and To Date are required"))

	# Prior period = same duration ending exactly one year before to_date
	days = (frappe.utils.getdate(to_date) - frappe.utils.getdate(from_date)).days
	prior_to   = str(frappe.utils.add_days(from_date, -1))
	prior_from = str(frappe.utils.add_days(prior_to, -days))

	current = _build_ratios(company, from_date, to_date)
	prior   = _build_ratios(company, prior_from, prior_to)

	data = []
	prev_cat = None

	for cat, key, label, benchmark in RATIO_META:
		if cat != prev_cat:
			if prev_cat is not None:
				data.append({"category": "", "ratio_name": "", "current": "", "prior": "", "benchmark": ""})
			data.append({
				"category": cat,
				"ratio_name": "",
				"current": "",
				"prior": "",
				"benchmark": "",
				"bold": 1,
			})
			prev_cat = cat

		data.append({
			"category": "",
			"ratio_name": label,
			"current": current.get(key, "N/A"),
			"prior": prior.get(key, "N/A"),
			"benchmark": benchmark,
		})

	return data

"""
TN Tally Reconciliation — compare vouchers imported from Tally vs. ERPNext GL.

For each TN Import Log entry, shows the Tally voucher alongside the ERPNext
document it created (or the failure reason). Useful for post-import audit.

Filters: import_job, status (All / Success / Skipped / Failed).
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt


def execute(filters=None):
    filters = filters or {}
    columns = get_columns()
    data    = get_data(filters)
    return columns, data


def get_columns():
    return [
        {"label": _("Tally Voucher No"), "fieldname": "tally_voucher_no", "fieldtype": "Data",   "width": 150},
        {"label": _("Tally Date"),       "fieldname": "tally_date",        "fieldtype": "Date",   "width": 100},
        {"label": _("Voucher Type"),     "fieldname": "voucher_type",       "fieldtype": "Data",   "width": 120},
        {"label": _("Status"),           "fieldname": "status",             "fieldtype": "Data",   "width": 80},
        {"label": _("ERPNext Voucher"),  "fieldname": "erpnext_voucher",    "fieldtype": "Data",   "width": 160},
        {"label": _("Error / Remark"),   "fieldname": "error_message",      "fieldtype": "Data",   "width": 300},
    ]


def get_data(filters):
    import_job = filters.get("import_job")
    status     = filters.get("status")

    if not import_job:
        return []

    log_filters = {"import_job": import_job}
    if status and status != "All":
        log_filters["status"] = status

    rows = frappe.db.get_all(
        "TN Import Log",
        filters=log_filters,
        fields=["tally_voucher_no", "tally_date", "voucher_type",
                "status", "erpnext_voucher", "error_message"],
        order_by="tally_date, tally_voucher_no",
        limit=5000,
    )

    # Summary counters
    success = skipped = failed = 0
    for r in rows:
        if r.status == "Success":  success += 1
        elif r.status == "Skipped": skipped += 1
        else: failed += 1

    if rows:
        rows.append({
            "tally_voucher_no": f"TOTAL — {success} success / {skipped} skipped / {failed} failed",
            "tally_date": None,
            "voucher_type": "",
            "status": "",
            "erpnext_voucher": "",
            "error_message": "",
            "bold": 1,
        })

    return rows

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_transfer_pricing/tn_transfer_pricing.py
"""
TN Transfer Pricing — inter-company transaction analysis.
Lists all TN Transfer Pricing Log entries with deviation highlights.
Filters: from_company, to_company, from_date, to_date, flagged_only.
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt


def execute(filters=None):
    filters = filters or {}
    columns = get_columns()
    data    = get_data(filters)
    return columns, data


def get_columns():
    return [
        {"label": _("Date"),            "fieldname": "transaction_date", "fieldtype": "Date",     "width": 100},
        {"label": _("From Company"),    "fieldname": "from_company",     "fieldtype": "Link",     "options": "Company", "width": 160},
        {"label": _("To Company"),      "fieldname": "to_company",       "fieldtype": "Link",     "options": "Company", "width": 160},
        {"label": _("Type"),            "fieldname": "transaction_type", "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 100},
        {"label": _("Amount"),          "fieldname": "amount",           "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 120},
        {"label": _("Arm's Length"),    "fieldname": "arm_length_price", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 120},
        {"label": _("Variance"),        "fieldname": "variance",         "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 110},
        {"label": _("Flagged"),         "fieldname": "flagged",          "fieldtype": "Check",    "width": 70},
        {"label": _("Reference"),       "fieldname": "reference_name",   "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 140},
        {"label": _("Notes"),           "fieldname": "notes",            "fieldtype": "Data",     "width": 200},
    ]


def get_data(filters):
    conds = []
    params = {}

    if filters.get("from_company"):
        conds.append("from_company = %(from_company)s")
        params["from_company"] = filters["from_company"]

    if filters.get("to_company"):
        conds.append("to_company = %(to_company)s")
        params["to_company"] = filters["to_company"]

    if filters.get("from_date"):
        conds.append("transaction_date >= %(from_date)s")
        params["from_date"] = filters["from_date"]

    if filters.get("to_date"):
        conds.append("transaction_date <= %(to_date)s")
        params["to_date"] = filters["to_date"]

    if filters.get("flagged_only"):
        conds.append("flagged = 1")

    where = ("WHERE " + " AND ".join(conds)) if conds else ""

    rows = frappe.db.sql(f"""
        SELECT transaction_date, from_company, to_company, transaction_type,
               amount, arm_length_price, variance, flagged, reference_name, notes
        FROM `tabTN Transfer Pricing Log`
        {where}
        ORDER BY transaction_date DESC, from_company
    """, params, as_dict=True)

    # Highlight flagged rows
    for r in rows:
        if r.flagged:
            r["bold"] = 0
            r["indicator"] = "red"

    return rows

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/report/tn_trial_balance/tn_trial_balance.py
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt, getdate


def execute(filters=None):
	filters = filters or {}
	columns = get_columns()
	data = get_data(filters)
	return columns, data


def get_columns():
	return [
		{"label": _("Account"), "fieldname": "account", "fieldtype": "Link", "options": "Account", "width": 300},
		{"label": _("Account Type"), "fieldname": "account_type", "fieldtype": "Data", "width": 120},
		{"label": _("Opening Dr"), "fieldname": "opening_dr", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Opening Cr"), "fieldname": "opening_cr", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Debit"), "fieldname": "debit", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Credit"), "fieldname": "credit", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Closing Dr"), "fieldname": "closing_dr", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
		{"label": _("Closing Cr"), "fieldname": "closing_cr", "fieldtype": "Currency", "width": 130},
	]


def get_data(filters):
	company = filters.get("company")
	from_date = filters.get("from_date")
	to_date = filters.get("to_date")
	fiscal_year = filters.get("fiscal_year")

	if not company:
		frappe.throw(_("Company is required"))

	# Determine fiscal year start for opening balance
	if fiscal_year:
		fy = frappe.get_doc("Fiscal Year", fiscal_year)
		fy_start = fy.year_start_date
	else:
		fy_start = from_date

	# Opening: sum of all GL entries before from_date
	opening_data = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT
			gle.account,
			SUM(gle.debit) as opening_dr,
			SUM(gle.credit) as opening_cr
		FROM `tabGL Entry` gle
		WHERE gle.company = %(company)s
			AND gle.posting_date < %(from_date)s
			AND gle.is_cancelled = 0
		GROUP BY gle.account
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date or fy_start}, as_dict=True)

	# Period: from_date to to_date
	period_data = frappe.db.sql("""
		SELECT
			gle.account,
			SUM(gle.debit) as debit,
			SUM(gle.credit) as credit
		FROM `tabGL Entry` gle
		WHERE gle.company = %(company)s
			AND gle.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
			AND gle.is_cancelled = 0
		GROUP BY gle.account
	""", {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date}, as_dict=True)

	# Get account details
	accounts = {
		a.name: a for a in frappe.get_all(
			"Account",
			filters={"company": company},
			fields=["name", "account_name", "account_type", "root_type", "parent_account", "is_group"],
		)
	}

	# Merge
	merged = {}
	for row in opening_data:
		merged.setdefault(row.account, {}).update({
			"opening_dr": flt(row.opening_dr),
			"opening_cr": flt(row.opening_cr),
		})
	for row in period_data:
		merged.setdefault(row.account, {}).update({
			"debit": flt(row.debit),
			"credit": flt(row.credit),
		})

	data = []
	total_opening_dr = total_opening_cr = total_dr = total_cr = total_closing_dr = total_closing_cr = 0

	for account, values in sorted(merged.items(), key=lambda x: x[0]):
		acc = accounts.get(account, {})
		opening_dr = flt(values.get("opening_dr", 0))
		opening_cr = flt(values.get("opening_cr", 0))
		debit = flt(values.get("debit", 0))
		credit = flt(values.get("credit", 0))

		# Net opening
		net_opening = opening_dr - opening_cr
		closing_dr = max(net_opening + debit - credit, 0)
		closing_cr = max(-(net_opening + debit - credit), 0)

		row = {
			"account": account,
			"account_type": acc.get("account_type", ""),
			"opening_dr": opening_dr if net_opening > 0 else 0,
			"opening_cr": opening_cr if net_opening < 0 else 0,
			"debit": debit,
			"credit": credit,
			"closing_dr": closing_dr,
			"closing_cr": closing_cr,
		}
		data.append(row)

		total_opening_dr += row["opening_dr"]
		total_opening_cr += row["opening_cr"]
		total_dr += debit
		total_cr += credit
		total_closing_dr += closing_dr
		total_closing_cr += closing_cr

	# Totals row
	data.append({
		"account": _("TOTAL"),
		"account_type": "",
		"opening_dr": total_opening_dr,
		"opening_cr": total_opening_cr,
		"debit": total_dr,
		"credit": total_cr,
		"closing_dr": total_closing_dr,
		"closing_cr": total_closing_cr,
		"bold": 1,
	})

	return data

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/tally_import/exporter.py
"""
TallyNext → Tally XML Exporter

Generates Tally-compatible XML from ERPNext GL entries.
CA can import directly into Tally Prime / Tally ERP 9.
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt


def export_to_tally_xml(company, from_date, to_date, voucher_types=None):
    """Return Tally XML string for the given date range."""
    types = voucher_types or ["Journal Entry", "Payment Entry", "Sales Invoice", "Purchase Invoice"]

    # Fetch GL entries grouped by voucher
    vouchers = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT
            g.voucher_no, g.voucher_type, g.posting_date,
            g.account, g.party, g.debit, g.credit, g.remarks
        FROM `tabGL Entry` g
        WHERE g.company = %(company)s
            AND g.posting_date BETWEEN %(from_date)s AND %(to_date)s
            AND g.voucher_type IN %(types)s
            AND g.is_cancelled = 0
        ORDER BY g.voucher_no, g.account
    """, {"company": company, "from_date": from_date, "to_date": to_date,
          "types": types}, as_dict=True)

    # Group by voucher_no
    vch_map = {}
    for row in vouchers:
        vch_map.setdefault(row.voucher_no, []).append(row)

    lines = ['<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>',
             '<ENVELOPE>', '<HEADER><VERSION>1</VERSION><TALLYREQUEST>Import Data</TALLYREQUEST></HEADER>',
             '<BODY><IMPORTDATA><REQUESTDESC><REPORTNAME>Vouchers</REPORTNAME></REQUESTDESC>',
             '<REQUESTDATA>']

    for vno, entries in vch_map.items():
        first  = entries[0]
        vtype  = _erpnext_to_tally_type(first.voucher_type)
        date_s = str(first.posting_date).replace("-", "")
        narr   = first.remarks or ""

        lines.append(f'<TALLYMESSAGE><VOUCHER VCHTYPE="{vtype}" ACTION="Create">')
        lines.append(f'<DATE>{date_s}</DATE>')
        lines.append(f'<VOUCHERTYPENAME>{vtype}</VOUCHERTYPENAME>')
        lines.append(f'<VOUCHERNUMBER>{vno}</VOUCHERNUMBER>')
        lines.append(f'<NARRATION>{_esc(narr)}</NARRATION>')
        lines.append('<ALLLEDGERENTRIES.LIST>')

        for e in entries:
            # Tally sign convention: debit = negative AMOUNT
            if flt(e.debit) > 0:
                amount = -flt(e.debit)
            else:
                amount = flt(e.credit)
            ledger = e.party or e.account
            lines.append(f'<LEDGERNAME>{_esc(ledger)}</LEDGERNAME>')
            lines.append(f'<AMOUNT>{amount}</AMOUNT>')

        lines.append('</ALLLEDGERENTRIES.LIST>')
        lines.append('</VOUCHER></TALLYMESSAGE>')

    lines += ['</REQUESTDATA></IMPORTDATA></BODY></ENVELOPE>']
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _erpnext_to_tally_type(etype):
    return {
        "Journal Entry":    "Journal",
        "Payment Entry":    "Payment",
        "Sales Invoice":    "Sales",
        "Purchase Invoice": "Purchase",
    }.get(etype, "Journal")


def _esc(s):
    return str(s).replace("&", "&amp;").replace("<", "&lt;").replace(">", "&gt;")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/tally_import/importer.py
"""
Background import job — converts parsed Tally vouchers into ERPNext documents.
Runs as a Frappe background job (queue=long).
"""

import json
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import flt, getdate


def run_import(import_job):
    job_doc = frappe.get_doc("TN Tally Import Job", import_job)
    cached  = frappe.cache().get_value(f"tally_import_{import_job}")
    if not cached:
        _fail(job_doc, "Parsed data expired — please re-parse the XML.")
        return

    data     = json.loads(cached)
    vouchers = data.get("vouchers", [])
    mappings = _build_mapping_dict(import_job)

    total   = len(vouchers)
    success = skipped = failed = 0
    log_lines = []

    for i, vch in enumerate(vouchers):
        try:
            erpnext_vch = _import_voucher(vch, job_doc, mappings)
            if erpnext_vch:
                success += 1
                _log(import_job, vch, "Success", erpnext_vch)
                log_lines.append(f"✓ {vch['voucher_no']} → {erpnext_vch}")
            else:
                skipped += 1
                _log(import_job, vch, "Skipped")
                log_lines.append(f"↷ {vch['voucher_no']} skipped")
        except Exception as e:
            failed += 1
            _log(import_job, vch, "Failed", error=str(e))
            log_lines.append(f"✗ {vch['voucher_no']}: {e}")

        # Update progress every 50 vouchers
        if i % 50 == 0:
            frappe.db.set_value("TN Tally Import Job", import_job, {
                "progress_pct": round((i + 1) / total * 100, 1),
            })
            frappe.db.commit()

    frappe.db.set_value("TN Tally Import Job", import_job, {
        "status":       "Complete" if failed == 0 else "Failed",
        "progress_pct": 100,
        "import_log":   "\n".join(log_lines[-500:]),  # last 500 lines
        "error_summary": f"{success} imported, {skipped} skipped, {failed} failed",
    })
    frappe.db.commit()


def _build_mapping_dict(import_job):
    rows = frappe.db.get_all("TN Ledger Mapping",
        filters={"import_job": import_job},
        fields=["tally_ledger_name", "erpnext_account"])
    return {r.tally_ledger_name: r.erpnext_account for r in rows if r.erpnext_account}


def _import_voucher(vch, job_doc, mappings):
    vch_type = vch.get("voucher_type", "")
    if vch_type in ("Payment", "Receipt"):
        return _create_payment_entry(vch, job_doc, mappings)
    elif vch_type in ("Sales", "Credit Note"):
        return _create_simple_journal(vch, job_doc, mappings)
    elif vch_type in ("Purchase", "Debit Note"):
        return _create_simple_journal(vch, job_doc, mappings)
    else:
        return _create_journal_entry(vch, job_doc, mappings)


def _create_journal_entry(vch, job_doc, mappings):
    je = frappe.new_doc("Journal Entry")
    je.company      = job_doc.company
    je.posting_date = getdate(vch["date"])
    je.user_remark  = vch.get("narration", "")
    je.voucher_type = "Journal Entry"

    for entry in vch.get("entries", []):
        acct = mappings.get(entry["ledger"])
        if not acct:
            continue
        row = je.append("accounts", {
            "account": acct,
            "cost_center": frappe.db.get_value("Cost Center",
                {"company": job_doc.company, "is_group": 0}, "name"),
        })
        if entry["is_debit"]:
            row.debit_in_account_currency = flt(entry["amount"])
        else:
            row.credit_in_account_currency = flt(entry["amount"])

    if not je.accounts or len(je.accounts) < 2:
        return None

    je.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    je.submit()
    return je.name


def _create_payment_entry(vch, job_doc, mappings):
    """Simple payment — create as journal entry for safety."""
    return _create_simple_journal(vch, job_doc, mappings)


def _create_simple_journal(vch, job_doc, mappings):
    return _create_journal_entry(vch, job_doc, mappings)


def _create_sales_invoice(vch, job_doc, mappings):
    return _create_journal_entry(vch, job_doc, mappings)


def _create_purchase_invoice(vch, job_doc, mappings):
    return _create_journal_entry(vch, job_doc, mappings)


def _log(import_job, vch, status, erpnext_vch=None, error=None):
    try:
        frappe.get_doc({
            "doctype":       "TN Import Log",
            "import_job":    import_job,
            "tally_voucher_no": vch.get("voucher_no", ""),
            "tally_date":    vch.get("date"),
            "voucher_type":  vch.get("voucher_type", ""),
            "status":        status,
            "erpnext_voucher": erpnext_vch or "",
            "error_message": error or "",
        }).insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    except Exception:
        pass


def _fail(job_doc, msg):
    frappe.db.set_value("TN Tally Import Job", job_doc.name, {
        "status": "Failed",
        "error_summary": msg,
    })
    frappe.db.commit()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/tally_import/mapper.py
"""
Auto-map Tally ledger names to ERPNext accounts using name similarity.
Saves TN Ledger Mapping records for manual review / override.
"""

import frappe
from frappe.utils import flt
from difflib import SequenceMatcher


# Tally group → ERPNext account root / type hint
TALLY_GROUP_HINTS = {
    "Bank Accounts":           ("Bank",      "Asset"),
    "Bank OD Accounts":        ("Bank",      "Liability"),
    "Cash-in-Hand":            ("Cash",      "Asset"),
    "Sundry Debtors":          ("Receivable","Asset"),
    "Sundry Creditors":        ("Payable",   "Liability"),
    "Fixed Assets":            ("Fixed Asset","Asset"),
    "Investments":             ("Investments","Asset"),
    "Current Liabilities":     (None,        "Liability"),
    "Capital Account":         (None,        "Equity"),
    "Reserves & Surplus":      (None,        "Equity"),
    "Sales Accounts":          ("Income Account","Income"),
    "Purchase Accounts":       ("Cost of Goods Sold","Expense"),
    "Direct Expenses":         (None,        "Expense"),
    "Indirect Expenses":       (None,        "Expense"),
    "Direct Income":           ("Income Account","Income"),
    "Indirect Income":         ("Income Account","Income"),
    "Duties & Taxes":          ("Tax",       "Liability"),
    "Provisions":              (None,        "Liability"),
    "Loans & Advances (Asset)":(None,        "Asset"),
    "Loans (Liability)":       ("Loan",      "Liability"),
}


def auto_map_ledgers(import_job, company, ledgers):
    """Create TN Ledger Mapping records, auto-matching where possible."""
    # Fetch all ERPNext accounts for this company once
    erpnext_accounts = frappe.db.get_all(
        "Account",
        filters={"company": company, "is_group": 0},
        fields=["name", "account_name", "account_type", "root_type"],
    )
    account_names = [a.account_name.lower() for a in erpnext_accounts]

    for led in ledgers:
        if frappe.db.exists("TN Ledger Mapping", {
            "import_job": import_job,
            "tally_ledger_name": led["name"],
        }):
            continue  # already mapped

        best_account, confidence = _find_best_match(led, erpnext_accounts, account_names)
        hint_type, _ = TALLY_GROUP_HINTS.get(led.get("group", ""), (None, None))

        doc = frappe.new_doc("TN Ledger Mapping")
        doc.import_job          = import_job
        doc.company             = company
        doc.tally_ledger_name   = led["name"]
        doc.tally_group         = led.get("group", "")
        doc.tally_opening_balance = flt(led.get("opening_balance", 0))
        doc.erpnext_account     = best_account
        doc.new_account_type    = hint_type or ""
        doc.mapped_by           = "Auto"
        doc.confidence          = confidence
        doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    frappe.db.commit()


def _find_best_match(led, accounts, account_names_lower):
    tally_name = led["name"].lower()
    best_score  = 0
    best_acct   = ""

    for i, acct in enumerate(accounts):
        score = SequenceMatcher(None, tally_name, account_names_lower[i]).ratio()
        if score > best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_acct  = acct.name

    # Only accept if confidence is reasonable
    if best_score < 0.5:
        return "", round(best_score * 100, 0)
    return best_acct, round(best_score * 100, 0)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/tally_import/parser.py
"""
Tally XML Parser for TallyNext

Parses Tally's Data Export XML format (supported by Tally ERP 9, Tally Prime).

Returns:
  {
    "ledgers":  [ {name, group, opening_balance, is_revenue, currency} ],
    "vouchers": [ {voucher_no, date, voucher_type, narration, entries: [{ledger, amount, is_debit}]} ],
    "costcentres": [ {name} ],
  }

Usage in Tally:
  Gateway → Data → Export Data → In XML format (choose Company + Date range)
"""

import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
import frappe
from frappe.utils import flt


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tally voucher type → ERPNext type mapping
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
VOUCHER_TYPE_MAP = {
    "Payment":       "Payment Entry",
    "Receipt":       "Payment Entry",
    "Journal":       "Journal Entry",
    "Contra":        "Journal Entry",
    "Sales":         "Sales Invoice",
    "Purchase":      "Purchase Invoice",
    "Credit Note":   "Sales Invoice",   # return
    "Debit Note":    "Purchase Invoice",# return
    "Stock Journal": "Journal Entry",
}

# Tally groups that map to revenue/income in ERPNext
INCOME_GROUPS = {
    "Sales Accounts", "Indirect Income", "Direct Income",
    "Other Income", "Income (Direct)", "Income (Indirect)",
}


def parse_tally_xml(file_path):
    try:
        tree = ET.parse(file_path)
        root = tree.getroot()
    except ET.ParseError as e:
        frappe.throw(f"Invalid Tally XML: {e}")

    # Tally XML can have different root structures
    # Try both ENVELOPE and direct BODY
    body = root.find(".//BODY") or root

    ledgers     = _parse_ledgers(body)
    costcentres = _parse_costcentres(body)
    vouchers    = _parse_vouchers(body)

    return {
        "ledgers":     ledgers,
        "costcentres": costcentres,
        "vouchers":    vouchers,
    }


def _text(el, tag, default=""):
    node = el.find(tag)
    return (node.text or "").strip() if node is not None else default


def _parse_ledgers(body):
    ledgers = []
    for master in body.findall(".//MASTERS/LEDGER") + body.findall(".//LEDGER"):
        name  = master.get("NAME") or _text(master, "NAME")
        group = _text(master, "PARENT")
        if not name:
            continue
        opening = flt(_text(master, "OPENINGBALANCE", "0").replace(",", ""))
        ledgers.append({
            "name":            name,
            "group":           group,
            "opening_balance": opening,
            "is_revenue":      group in INCOME_GROUPS,
        })
    return ledgers


def _parse_costcentres(body):
    ccs = []
    for cc in body.findall(".//COSTCENTRE") + body.findall(".//COSTCENTER"):
        name = cc.get("NAME") or _text(cc, "NAME")
        if name:
            ccs.append({"name": name})
    return ccs


def _parse_vouchers(body):
    vouchers = []
    for vch in body.findall(".//VOUCHER"):
        vch_type = _text(vch, "VOUCHERTYPENAME")
        vch_no   = _text(vch, "VOUCHERNUMBER") or _text(vch, "ALTERID")
        date_str = _text(vch, "DATE")
        narr     = _text(vch, "NARRATION")
        is_cancelled = _text(vch, "ISCANCELLED").upper() == "YES"

        if is_cancelled:
            continue
        if not date_str or not vch_type:
            continue

        # Parse Tally date: YYYYMMDD
        try:
            date = f"{date_str[:4]}-{date_str[4:6]}-{date_str[6:8]}"
        except Exception:
            continue

        entries = []
        for le in vch.findall(".//ALLLEDGERENTRIES.LIST") + vch.findall(".//LEDGERENTRIES.LIST"):
            ledger  = _text(le, "LEDGERNAME")
            amount  = flt(_text(le, "AMOUNT", "0").replace(",", ""))
            # Tally: positive amount = credit, negative = debit
            is_debit = amount < 0
            entries.append({
                "ledger":    ledger,
                "amount":    abs(amount),
                "is_debit":  is_debit,
                "cost_centre": _text(le, "COSTCENTRENAME"),
            })

        if entries:
            vouchers.append({
                "voucher_no":   vch_no,
                "date":         date,
                "voucher_type": vch_type,
                "narration":    narr,
                "entries":      entries,
            })

    return vouchers

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/tally_import/validator.py
"""Pre-import validation — must return empty list before import is allowed."""

import frappe
from frappe import _


def validate(import_job, company):
    errors = []

    mappings = frappe.db.get_all("TN Ledger Mapping",
        filters={"import_job": import_job},
        fields=["tally_ledger_name", "erpnext_account", "create_new", "new_account_name"])

    # 1. Check all ledgers are mapped
    unmapped = [m.tally_ledger_name for m in mappings
                if not m.erpnext_account and not m.create_new]
    for name in unmapped:
        errors.append({"type": "Unmapped Ledger", "detail": name})

    # 2. New accounts must have a name
    missing_name = [m.tally_ledger_name for m in mappings
                    if m.create_new and not m.new_account_name]
    for name in missing_name:
        errors.append({"type": "Missing New Account Name", "detail": name})

    # 3. Check no duplicate voucher numbers already exist in ERPNext
    job = frappe.get_doc("TN Tally Import Job", import_job)
    cached = frappe.cache().get_value(f"tally_import_{import_job}")
    if cached:
        import json
        data = json.loads(cached)
        voucher_nos = {v["voucher_no"] for v in data.get("vouchers", []) if v.get("voucher_no")}
        skip = frappe.db.get_value("TN Tally Import Job", import_job, "skip_duplicate_vouchers")
        if not skip:
            existing = frappe.db.sql("""
                SELECT name FROM `tabJournal Entry` WHERE name IN %(nos)s AND company=%(co)s
                UNION
                SELECT name FROM `tabPayment Entry` WHERE name IN %(nos)s AND company=%(co)s
            """, {"nos": list(voucher_nos) or ["__none__"], "co": company})
            for row in existing:
                errors.append({"type": "Duplicate Voucher", "detail": row[0]})

    return errors

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/tallynext/tallynext/tallynext/whatsapp/service.py
"""
TallyNext WhatsApp Service

Supports three providers:
  - WATI       (api.wati.io)
  - Interakt   (api.interakt.ai)
  - Meta Cloud API (graph.facebook.com)

Entry points:
  send_whatsapp(company, mobile, message_type, template_name, params, reference_*)
  send_invoice_whatsapp(sales_invoice_name)
  send_payment_reminder(sales_invoice_name)
"""

import json
import frappe
from frappe import _
from frappe.utils import now_datetime


# ── Public API ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@frappe.whitelist()
def send_whatsapp(company, mobile, message_type="Custom",
                  template_name=None, template_params=None,
                  reference_doctype=None, reference_name=None,
                  message_body=None):
    """Queue and immediately send a WhatsApp message."""
    config = _get_config(company)
    if not config:
        frappe.throw(_(f"No active WhatsApp config found for company {company}."))

    log = frappe.get_doc({
        "doctype":           "TN WhatsApp Log",
        "company":           company,
        "recipient_mobile":  mobile,
        "message_type":      message_type,
        "status":            "Queued",
        "reference_doctype": reference_doctype or "",
        "reference_name":    reference_name   or "",
        "template_name":     template_name    or "",
        "message_body":      message_body     or "",
    })
    log.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    frappe.db.commit()

    params = json.loads(template_params) if isinstance(template_params, str) else (template_params or [])

    try:
        provider = config.provider
        if provider == "WATI":
            resp = _send_wati(config, mobile, template_name, params)
        elif provider == "Interakt":
            resp = _send_interakt(config, mobile, template_name, params)
        else:  # Meta Cloud API
            resp = _send_meta(config, mobile, template_name, params)

        frappe.db.set_value("TN WhatsApp Log", log.name, {
            "status":        "Sent",
            "sent_at":       now_datetime(),
            "response_json": json.dumps(resp),
        })
    except Exception as e:
        frappe.db.set_value("TN WhatsApp Log", log.name, {
            "status":        "Failed",
            "error_message": str(e),
        })
    frappe.db.commit()
    return log.name


@frappe.whitelist()
def send_invoice_whatsapp(sales_invoice_name):
    """Send a WhatsApp message for a submitted Sales Invoice."""
    inv = frappe.get_doc("Sales Invoice", sales_invoice_name)
    config = _get_config(inv.company)
    if not config:
        frappe.throw(_("No active WhatsApp config for this company."))

    mobile = frappe.db.get_value("Customer", inv.customer, "mobile_no")
    if not mobile:
        frappe.throw(_("Customer has no mobile number set."))

    template = config.invoice_template or "invoice_notification"
    params   = [inv.customer_name, inv.name, str(inv.grand_total), str(inv.due_date)]
    return send_whatsapp(
        company=inv.company,
        mobile=mobile,
        message_type="Invoice",
        template_name=template,
        template_params=params,
        reference_doctype="Sales Invoice",
        reference_name=inv.name,
    )


@frappe.whitelist()
def send_payment_reminder(sales_invoice_name):
    """Send a payment reminder WhatsApp for an overdue Sales Invoice."""
    inv = frappe.get_doc("Sales Invoice", sales_invoice_name)
    config = _get_config(inv.company)
    if not config:
        frappe.throw(_("No active WhatsApp config for this company."))

    mobile = frappe.db.get_value("Customer", inv.customer, "mobile_no")
    if not mobile:
        frappe.throw(_("Customer has no mobile number set."))

    template = config.payment_template or "payment_reminder"
    params   = [inv.customer_name, inv.name, str(inv.outstanding_amount), str(inv.due_date)]
    return send_whatsapp(
        company=inv.company,
        mobile=mobile,
        message_type="Payment Reminder",
        template_name=template,
        template_params=params,
        reference_doctype="Sales Invoice",
        reference_name=inv.name,
    )


# ── Provider implementations ────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _send_wati(config, mobile, template_name, params):
    import requests
    url = f"https://live-mt-server.wati.io/api/v1/sendTemplateMessage?whatsappNumber={mobile}"
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {config.get_password('api_key')}",
               "Content-Type": "application/json"}
    payload = {
        "template_name": template_name,
        "broadcast_name": template_name,
        "parameters": [{"name": f"param{i+1}", "value": str(v)} for i, v in enumerate(params)],
    }
    resp = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=15)
    resp.raise_for_status()
    return resp.json()


def _send_interakt(config, mobile, template_name, params):
    import requests
    url = "https://api.interakt.ai/v1/public/message/"
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Basic {config.get_password('api_key')}",
               "Content-Type": "application/json"}
    payload = {
        "countryCode": "+91",
        "phoneNumber": mobile,
        "callbackData": template_name,
        "type":         "Template",
        "template": {
            "name":           template_name,
            "languageCode":   "en",
            "bodyValues":     params,
        },
    }
    resp = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=15)
    resp.raise_for_status()
    return resp.json()


def _send_meta(config, mobile, template_name, params):
    import requests
    phone_id = config.phone_number_id
    token    = config.get_password("api_key")
    url = f"https://graph.facebook.com/v18.0/{phone_id}/messages"
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}", "Content-Type": "application/json"}
    components = []
    if params:
        components.append({
            "type":       "body",
            "parameters": [{"type": "text", "text": str(p)} for p in params],
        })
    payload = {
        "messaging_product": "whatsapp",
        "to":                mobile,
        "type":              "template",
        "template": {
            "name":     template_name,
            "language": {"code": "en"},
            "components": components,
        },
    }
    resp = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=15)
    resp.raise_for_status()
    return resp.json()


# ── Helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _get_config(company):
    rec = frappe.db.get_value(
        "TN WhatsApp Config",
        {"company": company, "is_active": 1},
        ["name", "provider", "api_key", "api_secret",
         "waba_id", "phone_number_id",
         "invoice_template", "payment_template"],
        as_dict=True,
    )
    if not rec:
        return None
    return frappe.get_doc("TN WhatsApp Config", rec.name)
